===== FILE: src/App.tsx =====

import React from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter, Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './context/AuthContext';
import { Layout } from './components/Layout';
import { LoginPage } from './pages/LoginPage';
import { RegisterPage } from './pages/RegisterPage';
import { DashboardPage } from './pages/DashboardPage';
import { NotificationsPage } from './pages/NotificationsPage';
import { NotificationDetailsPage } from './pages/NotificationDetailsPage';
import { ChangeHistoryPage } from './pages/ChangeHistoryPage';
import { SourcesPage } from './pages/SourcesPage';
import { AlertsPage } from './pages/AlertsPage';
import { SavedPage } from './pages/SavedPage';
import { ProfilePage } from './pages/ProfilePage';
import { AdminPage } from './pages/AdminPage';

function ProtectedRoute({ children, adminOnly = false }: { children: React.ReactNode; adminOnly?: boolean }) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center text-sm text-slate-500">
        Loading GovTrack workspace...
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  if (adminOnly && user.role !== 'admin') {
    return <Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />;
  }

  return <Layout>{children}</Layout>;
}

function PublicOnlyRoute({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  if (loading) return null;
  if (user) return <Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />;
  return <>{children}</>;
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <Routes>
          <Route
            path="/login"
            element={
              <PublicOnlyRoute>
                <LoginPage />
              </PublicOnlyRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/register"
            element={
              <PublicOnlyRoute>
                <RegisterPage />
              </PublicOnlyRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/dashboard"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <DashboardPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/notifications"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <NotificationsPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/notifications/:id"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <NotificationDetailsPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/changes"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <ChangeHistoryPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/sources"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <SourcesPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/alerts"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <AlertsPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/saved"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <SavedPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/profile"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <ProfilePage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route
            path="/admin"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute adminOnly>
                <AdminPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />
          <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />} />
        </Routes>
      </BrowserRouter>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/api/client.ts =====

const TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY = 'govtrack_access_token';

export function getStoredToken(): string | null {
  return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY);
}

export function setStoredToken(token: string | null): void {
  if (token) {
    localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY, token);
  } else {
    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY);
  }
}

export async function apiRequest<T>(
  path: string,
  options: RequestInit = {}
): Promise<T> {
  const token = getStoredToken();
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    ...(options.headers as Record<string, string> | undefined),
  };

  if (token) {
    headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  const response = await fetch(`/api${path}`, {
    ...options,
    headers,
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = `Request failed (${response.status})`;
    try {
      const data = await response.json();
      if (data?.detail) {
        message = typeof data.detail === 'string' ? data.detail : JSON.stringify(data.detail);
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore json parse errors
    }
    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return response.json() as Promise<T>;
}

===== FILE: src/components/DiffViewer.tsx =====

import type { ChangeHistoryItem, FieldDiff } from '../types';
import { formatFieldValue } from './StatusBadge';

const FIELD_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  title: 'Notification Title',
  organization_name: 'Organization',
  notification_number: 'Notification No.',
  vacancy_count: 'Vacancy Count',
  job_category: 'Job Category',
  application_start_date: 'Application Start',
  application_deadline: 'Application Deadline',
  exam_date: 'Examination Date',
  admit_card_date: 'Admit Card Date',
  result_date: 'Result Date',
  description: 'Description',
  pdf_url: 'Official PDF Link',
  source_url: 'Original Source URL',
  published_date: 'Published Date',
  notification_status: 'Notification Status',
  lifecycle_status: 'Notification Status',
};

export function DiffViewer({
  diffs,
  changeType,
}: {
  diffs: FieldDiff[];
  changeType?: ChangeHistoryItem['change_type'];
}) {
  const meaningful = (diffs || []).filter((d) => d.old_value !== null || d.new_value !== null);
  if (meaningful.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const isPureAddition =
    changeType === 'NEW' || meaningful.every((d) => d.old_value === null || d.old_value === '');

  if (isPureAddition) {
    const announced = meaningful.filter((d) => d.new_value !== null && d.new_value !== '');
    if (announced.length === 0) return null;
    return (
      <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-lg bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-xs">
          {announced.map((d, idx) => (
            <div key={`${d.field}-${idx}`} className="min-w-0">
              <dt className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
                {FIELD_LABELS[d.field] || d.field}
              </dt>
              <dd className="text-slate-900 font-medium break-words tabular-nums">
                {formatFieldValue(d.field, d.new_value)}
              </dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-lg bg-white">
      <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600">
            <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold w-44">Changed Field</th>
            <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">Previous Official</th>
            <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">New Official</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
          {meaningful.map((d, idx) => (
            <tr key={`${d.field}-${idx}`} className="align-top">
              <td className="py-2 px-3 font-medium text-slate-800 whitespace-nowrap">
                {FIELD_LABELS[d.field] || d.field}
              </td>
              <td className="py-2 px-3 font-mono text-red-700 bg-red-50/40 break-all">
                {d.old_value ? (
                  formatFieldValue(d.field, d.old_value)
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-slate-400 italic">Unavailable</span>
                )}
              </td>
              <td className="py-2 px-3 font-mono text-emerald-800 bg-emerald-50/40 break-all">
                {d.new_value ? (
                  formatFieldValue(d.field, d.new_value)
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-slate-400 italic">Unavailable</span>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/StatusBadge.tsx =====

import {
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  FileEdit,
  PlusCircle,
  ShieldAlert,
  Trash2,
  XCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';

export function FreshnessStatus({ status }: { status: string }) {
  switch (status) {
    case 'Fresh':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>Fresh</span>
        </span>
      );
    case 'Stale':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>Stale</span>
        </span>
      );
    case 'Rate Limited':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          <ShieldAlert className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>Rate Limited</span>
        </span>
      );
    case 'Error':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-red-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          <XCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>Error</span>
        </span>
      );
    default:
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 whitespace-nowrap">
          <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>{status}</span>
        </span>
      );
  }
}

export function ChangeTypeLabel({ type }: { type: string }) {
  switch (type) {
    case 'NEW':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          <PlusCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>New</span>
        </span>
      );
    case 'UPDATED':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          <FileEdit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>Updated</span>
        </span>
      );
    case 'REMOVED':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-red-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>Removed</span>
        </span>
      );
    default:
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>{type}</span>
        </span>
      );
  }
}

export function formatTimestamp(iso?: string | null): string {
  if (!iso) return 'Unavailable';
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return iso;
  return d.toLocaleString('en-IN', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
}

export function formatHumanDate(val?: string | null): string {
  if (!val) return 'Unavailable';
  const trimmed = val.trim();
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(trimmed)) {
    const [y, m, d] = trimmed.split('-').map(Number);
    const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
    if (!Number.isNaN(dt.getTime())) {
      return dt.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
        day: '2-digit',
        month: 'long',
        year: 'numeric',
        timeZone: 'UTC',
      });
    }
  }
  return trimmed;
}

const DATE_FIELDS = new Set([
  'exam_date',
  'application_deadline',
  'application_start_date',
  'admit_card_date',
  'result_date',
  'published_date',
]);

export function formatFieldValue(field?: string | null, val?: string | null): string {
  if (!val) return 'Unavailable';
  if (field && DATE_FIELDS.has(field)) {
    return formatHumanDate(val);
  }
  return val;
}

export function getFieldNoun(field?: string | null): string {
  if (!field) return 'Value';
  if (DATE_FIELDS.has(field)) return 'Date';
  if (field === 'vacancy_count') return 'Vacancies';
  if (field === 'notification_status' || field === 'lifecycle_status') return 'Status';
  if (field === 'pdf_url') return 'PDF URL';
  if (field === 'source_url') return 'URL';
  if (field === 'title') return 'Title';
  return 'Value';
}

export function formatDeadlineCountdown(dateStr?: string | null): { text: string; urgent: boolean; closed: boolean } {
  if (!dateStr) return { text: 'Unavailable', urgent: false, closed: false };
  const target = new Date(`${dateStr}T23:59:59`);
  if (Number.isNaN(target.getTime())) return { text: dateStr, urgent: false, closed: false };
  const diffDays = Math.ceil((target.getTime() - Date.now()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  if (diffDays < 0) return { text: `Closed (${dateStr})`, urgent: false, closed: true };
  if (diffDays === 0) return { text: 'Closes today', urgent: true, closed: false };
  if (diffDays <= 7) return { text: `${diffDays}d left (${dateStr})`, urgent: true, closed: false };
  return { text: `${diffDays}d left (${dateStr})`, urgent: false, closed: false };
}

===== FILE: src/components/Layout.tsx =====

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Bell,
  Bookmark,
  FileText,
  Globe,
  History,
  LayoutDashboard,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  Search,
  ShieldCheck,
  UserCircle,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

interface NavItem {
  to: string;
  label: string;
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  adminOnly?: boolean;
}

const NAV_ITEMS: NavItem[] = [
  { to: '/dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { to: '/notifications', label: 'Notifications', icon: FileText },
  { to: '/changes', label: 'Change History', icon: History },
  { to: '/sources', label: 'Monitoring Sources', icon: Globe },
  { to: '/alerts', label: 'Alerts', icon: Bell },
  { to: '/saved', label: 'Saved Notifications', icon: Bookmark },
  { to: '/profile', label: 'Profile', icon: UserCircle },
  { to: '/admin', label: 'Admin Panel', icon: ShieldCheck, adminOnly: true },
];

const ROUTE_TITLES: Record<string, string> = {
  '/dashboard': 'Overview Dashboard',
  '/notifications': 'Recruitment Notifications',
  '/changes': 'Change History Audit',
  '/sources': 'Monitoring Sources',
  '/alerts': 'Alerts & Review Center',
  '/saved': 'Saved Notifications',
  '/profile': 'Account Profile',
  '/admin': 'Admin & Scheduler Panel',
};

export function Layout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [unreadAlerts, setUnreadAlerts] = useState(0);
  const [quickQuery, setQuickQuery] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    if (!user) return;
    apiRequest<{ items: { id: number }[] }>('/alerts?unread_only=true')
      .then((res) => {
        if (!cancelled) setUnreadAlerts(res.items.length);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // Non-blocking badge count fetch
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [user, location.pathname]);

  const visibleItems = NAV_ITEMS.filter((item) => !item.adminOnly || user?.role === 'admin');
  const currentTitle =
    Object.entries(ROUTE_TITLES).find(([path]) => location.pathname.startsWith(path))?.[1] ||
    'Notification Details';

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate('/login');
  };

  const handleQuickSearch = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const trimmed = quickQuery.trim();
    if (trimmed) {
      navigate(`/notifications?q=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}`);
    } else {
      navigate('/notifications');
    }
    setMobileOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex">
      {/* Mobile Sidebar Backdrop */}
      {mobileOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-slate-900/40 lg:hidden"
          onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Fixed/Sticky Sidebar */}
      <aside
        className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 w-64 bg-white border-r border-slate-200 flex flex-col transition-transform duration-200 lg:sticky lg:top-0 lg:h-screen lg:shrink-0 lg:translate-x-0 ${
          mobileOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="h-16 px-5 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
          <Link
            to="/dashboard"
            className="text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-blue-700 font-display whitespace-nowrap"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
          >
            GovTrack
          </Link>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
            className="lg:hidden p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 rounded-md"
            aria-label="Close navigation menu"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4 space-y-1" aria-label="Primary navigation">
          {visibleItems.map((item) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            const active =
              location.pathname === item.to ||
              (item.to !== '/dashboard' && location.pathname.startsWith(`${item.to}/`));
            return (
              <Link
                key={item.to}
                to={item.to}
                onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700'
                    : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                  <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 ${active ? 'text-blue-700' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
                  <span className="truncate">{item.label}</span>
                </span>
                {item.to === '/alerts' && unreadAlerts > 0 && (
                  <span className="text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-blue-700 tabular-nums shrink-0">
                    {unreadAlerts}
                  </span>
                )}
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-200 shrink-0">
          <div className="mb-3">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 truncate">{user?.full_name}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">
              {user?.role === 'admin' ? 'Administrator' : 'Aspirant'} · {user?.email}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Sign Out</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Main Content Area */}
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex flex-col">
        <header className="h-16 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 sticky top-0 z-30">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
              className="lg:hidden p-2 -ml-2 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 rounded-lg"
              aria-label="Open navigation menu"
            >
              <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm min-w-0">
              <span className="text-slate-400 hidden sm:inline whitespace-nowrap">GovTrack</span>
              <span className="text-slate-300 hidden sm:inline" aria-hidden="true">/</span>
              <h1 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 truncate">{currentTitle}</h1>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
            <form onSubmit={handleQuickSearch} className="hidden md:flex items-center relative">
              <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 pointer-events-none" />
              <input
                type="search"
                value={quickQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setQuickQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search exam, ref, portal..."
                aria-label="Quick search notifications"
                className="w-56 lg:w-64 pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
              />
            </form>
            <Link
              to="/sources"
              className="px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-800 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              Monitor Sources
            </Link>
          </div>
        </header>

        <main className="flex-1 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto">
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/context/AuthContext.tsx =====

import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { apiRequest, getStoredToken, setStoredToken } from '../api/client';
import type { User } from '../types';

interface AuthContextValue {
  user: User | null;
  loading: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  register: (fullName: string, email: string, password: string, targetExams: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => void;
  refreshUser: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const refreshUser = useCallback(async () => {
    const token = getStoredToken();
    if (!token) {
      setUser(null);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    try {
      const data = await apiRequest<{ user: User }>('/auth/me');
      setUser(data.user);
    } catch {
      setStoredToken(null);
      setUser(null);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    refreshUser();
  }, [refreshUser]);

  const login = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    const data = await apiRequest<{ access_token: string; user: User }>('/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
    });
    setStoredToken(data.access_token);
    setUser(data.user);
  };

  const register = async (fullName: string, email: string, password: string, targetExams: string) => {
    const data = await apiRequest<{ access_token: string; user: User }>('/auth/register', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ full_name: fullName, email, password, target_exams: targetExams }),
    });
    setStoredToken(data.access_token);
    setUser(data.user);
  };

  const logout = () => {
    setStoredToken(null);
    setUser(null);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, loading, login, register, logout, refreshUser }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider');
  return ctx;
}


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: src/pages/ChangeHistoryPage.tsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { CheckCircle2, ExternalLink, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import type { ChangeHistoryItem, MonitoringSource } from '../types';
import {
  ChangeTypeLabel,
  formatFieldValue,
  formatTimestamp,
  getFieldNoun,
} from '../components/StatusBadge';
import { DiffViewer } from '../components/DiffViewer';

export function ChangeHistoryPage() {
  const [changes, setChanges] = useState<ChangeHistoryItem[]>([]);
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<MonitoringSource[]>([]);
  const [changeType, setChangeType] = useState('all');
  const [sourceCode, setSourceCode] = useState('all');
  const [changedField, setChangedField] = useState('all');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [scrapingAll, setScrapingAll] = useState(false);
  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState('');
  const [verifyingId, setVerifyingId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const loadChanges = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (changeType !== 'all') params.set('change_type', changeType);
      if (sourceCode !== 'all') params.set('source_code', sourceCode);
      if (changedField !== 'all') params.set('changed_field', changedField);
      const [chgRes, srcRes] = await Promise.all([
        apiRequest<{ items: ChangeHistoryItem[] }>(`/changes?${params.toString()}`),
        apiRequest<{ items: MonitoringSource[] }>('/sources'),
      ]);
      setChanges(chgRes.items);
      setSources(srcRes.items);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [changeType, sourceCode, changedField]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadChanges();
  }, [loadChanges]);

  const handleCheckLive = async () => {
    setScrapingAll(true);
    setStatusMessage('');
    try {
      const res = await apiRequest<{
        runs: { source_code: string; mode_used: string; new_count: number; updated_count: number }[];
      }>('/sources/scrape-all-live', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({}),
      });
      const updatedTotal = res.runs.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.updated_count || 0), 0);
      const newTotal = res.runs.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.new_count || 0), 0);
      const livePortals = res.runs.filter((r) => r.mode_used === 'live').length;
      setStatusMessage(
        `Checked ${res.runs.length} portals (${livePortals} live). Detected ${updatedTotal} field/date update(s) and ${newTotal} new official notice(s).`
      );
      await loadChanges();
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMessage(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Live check failed');
    } finally {
      setScrapingAll(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerify = async (changeId: number) => {
    setVerifyingId(changeId);
    try {
      const res = await apiRequest<{ verified_by: string }>(`/changes/${changeId}/verify`, {
        method: 'POST',
      });
      setChanges((prev) =>
        prev.map((c) => (c.id === changeId ? { ...c, is_verified: true, verified_by: res.verified_by } : c))
      );
    } finally {
      setVerifyingId(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Change History Ledger</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Field-level change detection log tracking exam dates, deadlines, admit cards, results, vacancies, and official status updates.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <select
            aria-label="Filter by change type"
            value={changeType}
            onChange={(e) => setChangeType(e.target.value)}
            className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white"
          >
            <option value="all">All Change Events</option>
            <option value="UPDATED">Field &amp; Date Updates Only</option>
            <option value="NEW">New Official Notices</option>
            <option value="REMOVED">Removed Notices</option>
          </select>
          <select
            aria-label="Filter by changed field"
            value={changedField}
            onChange={(e) => setChangedField(e.target.value)}
            className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white"
          >
            <option value="all">All Tracked Fields</option>
            <option value="exam_date">Exam Date Changes</option>
            <option value="application_deadline">Application Deadline</option>
            <option value="application_start_date">Application Start Date</option>
            <option value="admit_card_date">Admit Card Date</option>
            <option value="result_date">Result Date</option>
            <option value="vacancy_count">Vacancy Count</option>
            <option value="notification_status">Status / Cancellation</option>
            <option value="pdf_url">Official PDF Link</option>
          </select>
          <select
            aria-label="Filter by source"
            value={sourceCode}
            onChange={(e) => setSourceCode(e.target.value)}
            className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white"
          >
            <option value="all">All Portals</option>
            {sources.map((s) => (
              <option key={s.id} value={s.code}>
                {s.code.toUpperCase()}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={scrapingAll}
            onClick={handleCheckLive}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${scrapingAll ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
            <span>{scrapingAll ? 'Checking Live Portals...' : 'Check Live Sources'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {statusMessage && (
        <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-900">
          {statusMessage}
        </div>
      )}

      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-32 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : changes.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-8 text-center space-y-3">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">No matching field-level changes recorded yet</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto">
            Change History records individual field changes whenever a live check detects an updated exam date, deadline extension, vacancy revision, admit card release, result update, or new official notice.
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={scrapingAll}
            onClick={handleCheckLive}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${scrapingAll ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
            <span>{scrapingAll ? 'Scanning Portals...' : 'Scan Live Official Portals Now'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3.5">
          {changes.map((chg) => {
            const noun = getFieldNoun(chg.changed_field);
            const isFieldUpdate = chg.change_type === 'UPDATED' || chg.change_type === 'REMOVED';
            const headline =
              chg.change_label ||
              (chg.change_type === 'NEW'
                ? 'NEW NOTIFICATION PUBLISHED'
                : chg.change_type === 'REMOVED'
                  ? 'NOTIFICATION REMOVED FROM PORTAL'
                  : 'NOTIFICATION UPDATED');

            return (
              <article key={chg.id} className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5 space-y-3.5">
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-block text-xs font-bold tracking-wide uppercase px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md ${
                          headline.includes('CANCELLED') || chg.change_type === 'REMOVED'
                            ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200'
                            : chg.change_type === 'UPDATED'
                              ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200'
                              : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {headline}
                      </span>
                      <ChangeTypeLabel type={chg.change_type} />
                      <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">{chg.run_id}</span>
                    </div>

                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display pt-1">
                      <span className="text-blue-700">{chg.source_code.toUpperCase()}</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-400 mx-1.5">–</span>
                      {chg.notification_id ? (
                        <Link to={`/notifications/${chg.notification_id}`} className="hover:text-blue-700">
                          {chg.notification_title}
                        </Link>
                      ) : (
                        chg.notification_title
                      )}
                    </h3>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                    <a
                      href={chg.source_url}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 whitespace-nowrap"
                    >
                      <span>View Official Source</span>
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </a>
                    {chg.is_verified ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>Verified ({chg.verified_by})</span>
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={verifyingId === chg.id}
                        onClick={() => handleVerify(chg.id)}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        {verifyingId === chg.id ? 'Verifying...' : 'Verify Change'}
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {isFieldUpdate ? (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-3.5 text-sm">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Previous {noun}</p>
                      <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono font-semibold text-red-700 break-words tabular-nums">
                        {formatFieldValue(chg.changed_field, chg.previous_value)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">New {noun}</p>
                      <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono font-semibold text-emerald-800 break-words tabular-nums">
                        {formatFieldValue(chg.changed_field, chg.new_value)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <DiffViewer diffs={chg.changed_fields} changeType={chg.change_type} />
                )}

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 pt-1 border-t border-slate-100">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-5 gap-y-1">
                    <span>
                      <strong className="text-slate-700">Detected At:</strong>{' '}
                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{formatTimestamp(chg.detected_at)}</span>
                    </span>
                    <span>
                      <strong className="text-slate-700">Source:</strong>{' '}
                      {chg.source_name || `Official ${chg.source_code.toUpperCase()} website`}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {chg.notification_id && (
                    <Link
                      to={`/notifications/${chg.notification_id}`}
                      className="font-semibold text-blue-700 hover:underline"
                    >
                      Inspect Full Version History →
                    </Link>
                  )}
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight, ExternalLink, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import type { DashboardPayload } from '../types';
import {
  ChangeTypeLabel,
  FreshnessStatus,
  formatDeadlineCountdown,
  formatFieldValue,
  formatTimestamp,
} from '../components/StatusBadge';

export function DashboardPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<DashboardPayload | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [syncingId, setSyncingId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const payload = await apiRequest<DashboardPayload>('/dashboard');
      setData(payload);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load dashboard');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  const handleQuickScrape = async (sourceId: number) => {
    setSyncingId(sourceId);
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/sources/${sourceId}/scrape`, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({}),
      });
      await loadDashboard();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Source check failed');
    } finally {
      setSyncingId(null);
    }
  };

  if (loading && !data) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-24 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="h-64 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error && !data) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 bg-white border border-red-200 rounded-xl">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-700">{error}</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={loadDashboard}
          className="mt-3 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-800 cursor-pointer"
        >
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!data) return null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      {/* Top Header Strip */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Monitoring Overview</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Centralized tracking across {data.stats.monitored_sources} official Indian recruitment portals.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={loadDashboard}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
        >
          <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
          <span>Refresh View</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* 4-Column KPI Strip */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5">
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Active Notifications</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 tabular-nums font-display">
            {data.stats.active_notifications}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Across {data.stats.monitored_sources} portals</p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5">
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Healthy Sources</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-blue-700 tabular-nums font-display">
            {data.stats.healthy_sources}/{data.stats.monitored_sources}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Freshness verified</p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5">
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Detected Changes</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 tabular-nums font-display">
            {data.stats.total_changes}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Field-level audit entries</p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5">
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Unread Alerts</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 tabular-nums font-display">
            {data.stats.unread_alerts}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">{data.stats.saved_count} saved bookmarks</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Upcoming Deadlines + Recent Changes 2-Column Section */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 items-start">
        {/* Upcoming Deadlines */}
        <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
          <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 font-display">Upcoming Application Deadlines</h3>
            <Link
              to="/notifications"
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap"
            >
              <span>View all</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </Link>
          </div>
          {data.upcoming_deadlines.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-6 text-sm text-slate-500">No upcoming deadlines found.</div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {data.upcoming_deadlines.map((item) => {
                const dl = formatDeadlineCountdown(item.application_deadline);
                return (
                  <li key={item.id} className="px-5 py-3.5 hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition-colors">
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <Link
                          to={`/notifications/${item.id}`}
                          className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 hover:text-blue-700 line-clamp-1"
                        >
                          {item.title}
                        </Link>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                          {item.source_code.toUpperCase()}
                          <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
                          <span>{item.notification_number || 'Ref Unavailable'}</span>
                          <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
                          <span className="tabular-nums">
                            {item.vacancy_count ? `${item.vacancy_count} vacancies` : 'Vacancies Unavailable'}
                          </span>
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <span
                        className={`text-xs font-mono tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                          dl.urgent ? 'text-amber-700 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-600'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {dl.text}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          )}
        </section>

        {/* Recent Change History */}
        <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
          <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 font-display">Recent Change Activity</h3>
            <Link
              to="/changes"
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap"
            >
              <span>Full log</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </Link>
          </div>
          {data.recent_changes.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-6 text-sm text-slate-500">No field-level changes detected yet.</div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {data.recent_changes.map((chg) => (
                <li key={chg.id} className="px-5 py-3.5 hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition-colors">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <ChangeTypeLabel type={chg.change_type} />
                        {chg.change_label && (
                          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
                            {chg.change_label}
                          </span>
                        )}
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-500 uppercase">{chg.source_code}</span>
                      </div>
                      <Link
                        to={chg.notification_id ? `/notifications/${chg.notification_id}` : '/changes'}
                        className="mt-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 hover:text-blue-700 line-clamp-1"
                      >
                        {chg.notification_title}
                      </Link>
                      {chg.change_type === 'UPDATED' && (
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-slate-700 truncate">
                          <span className="text-red-700">
                            {formatFieldValue(chg.changed_field, chg.previous_value)}
                          </span>
                          <span className="mx-1.5 text-slate-400">→</span>
                          <span className="text-emerald-700 font-semibold">
                            {formatFieldValue(chg.changed_field, chg.new_value)}
                          </span>
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono tabular-nums">
                        {formatTimestamp(chg.detected_at)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
                      {chg.run_id}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* Monitoring Source Freshness Table */}
      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 font-display">Source Freshness &amp; Status</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
              Last attempted vs. last successful check across all 10 official government recruitment websites.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Link
            to="/sources"
            className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 hover:underline whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            Manage Sources
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Portal</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Freshness</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Dataset Mode</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">Parsed</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Last Successful Sync</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">Action</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {data.sources.map((src) => (
                <tr key={src.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                  <td className="py-3 px-4">
                    <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{src.code.toUpperCase()}</div>
                    <a
                      href={src.base_url}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="text-xs text-slate-500 hover:text-blue-700 inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      <span className="truncate max-w-[220px]">{src.base_url}</span>
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                    </a>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4">
                    <FreshnessStatus status={src.freshness_status} />
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs text-slate-600">
                    {src.is_demo_data ? 'Offline HTML Fixture (Demo)' : 'Live Verified'}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-slate-700">
                    {src.last_parsed_count}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-slate-600 whitespace-nowrap">
                    {formatTimestamp(src.last_successful_at)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={syncingId === src.id}
                      onClick={() => handleQuickScrape(src.id)}
                      className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-md transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      {syncingId === src.id ? 'Checking...' : 'Check Now'}
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/pages/LoginPage.tsx =====

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight, CheckCircle2, Shield } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

export function LoginPage() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await login(email, password);
      navigate('/dashboard');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to sign in');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const fillDemo = (demoEmail: string, demoPassword: string) => {
    setEmail(demoEmail);
    setPassword(demoPassword);
    setError('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex flex-col lg:flex-row">
      {/* Left Institutional Value Panel */}
      <div className="lg:w-1/2 bg-blue-900 text-white p-8 sm:p-12 lg:p-16 flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div>
          <span className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight font-display">GovTrack</span>
          <h1 className="mt-10 text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight leading-tight text-balance font-display">
            Intelligent Government Recruitment Notification Monitoring
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-4 text-blue-100 text-base leading-relaxed max-w-lg">
            Automatically track announcements, vacancy revisions, deadline extensions, admit cards, and exam schedules across 10 official Indian recruitment portals with auditable before-and-after change evidence.
          </p>

          <ul className="mt-8 space-y-3 text-sm text-blue-100">
            <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-300 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>Dedicated parsers for UPSC, SSC, TNPSC, TNUSRB, RRB, IBPS, SBI, RBI, NTA, and India Post</span>
            </li>
            <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-300 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>Versioned before-and-after field diffing with duplicate suppression</span>
            </li>
            <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-300 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>Respectful robots.txt &amp; HTTP 403/429 handling — errors never cause false deletions</span>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </div>

        <p className="mt-10 text-xs text-blue-200">
          Public non-sensitive notification monitoring · Source-attributed &amp; timestamped
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Right Sign In Form */}
      <div className="lg:w-1/2 flex items-center justify-center p-6 sm:p-12">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6 sm:p-8">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Sign in to GovTrack</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">Access your recruitment monitoring workspace.</p>

          {error && (
            <div className="mt-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-xs text-red-700">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-6 space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                Email Address
              </label>
              <input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="aspirant@govtrack.in"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600 focus:border-blue-600"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                Password
              </label>
              <input
                id="password"
                type="password"
                required
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Enter your password"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600 focus:border-blue-600"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"

              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-4 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >

===== FILE: src/pages/NotificationDetailsPage.tsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Bookmark, ExternalLink, FileDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import type {
  ChangeHistoryItem,
  MonitoringSource,
  NotificationVersionItem,
  RecruitmentNotification,
} from '../types';
import {
  ChangeTypeLabel,
  formatFieldValue,
  formatHumanDate,
  formatTimestamp,
  getFieldNoun,
} from '../components/StatusBadge';
import { DiffViewer } from '../components/DiffViewer';

interface DetailPayload {
  notification: RecruitmentNotification;
  source: MonitoringSource | null;
  changes: ChangeHistoryItem[];
  versions?: NotificationVersionItem[];
}

export function NotificationDetailsPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const [data, setData] = useState<DetailPayload | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const loadDetail = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const payload = await apiRequest<DetailPayload>(`/notifications/${id}`);
      setData(payload);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Notification not found');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDetail();
  }, [loadDetail]);

  const toggleSave = async () => {
    if (!data) return;
    const notif = data.notification;
    if (notif.is_saved) {
      await apiRequest(`/saved/${notif.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    } else {
      await apiRequest(`/saved/${notif.id}`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ notes: '' }) });
    }
    setData({
      ...data,
      notification: { ...notif, is_saved: !notif.is_saved },
    });
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="h-64 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />;
  }

  if (error || !data) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-700">{error || 'Notification not found'}</p>
        <Link to="/notifications" className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700">
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>Back to Notifications</span>
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const { notification: n, changes, versions = [] } = data;
  const fieldRows: [string, string | null, boolean][] = [
    ['Organization Name', n.organization_name, false],
    ['Notification Number', n.notification_number, false],
    ['Notification Status', n.lifecycle_status.toUpperCase(), false],
    ['Vacancy Count', n.vacancy_count, false],
    ['Job Category', n.job_category, false],
    ['Published Date', n.published_date, true],
    ['Application Start Date', n.application_start_date, true],
    ['Application Deadline', n.application_deadline, true],
    ['Examination Date', n.exam_date, true],
    ['Admit Card Release Date', n.admit_card_date, true],
    ['Result Date', n.result_date, true],
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <Link
          to="/notifications"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-blue-700"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>Back to Notifications</span>
        </Link>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={toggleSave}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg border cursor-pointer ${
            n.is_saved
              ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-200 text-blue-700'
              : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
          }`}
        >
          <Bookmark className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${n.is_saved ? 'fill-blue-700' : ''}`} />
          <span>{n.is_saved ? 'Bookmarked' : 'Save Notification'}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Main Notification Card */}
      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6">
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
          <span className="font-semibold text-blue-700">{n.source_code.toUpperCase()}</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span>{n.is_demo_data ? 'Offline Fixture Snapshot (Demo)' : 'Live Verified Extraction'}</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span className="font-mono">Key: {n.entity_key}</span>
        </div>

        <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display text-balance">{n.title}</h2>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">
          {n.description || 'No extended description provided on the official notice.'}
        </p>

        {/* Source Attribution & Official PDF Links */}
        <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <a
            href={n.source_url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <span>Open Original Source</span>
            <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </a>
          {n.pdf_url ? (
            <a
              href={n.pdf_url}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap"
            >
              <FileDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Official Notification PDF</span>
            </a>
          ) : (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap">
              Official PDF Not Published Separately
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Structured Extraction Table */}
        <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-slate-200">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-3">
            Structured Fields Extracted (Unextracted fields marked Unavailable)
          </h3>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-x-6 gap-y-4">
            {fieldRows.map(([label, val, isDate]) => (
              <div key={label} className="border-b border-slate-100 pb-2.5">
                <dt className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</dt>
                <dd className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 tabular-nums">
                  {val ? (
                    isDate ? formatHumanDate(val) : val
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-slate-400 font-normal">Unavailable</span>
                  )}
                </dd>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Provenance Timestamps */}
        <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 text-xs text-slate-500 flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-1 font-mono tabular-nums">
          <span>First Seen: {formatTimestamp(n.first_seen_at)}</span>
          <span>Last Checked: {formatTimestamp(n.last_seen_at)}</span>
          <span>Run ID: {n.last_run_id || 'Unavailable'}</span>
          <span>SHA-256: {n.content_hash.slice(0, 12)}...</span>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Field-Level Change History */}
      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6">
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display mb-4">
          Field-Level Change History ({changes.length})
        </h3>
        {changes.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">No change history recorded for this item yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {changes.map((chg) => {
              const noun = getFieldNoun(chg.changed_field);
              return (
                <div key={chg.id} className="pb-4 border-b border-slate-100 last:border-b-0 last:pb-0 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      {chg.change_label && (
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                          {chg.change_label}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <ChangeTypeLabel type={chg.change_type} />
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-600">{formatTimestamp(chg.detected_at)}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-500">{chg.run_id}</span>
                    </div>
                    {chg.is_verified && (
                      <span className="text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700">
                        Verified by {chg.verified_by}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  {chg.change_type === 'UPDATED' ? (
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs">
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-slate-500">Previous {noun}: </span>
                        <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-red-700">
                          {formatFieldValue(chg.changed_field, chg.previous_value)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-slate-500">New {noun}: </span>
                        <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-emerald-800">
                          {formatFieldValue(chg.changed_field, chg.new_value)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <DiffViewer diffs={chg.changed_fields} changeType={chg.change_type} />
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Version Snapshot History */}
      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6">
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display mb-4">
          Saved Version Snapshots ({versions.length})
        </h3>
        {versions.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">No historical version snapshots saved yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-lg">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600">
                  <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">Version</th>
                  <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">Captured At</th>
                  <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">Status</th>
                  <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">Apply Window</th>
                  <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">Exam Date</th>
                  <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">Admit / Result</th>
                  <th className="py-2 px-3 font-semibold">Vacancies</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {versions.map((ver) => (
                  <tr key={ver.id} className="align-top">
                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono font-bold text-blue-700">v{ver.version_number}</td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono text-slate-600 whitespace-nowrap">
                      {formatTimestamp(ver.captured_at)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 uppercase font-semibold text-slate-700">
                      {ver.notification_status}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 tabular-nums">
                      {formatHumanDate(ver.application_start_date)} → {formatHumanDate(ver.application_deadline)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-semibold text-slate-900 tabular-nums">
                      {formatHumanDate(ver.exam_date)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 tabular-nums">
                      Admit: {formatHumanDate(ver.admit_card_date)} / Result: {formatHumanDate(ver.result_date)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 px-3 tabular-nums">
                      {ver.vacancy_count || 'Unavailable'}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
