===== FILE: src/pages/NotificationsPage.tsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Bookmark, ExternalLink, FileDown, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import type { MonitoringSource, RecruitmentNotification } from '../types';
import { formatDeadlineCountdown, formatHumanDate } from '../components/StatusBadge';

export function NotificationsPage() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<RecruitmentNotification[]>([]);
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<MonitoringSource[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const [sourceCode, setSourceCode] = useState('all');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('all');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('all');
  const [sort, setSort] = useState('deadline');

  const fetchNotifications = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (q.trim()) params.set('q', q.trim());
      if (sourceCode !== 'all') params.set('source_code', sourceCode);
      if (category !== 'all') params.set('category', category);
      if (statusFilter !== 'all') params.set('status_filter', statusFilter);
      params.set('sort', sort);

      const [notifRes, srcRes] = await Promise.all([
        apiRequest<{ items: RecruitmentNotification[]; categories: string[] }>(`/notifications?${params.toString()}`),
        apiRequest<{ items: MonitoringSource[] }>('/sources'),
      ]);
      setItems(notifRes.items);
      setCategories(notifRes.categories);
      setSources(srcRes.items);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load notifications');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [q, sourceCode, category, statusFilter, sort]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchNotifications();
  }, [fetchNotifications]);

  const toggleSave = async (notif: RecruitmentNotification) => {
    try {
      if (notif.is_saved) {
        await apiRequest(`/saved/${notif.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      } else {
        await apiRequest(`/saved/${notif.id}`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ notes: '' }) });
      }
      setItems((prev) =>
        prev.map((item) => (item.id === notif.id ? { ...item, is_saved: !notif.is_saved } : item))
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to update bookmark');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Recruitment Notifications</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
          Search and filter structured recruitment records extracted from all 10 official government portals.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Filter Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3">
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search by exam title, ref number, or keyword..."
            className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
          />
        </div>

        <select
          aria-label="Filter by portal"
          value={sourceCode}
          onChange={(e) => setSourceCode(e.target.value)}
          className="px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
        >
          <option value="all">All Portals (10)</option>
          {sources.map((s) => (
            <option key={s.id} value={s.code}>
              {s.code.toUpperCase()} — {s.organization_name.split('(')[0].trim()}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <select
          aria-label="Filter by category"
          value={category}
          onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
          className="px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
        >
          <option value="all">All Categories</option>
          {categories.map((cat) => (
            <option key={cat} value={cat}>
              {cat}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <select
            aria-label="Filter by status"
            value={statusFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="flex-1 px-2.5 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
          >
            <option value="all">All States</option>
            <option value="active">Active</option>
            <option value="updated">Updated</option>
            <option value="cancelled">Cancelled</option>
            <option value="removed">Removed</option>
          </select>
          <select
            aria-label="Sort notifications"
            value={sort}
            onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)}
            className="flex-1 px-2.5 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
          >
            <option value="deadline">Deadline</option>
            <option value="newest">Newest</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-red-700">{error}</div>
      )}

      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-28 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-10 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">No matching recruitment notifications</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Adjust your search keywords or reset portal filters.</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setQ('');
              setSourceCode('all');
              setCategory('all');
              setStatusFilter('all');
            }}
            className="mt-4 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-800 cursor-pointer"
          >
            Reset Filters
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {items.map((item) => {
            const dl = formatDeadlineCountdown(item.application_deadline);
            return (
              <article
                key={item.id}
                className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5 hover:border-blue-300 transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-4">
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-blue-700">{item.source_code.toUpperCase()}</span>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span>{item.job_category || 'General'}</span>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="font-mono">{item.notification_number || 'Ref Unavailable'}</span>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span>{item.is_demo_data ? 'Offline Fixture' : 'Live Verified'}</span>
                      {item.lifecycle_status !== 'active' && (
                        <>
                          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                          <span
                            className={`font-semibold ${
                              item.lifecycle_status === 'removed' || item.lifecycle_status === 'cancelled'
                                ? 'text-red-700'
                                : 'text-amber-700'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {item.lifecycle_status === 'removed'
                              ? 'Removed from portal'
                              : item.lifecycle_status === 'cancelled'
                                ? 'Cancelled on portal'
                                : 'Recently Updated'}
                          </span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <h3 className="mt-1.5 text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">
                      <Link to={`/notifications/${item.id}`} className="hover:text-blue-700">
                        {item.title}
                      </Link>
                    </h3>

                    {item.description && (
                      <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-slate-600 line-clamp-2">{item.description}</p>
                    )}

                    <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-slate-600 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 tabular-nums">
                      <span>
                        <strong className="text-slate-700">Apply From:</strong>{' '}
                        {formatHumanDate(item.application_start_date)}
                      </span>
                      <span>
                        <strong className="text-slate-700">Deadline:</strong>{' '}
                        <span className={dl.urgent ? 'text-amber-700 font-semibold' : ''}>{dl.text}</span>
                      </span>
                      <span>
                        <strong className="text-slate-700">Exam Date:</strong>{' '}
                        {formatHumanDate(item.exam_date)}
                      </span>
                      {item.admit_card_date && (
                        <span>
                          <strong className="text-slate-700">Admit Card:</strong>{' '}
                          {formatHumanDate(item.admit_card_date)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {item.result_date && (
                        <span>
                          <strong className="text-slate-700">Result Date:</strong>{' '}
                          {formatHumanDate(item.result_date)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <span>
                        <strong className="text-slate-700">Vacancies:</strong>{' '}
                        {item.vacancy_count || 'Unavailable'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex sm:flex-row md:flex-col items-center md:items-end gap-2 shrink-0">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleSave(item)}
                      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg border transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer ${
                        item.is_saved
                          ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-200 text-blue-700'
                          : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Bookmark className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${item.is_saved ? 'fill-blue-700' : ''}`} />
                      <span>{item.is_saved ? 'Saved' : 'Save'}</span>
                    </button>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <a
                        href={item.pdf_url || `/api/notifications/${item.id}/pdf`}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap"
                      >
                        <FileDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>{item.pdf_url ? 'Official PDF' : 'Summary PDF'}</span>
                      </a>
                      <a
                        href={item.source_url}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 hover:underline whitespace-nowrap"
                      >
                        <span>Source</span>
                        <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                      </a>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/pages/RegisterPage.tsx =====

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

export function RegisterPage() {
  const { register } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [targetExams, setTargetExams] = useState('UPSC, SSC, IBPS');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await register(fullName, email, password, targetExams);
      navigate('/dashboard');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Registration failed');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center p-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6 sm:p-8">
        <Link to="/login" className="text-base font-bold text-blue-700 font-display">
          GovTrack
        </Link>
        <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Create Aspirant Account</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
          Track official government recruitment deadlines and notification updates.
        </p>

        {error && (
          <div className="mt-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-xs text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-6 space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="fullName" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Full Name
            </label>
            <input
              id="fullName"
              type="text"
              required
              minLength={2}
              value={fullName}
              onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. Karthik Sundaram"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600 focus:border-blue-600"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="regEmail" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Email Address
            </label>
            <input
              id="regEmail"
              type="email"
              required
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600 focus:border-blue-600"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="regPassword" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Password (minimum 6 characters)
            </label>
            <input
              id="regPassword"
              type="password"
              required
              minLength={6}
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Choose a strong password"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600 focus:border-blue-600"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="targetExams" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Target Examinations (optional)
            </label>
            <input
              id="targetExams"
              type="text"
              value={targetExams}
              onChange={(e) => setTargetExams(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="UPSC, SSC, TNPSC, IBPS"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600 focus:border-blue-600"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={submitting}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-4 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <span>{submitting ? 'Creating Account...' : 'Register'}</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </form>

        <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-600">
          Already registered?{' '}
          <Link to="/login" className="font-semibold text-blue-700 hover:underline">
            Sign in
          </Link>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: src/pages/AlertsPage.tsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Check, CheckCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import type { AlertItem } from '../types';
import { ChangeTypeLabel, formatTimestamp } from '../components/StatusBadge';

export function AlertsPage() {
  const [alerts, setAlerts] = useState<AlertItem[]>([]);
  const [unreadOnly, setUnreadOnly] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const loadAlerts = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiRequest<{ items: AlertItem[] }>(`/alerts?unread_only=${unreadOnly}`);
      setAlerts(res.items);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [unreadOnly]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadAlerts();
  }, [loadAlerts]);

  const markRead = async (id: number) => {
    await apiRequest(`/alerts/${id}/read`, { method: 'POST' });
    setAlerts((prev) => prev.map((a) => (a.id === id ? { ...a, is_read: true } : a)));
  };

  const markAllRead = async () => {
    await apiRequest('/alerts/read-all', { method: 'POST' });
    setAlerts((prev) => prev.map((a) => ({ ...a, is_read: true })));
  };

  const unreadCount = alerts.filter((a) => !a.is_read).length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Alerts &amp; Review Center</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Real-time change notifications and source error alerts with duplicate suppression.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center p-1 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setUnreadOnly(false)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-md cursor-pointer ${
                !unreadOnly ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-600'
              }`}
            >
              All Alerts
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setUnreadOnly(true)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-md cursor-pointer ${
                unreadOnly ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-600'
              }`}
            >
              Unread Only
            </button>
          </div>

          {unreadCount > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={markAllRead}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
            >
              <CheckCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Mark All Read</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-20 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : alerts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
          {unreadOnly ? 'No unread alerts remaining.' : 'No alerts recorded yet.'}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl divide-y divide-slate-100">
          {alerts.map((alert) => (
            <div
              key={alert.id}
              className={`p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 ${
                alert.is_read ? 'bg-white' : 'bg-blue-50/35'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                  <ChangeTypeLabel type={alert.alert_type} />
                  <span className="text-slate-300" aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-blue-700 uppercase">{alert.source_code}</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-300" aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-slate-500 tabular-nums">{formatTimestamp(alert.created_at)}</span>
                  {!alert.is_read && (
                    <>
                      <span className="text-slate-300" aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="font-semibold text-blue-700">Unread</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>

                <h3 className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {alert.notification_id ? (
                    <Link to={`/notifications/${alert.notification_id}`} className="hover:text-blue-700">
                      {alert.title}
                    </Link>
                  ) : (
                    alert.title
                  )}
                </h3>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-600">{alert.message}</p>
              </div>

              {!alert.is_read && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => markRead(alert.id)}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Mark Read</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: src/pages/ProfilePage.tsx =====

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

export function ProfilePage() {
  const { user, refreshUser } = useAuth();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState(user?.full_name || '');
  const [targetExams, setTargetExams] = useState(user?.target_exams || '');
  const [emailAlerts, setEmailAlerts] = useState(Boolean(user?.email_alerts_enabled));
  const [profileMsg, setProfileMsg] = useState('');
  const [profileErr, setProfileErr] = useState('');

  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
  const [pwMsg, setPwMsg] = useState('');
  const [pwErr, setPwErr] = useState('');

  const handleProfileSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setProfileMsg('');
    setProfileErr('');
    try {
      await apiRequest('/profile', {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          full_name: fullName,
          target_exams: targetExams,
          email_alerts_enabled: emailAlerts,
        }),
      });
      await refreshUser();
      setProfileMsg('Profile preferences updated.');
    } catch (err) {
      setProfileErr(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to update profile');
    }
  };

  const handlePasswordChange = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setPwMsg('');
    setPwErr('');
    try {
      await apiRequest('/profile/password', {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: JSON.stringify({ current_password: currentPassword, new_password: newPassword }),
      });
      setCurrentPassword('');
      setNewPassword('');
      setPwMsg('Password updated successfully.');
    } catch (err) {
      setPwErr(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to change password');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-3xl">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Account Profile</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
          Manage your aspirant exam targets, alert preferences, and account password.
        </p>
      </div>

      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-4 border-b border-slate-100">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{user?.email}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Role: {user?.role === 'admin' ? 'Administrator' : 'Aspirant'} · Account Active
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleProfileSave} className="mt-5 space-y-4">
          {profileMsg && (
            <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-emerald-700">
              {profileMsg}
            </div>
          )}
          {profileErr && (
            <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-red-700">
              {profileErr}
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="profName" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Full Name
            </label>
            <input
              id="profName"
              type="text"
              required
              value={fullName}
              onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="profExams" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Target Examinations
            </label>
            <input
              id="profExams"
              type="text"
              value={targetExams}
              onChange={(e) => setTargetExams(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="UPSC, SSC, TNPSC, IBPS"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
            />
          </div>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-sm text-slate-700 cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={emailAlerts}
              onChange={(e) => setEmailAlerts(e.target.checked)}
              className="w-4 h-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-blue-700 focus:ring-blue-600"
            />
            <span>Receive dashboard change alert notifications</span>
          </label>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
          >
            Save Profile
          </button>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6">
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display mb-4">Change Password</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handlePasswordChange} className="space-y-4">
          {pwMsg && (
            <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-emerald-700">
              {pwMsg}
            </div>
          )}
          {pwErr && (
            <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-red-700">{pwErr}</div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="curPw" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                Current Password
              </label>
              <input
                id="curPw"
                type="password"
                required
                value={currentPassword}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="newPw" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                New Password
              </label>
              <input
                id="newPw"
                type="password"
                required
                minLength={6}
                value={newPassword}
                onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
          >
            Update Password
          </button>
        </form>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/pages/SourcesPage.tsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ExternalLink, Play, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import type { MonitoringSource } from '../types';
import { FreshnessStatus, formatTimestamp } from '../components/StatusBadge';

interface RunResultBanner {
  sourceCode: string;
  run_id: string;
  status: string;
  mode_used: string;
  http_status: number | null;
  parsed_count: number;
  new_count: number;
  updated_count: number;
  duplicate_count: number;
  removed_count: number;
  error_message: string | null;
}

export function SourcesPage() {
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<MonitoringSource[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busyKey, setBusyKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lastResult, setLastResult] = useState<RunResultBanner | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const loadSources = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const data = await apiRequest<{ items: MonitoringSource[] }>('/sources');
      setSources(data.items);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load sources');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadSources();
  }, [loadSources]);

  const triggerScrape = async (
    src: MonitoringSource,
    options: { mode_override?: string; fixture_override?: string; simulate_http_status?: number } = {},
    actionTag = 'default'
  ) => {
    setBusyKey(`${src.id}-${actionTag}`);
    setError('');
    try {
      const res = await apiRequest<{ source: MonitoringSource; run: Omit<RunResultBanner, 'sourceCode'> }>(
        `/sources/${src.id}/scrape`,
        {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify(options),
        }
      );
      setLastResult({ sourceCode: src.code, ...res.run });
      setSources((prev) => prev.map((item) => (item.id === src.id ? res.source : item)));
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Scrape run failed');
    } finally {
      setBusyKey(null);
    }
  };

  const [scrapingAllLive, setScrapingAllLive] = useState(false);
  const [batchSummary, setBatchSummary] = useState('');

  const handleScrapeAllLive = async () => {
    setScrapingAllLive(true);
    setError('');
    setBatchSummary('');
    try {
      const res = await apiRequest<{
        runs: { source_code: string; mode_used: string; parsed_count: number; new_count: number; updated_count: number }[];
      }>('/sources/scrape-all-live', { method: 'POST' });
      const liveCount = res.runs.filter((r) => r.mode_used === 'live').length;
      const totalNew = res.runs.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.new_count, 0);
      const totalParsed = res.runs.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.parsed_count, 0);
      setBatchSummary(
        `Completed live crawl across ${res.runs.length} portals (${liveCount} live HTTP 200 extractions, ${
          res.runs.length - liveCount
        } verified fixture fallbacks). Parsed ${totalParsed} notices (${totalNew} newly discovered).`
      );
      await loadSources();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Batch live scrape failed');
    } finally {
      setScrapingAllLive(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Monitoring Sources</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            10 approved Indian government recruitment portals with dedicated parsers, robots.txt compliance, and offline HTML replay fixtures.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={scrapingAllLive || busyKey !== null}
            onClick={handleScrapeAllLive}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{scrapingAllLive ? 'Scraping All Live...' : 'Scrape All Live Portals'}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={loadSources}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
          >
            <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
            <span>Refresh</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {batchSummary && (
        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl border bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-xs text-emerald-900 font-medium">
          {batchSummary}
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-red-700">{error}</div>
      )}

      {lastResult && (
        <div
          className={`p-4 rounded-xl border text-xs ${
            lastResult.status === 'success'
              ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-200 text-blue-900'
              : 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-200 text-amber-900'
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <span className="font-semibold">
              {lastResult.sourceCode.toUpperCase()} Scrape Run {lastResult.run_id} ({lastResult.status})
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
              Mode: {lastResult.mode_used} · HTTP {lastResult.http_status ?? 'N/A'} · Parsed {lastResult.parsed_count} · New{' '}
              {lastResult.new_count} · Updated {lastResult.updated_count} · Duplicates {lastResult.duplicate_count}
            </span>
          </div>
          {lastResult.error_message && <p className="mt-1.5 text-amber-800">{lastResult.error_message}</p>}
        </div>
      )}

      {loading && sources.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-32 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {sources.map((src) => (
            <section key={src.id} className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5">
              <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">{src.organization_name}</h3>
                    <FreshnessStatus status={src.freshness_status} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
                    <a
                      href={src.base_url}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="text-blue-700 hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      <span>{src.base_url}</span>
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </a>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span>Parser: {src.parser_key}</span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span>{src.is_demo_data ? 'Offline HTML Fixture (Demo)' : 'Live Verified'}</span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span className="tabular-nums">Every {src.crawl_interval_minutes}m (delay {src.rate_limit_seconds}s)</span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs text-slate-600">{src.robots_policy}</p>

                  {src.last_error && (
                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
                      Last Error (HTTP {src.last_http_status ?? 'N/A'}): {src.last_error}
                    </p>
                  )}

                  <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono tabular-nums flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1">
                    <span>Attempted: {formatTimestamp(src.last_attempted_at)}</span>
                    <span>Last Success: {formatTimestamp(src.last_successful_at)}</span>
                    <span>Parsed: {src.last_parsed_count}</span>
                    <span>Duplicates Suppressed: {src.last_duplicate_count}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Action Controls */}
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={busyKey !== null}
                    onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { mode_override: 'fixture' }, 'fixture')}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>{busyKey === `${src.id}-fixture` ? 'Running...' : 'Run Fixture Scrape'}</span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={busyKey !== null}
                    onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { mode_override: 'auto' }, 'live')}
                    className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {busyKey === `${src.id}-live` ? 'Fetching...' : 'Try Live (Auto Fallback)'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Built-in Change Detection & Failure Simulation Controls for UPSC / SSC */}
              {(src.code === 'upsc' || src.code === 'ssc') && (
                <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mr-1">Snapshot Replay Tests:</span>
                  {src.code === 'upsc' && (
                    <>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={busyKey !== null}
                        onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { fixture_override: 'upsc_v2_updated.html' }, 'v2')}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Replay v2 (Deadline Extended + New)
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={busyKey !== null}
                        onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { fixture_override: 'upsc_format_only.html' }, 'fmt')}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Replay Formatting-Only Edit
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={busyKey !== null}
                        onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { simulate_http_status: 404 }, '404')}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-red-700 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Simulate HTTP 404
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={busyKey !== null}
                        onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { fixture_override: 'upsc.html' }, 'restore')}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Restore Baseline
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                  {src.code === 'ssc' && (
                    <>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={busyKey !== null}
                        onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { fixture_override: 'ssc_v2_updated.html' }, 'v2')}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Replay v2 (Admit Card + Result)
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={busyKey !== null}
                        onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { simulate_http_status: 429 }, '429')}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Simulate HTTP 429
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={busyKey !== null}
                        onClick={() => triggerScrape(src, { fixture_override: 'ssc.html' }, 'restore')}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Restore Baseline
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/types.ts =====

export interface User {
  id: number;
  full_name: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'user' | 'admin';
  target_exams: string | null;
  email_alerts_enabled: boolean;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface MonitoringSource {
  id: number;
  code: string;
  name: string;
  organization_name: string;
  base_url: string;
  parser_key: string;
  fixture_filename: string;
  scrape_mode: 'live' | 'fixture' | 'auto';
  crawl_interval_minutes: number;
  rate_limit_seconds: number;
  robots_policy: string;
  permission_note: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  is_demo_data: boolean;
  freshness_status: 'Fresh' | 'Stale' | 'Error' | 'Rate Limited' | 'Pending';
  last_attempted_at: string | null;
  last_successful_at: string | null;
  last_http_status: number | null;
  last_error: string | null;
  last_parsed_count: number;
  last_duplicate_count: number;
}

export interface RecruitmentNotification {
  id: number;
  source_id: number;
  source_code: string;
  entity_key: string;
  content_hash: string;
  title: string;
  organization_name: string;
  notification_number: string | null;
  vacancy_count: string | null;
  job_category: string | null;
  application_start_date: string | null;
  application_deadline: string | null;
  exam_date: string | null;
  admit_card_date: string | null;
  result_date: string | null;
  description: string | null;
  pdf_url: string | null;
  source_url: string;
  published_date: string | null;
  lifecycle_status: 'active' | 'updated' | 'cancelled' | 'removed';
  is_removed: boolean;
  is_demo_data: boolean;
  first_seen_at: string;
  last_seen_at: string;
  source_updated_at: string | null;
  last_run_id: string | null;
  is_saved?: boolean;
  saved_notes?: string | null;
  saved_at?: string | null;
}

export interface NotificationVersionItem {
  id: number;
  notification_id: number;
  version_number: number;
  title: string;
  organization_name: string;
  notification_number: string | null;
  vacancy_count: string | null;
  job_category: string | null;
  application_start_date: string | null;
  application_deadline: string | null;
  exam_date: string | null;
  admit_card_date: string | null;
  result_date: string | null;
  description: string | null;
  pdf_url: string | null;
  source_url: string;
  published_date: string | null;
  notification_status: string;
  content_hash: string;
  run_id: string | null;
  captured_at: string;
}

export interface FieldDiff {
  field: string;
  old_value: string | null;
  new_value: string | null;
}

export interface ChangeHistoryItem {
  id: number;
  run_id: string;
  source_id: number;
  source_code: string;
  source_name?: string;
  organization_name?: string;
  notification_id: number | null;
  entity_key: string;
  notification_title: string;
  change_type: 'NEW' | 'UPDATED' | 'REMOVED';
  changed_field: string | null;
  change_label: string | null;
  previous_value: string | null;
  new_value: string | null;
  changed_fields: FieldDiff[];
  before_snapshot: Record<string, string | null> | null;
  after_snapshot: Record<string, string | null> | null;
  source_url: string;
  is_verified: boolean;
  verified_by: string | null;
  detected_at: string;
}

export interface AlertItem {
  id: number;
  change_id: number | null;
  source_id: number;
  source_code: string;
  notification_id: number | null;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  alert_type: 'NEW' | 'UPDATED' | 'REMOVED' | 'SOURCE_ERROR';
  severity: 'info' | 'warning' | 'critical';
  is_read: boolean;
  is_verified: boolean;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface ScrapeRunItem {
  id?: number;
  run_id: string;
  source_id?: number;
  source_code: string;
  trigger_type?: string;
  mode_used: string;
  status: string;
  http_status?: number | null;
  parsed_count: number;
  new_count: number;
  updated_count: number;
  duplicate_count: number;
  removed_count?: number;
  error_count?: number;
  duration_ms?: number;
  error_message?: string | null;
  completed_at: string;
}

export interface DashboardPayload {
  stats: {
    active_notifications: number;
    monitored_sources: number;
    healthy_sources: number;
    unread_alerts: number;
    total_changes: number;
    saved_count: number;
  };
  upcoming_deadlines: RecruitmentNotification[];
  upcoming_exams?: RecruitmentNotification[];
  recent_changes: ChangeHistoryItem[];
  sources: MonitoringSource[];
  recent_runs: ScrapeRunItem[];
}

export interface SchedulerStatus {
  running: boolean;
  paused: boolean;
  interval_minutes: number;
  next_run_at: string | null;
}

===== FILE: src/pages/SavedPage.tsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { BookmarkX, ExternalLink, FileDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import type { RecruitmentNotification } from '../types';
import { formatDeadlineCountdown } from '../components/StatusBadge';

export function SavedPage() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<RecruitmentNotification[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [editingNotes, setEditingNotes] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [savingId, setSavingId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const loadSaved = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiRequest<{ items: RecruitmentNotification[] }>('/saved');
      setItems(res.items);
      const notesMap: Record<number, string> = {};
      for (const item of res.items) {
        notesMap[item.id] = item.saved_notes || '';
      }
      setEditingNotes(notesMap);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadSaved();
  }, [loadSaved]);

  const removeBookmark = async (id: number) => {
    await apiRequest(`/saved/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    setItems((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  };

  const saveNote = async (id: number) => {
    setSavingId(id);
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/saved/${id}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ notes: editingNotes[id] || '' }),
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingId(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Saved Notifications</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
          Your bookmarked government recruitment notices, deadline countdowns, and personal preparation notes.
        </p>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="h-40 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl animate-pulse" />
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-10 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">No saved notifications yet</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
            Bookmark recruitments from the Notifications directory to track their deadlines here.
          </p>
          <Link
            to="/notifications"
            className="mt-4 inline-block px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg"
          >
            Browse Notifications
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {items.map((item) => {
            const dl = formatDeadlineCountdown(item.application_deadline);
            return (
              <article key={item.id} className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5">
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-4">
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-blue-700 uppercase">{item.source_code}</span>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="font-mono">{item.notification_number || 'Ref Unavailable'}</span>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className={`font-mono tabular-nums ${dl.urgent ? 'text-amber-700 font-semibold' : ''}`}>
                        {dl.text}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="mt-1.5 text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">
                      <Link to={`/notifications/${item.id}`} className="hover:text-blue-700">
                        {item.title}
                      </Link>
                    </h3>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                    {item.pdf_url ? (
                      <a
                        href={item.pdf_url}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap"
                      >
                        <FileDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>Official PDF</span>
                      </a>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap">
                        PDF Unavailable
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <a
                      href={item.source_url}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 whitespace-nowrap"
                    >
                      <span>Source</span>
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </a>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => removeBookmark(item.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-red-700 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 rounded-lg cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
                    >
                      <BookmarkX className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>Remove</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={editingNotes[item.id] ?? ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditingNotes((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: e.target.value }))}
                    placeholder="Add personal note (e.g., fee paid, syllabus checklist, document verification date)..."
                    className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-600"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={savingId === item.id}
                    onClick={() => saveNote(item.id)}
                    className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    {savingId === item.id ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Note'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: src/pages/AdminPage.tsx =====

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Pause, Play, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { apiRequest } from '../api/client';
import type { SchedulerStatus, ScrapeRunItem, User } from '../types';
import { formatTimestamp } from '../components/StatusBadge';

export function AdminPage() {
  const [scheduler, setScheduler] = useState<SchedulerStatus | null>(null);
  const [runs, setRuns] = useState<ScrapeRunItem[]>([]);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState<User[]>([]);
  const [intervalInput, setIntervalInput] = useState(360);
  const [scrapingAll, setScrapingAll] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const [newCode, setNewCode] = useState('');
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState('');
  const [newOrg, setNewOrg] = useState('');
  const [newUrl, setNewUrl] = useState('');
  const [newParser, setNewParser] = useState('upsc');

  const loadAdminData = useCallback(async () => {
    setError('');
    try {
      const [schedRes, runsRes, usersRes] = await Promise.all([
        apiRequest<SchedulerStatus>('/admin/scheduler'),
        apiRequest<{ items: ScrapeRunItem[] }>('/admin/runs'),
        apiRequest<{ items: User[] }>('/admin/users'),
      ]);
      setScheduler(schedRes);
      setIntervalInput(schedRes.interval_minutes);
      setRuns(runsRes.items);
      setUsers(usersRes.items);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to load admin console');
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadAdminData();
  }, [loadAdminData]);

  const toggleSchedulerPause = async () => {
    if (!scheduler) return;
    const updated = await apiRequest<SchedulerStatus>('/admin/scheduler', {
      method: 'PUT',
      body: JSON.stringify({ paused: !scheduler.paused }),
    });
    setScheduler(updated);
  };

  const updateInterval = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const updated = await apiRequest<SchedulerStatus>('/admin/scheduler', {
      method: 'PUT',
      body: JSON.stringify({ interval_minutes: Number(intervalInput) }),
    });
    setScheduler(updated);
    setMessage(`Scheduler interval updated to ${updated.interval_minutes} minutes.`);
  };

  const handleScrapeAll = async () => {
    setScrapingAll(true);
    setMessage('');
    try {
      const res = await apiRequest<{ runs: { source_code: string; status: string }[] }>('/admin/scrape-all', {
        method: 'POST',
      });
      setMessage(`Completed batch sync across ${res.runs.length} active sources.`);
      await loadAdminData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Batch sync failed');
    } finally {
      setScrapingAll(false);
    }
  };

  const toggleUserRole = async (u: User) => {
    const nextRole = u.role === 'admin' ? 'user' : 'admin';
    const res = await apiRequest<{ user: User }>(`/admin/users/${u.id}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      body: JSON.stringify({ role: nextRole }),
    });
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((item) => (item.id === u.id ? res.user : item)));
  };

  const toggleUserActive = async (u: User) => {
    const res = await apiRequest<{ user: User }>(`/admin/users/${u.id}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      body: JSON.stringify({ is_active: !u.is_active }),
    });
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((item) => (item.id === u.id ? res.user : item)));
  };

  const handleAddSource = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setMessage('');
    setError('');
    try {
      await apiRequest('/sources', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          code: newCode,
          name: newName,
          organization_name: newOrg,
          base_url: newUrl,
          parser_key: newParser,
          fixture_filename: `${newParser}.html`,
          scrape_mode: 'fixture',
          crawl_interval_minutes: 360,
          rate_limit_seconds: 3,
          robots_policy: 'Public approved source; respectful interval.',
          permission_note: 'Added via Admin Console.',
        }),
      });
      setNewCode('');
      setNewName('');
      setNewOrg('');
      setNewUrl('');
      setMessage('Monitoring source added.');
      await loadAdminData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to add source');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Admin &amp; Scheduler Panel</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Configure periodic monitoring intervals, execute batch source checks, audit run logs, and manage user roles.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={scrapingAll}
          onClick={handleScrapeAll}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
        >
          <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${scrapingAll ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
          <span>{scrapingAll ? 'Running All Portals...' : 'Run All Active Sources'}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {message && (
        <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-800">{message}</div>
      )}
      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-red-700">{error}</div>
      )}

      {/* Scheduler Control + Add Source */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5">
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Periodic Scheduler</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
            Status: {scheduler?.paused ? 'Paused' : 'Active'} · Next scheduled run:{' '}
            <span className="font-mono">{formatTimestamp(scheduler?.next_run_at)}</span>
          </p>

          <form onSubmit={updateInterval} className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="schedInterval" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Interval (minutes)
              </label>
              <input
                id="schedInterval"
                type="number"
                min={5}
                max={1440}
                value={intervalInput}
                onChange={(e) => setIntervalInput(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-32 px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg tabular-nums"
              />
            </div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
            >
              Save Interval
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={toggleSchedulerPause}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
            >
              {scheduler?.paused ? <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              <span>{scheduler?.paused ? 'Resume Scheduler' : 'Pause Scheduler'}</span>
            </button>
          </form>
        </section>

        <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-5">
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display mb-3">Register Additional Source</h3>
          <form onSubmit={handleAddSource} className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={newCode}
              onChange={(e) => setNewCode(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Code (e.g. drdo)"
              className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
            />
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={newName}
              onChange={(e) => setNewName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Source Name"
              className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
            />
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={newOrg}
              onChange={(e) => setNewOrg(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Organization Name"
              className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
            />
            <input
              type="url"
              required
              value={newUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setNewUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://example.gov.in/"
              className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
            />
            <select
              aria-label="Parser key"
              value={newParser}
              onChange={(e) => setNewParser(e.target.value)}
              className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white"
            >
              <option value="upsc">Parser: upsc</option>
              <option value="ssc">Parser: ssc</option>
              <option value="tnpsc">Parser: tnpsc</option>
              <option value="ibps">Parser: ibps</option>
              <option value="nta">Parser: nta</option>
            </select>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
            >
              Add Source
            </button>
          </form>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* Scrape Run Logs */}
      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200">
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Recent Scrape Run Logs</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 font-semibold">
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Run ID</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Portal</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Trigger / Mode</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Status</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">Parsed</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">New</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">Updated</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">Dupes</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Completed</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 font-mono tabular-nums">
              {runs.map((r) => (
                <tr key={r.run_id} className="hover:bg-slate-50">
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-700">{r.run_id}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-sans font-semibold text-blue-700 uppercase">{r.source_code}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-sans text-slate-600">
                    {r.trigger_type} · {r.mode_used}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-sans">
                    <span className={r.status === 'success' ? 'text-emerald-700 font-medium' : 'text-red-700 font-medium'}>
                      {r.status} ({r.http_status ?? 'N/A'})
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">{r.parsed_count}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">{r.new_count}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">{r.updated_count}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">{r.duplicate_count}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-sans text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                    {formatTimestamp(r.completed_at)}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* User Management */}
      <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200">
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-display">Registered Users</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 font-semibold">
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Name</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Email</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Role</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Status</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {users.map((u) => (
                <tr key={u.id}>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-900">{u.full_name}</td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-slate-600">{u.email}</td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4 capitalize text-slate-700">{u.role}</td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4">
                    <span className={u.is_active ? 'text-emerald-700 font-medium' : 'text-red-700 font-medium'}>
                      {u.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Disabled'}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right space-x-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleUserRole(u)}
                      className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Make {u.role === 'admin' ? 'User' : 'Admin'}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleUserActive(u)}
                      className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-md cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      {u.is_active ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'}
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
