===== FILE: backend/app/auth.py =====

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Optional
import jwt
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer
from passlib.context import CryptContext
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .config import ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES, JWT_ALGORITHM, JWT_SECRET_KEY
from .database import get_db
from .models import User

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")
bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)


def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    return pwd_context.hash(password)


def verify_password(plain_password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:
    return pwd_context.verify(plain_password, password_hash)


def create_access_token(user: User) -> str:
    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
    payload = {
        "sub": str(user.id),
        "email": user.email,
        "role": user.role,
        "exp": expire,
    }
    return jwt.encode(payload, JWT_SECRET_KEY, algorithm=JWT_ALGORITHM)


def get_current_user(
    credentials: Optional[HTTPAuthorizationCredentials] = Depends(bearer_scheme),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> User:
    if not credentials:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Authentication required")
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(credentials.credentials, JWT_SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[JWT_ALGORITHM])
        user_id = int(payload["sub"])
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid or expired token")

    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()
    if not user or not user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="User inactive or not found")
    return user


def get_optional_user(
    credentials: Optional[HTTPAuthorizationCredentials] = Depends(bearer_scheme),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> Optional[User]:
    if not credentials:
        return None
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(credentials.credentials, JWT_SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[JWT_ALGORITHM])
        user_id = int(payload["sub"])
        user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()
        return user if user and user.is_active else None
    except Exception:
        return None


def require_admin(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)) -> User:
    if current_user.role != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Admin role required")
    return current_user


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: backend/app/change_detector.py =====

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from sqlalchemy import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .diff_core import (
    IMPORTANT_ALERT_FIELDS,
    TRACKED_FIELDS,
    build_entity_key_from_parts,
    classify_field_change,
    compute_content_hash,
    compute_field_diffs,
    merge_preserving_missing_fields,
    normalize_str,
    resolve_notification_status,
)
from .models import (
    Alert,
    ChangeHistory,
    MonitoringSource,
    NotificationVersion,
    RecruitmentNotification,
    ScrapeRun,
)
from .scrapers.base import ParsedNotification

HISTORY_WORTHY_FIELDS = {
    "exam_date",
    "application_deadline",
    "application_start_date",
    "admit_card_date",
    "result_date",
    "vacancy_count",
    "notification_status",
    "title",
    "notification_number",
    "published_date",
    "pdf_url",
    "source_url",
}


def build_entity_key(source_code: str, item: ParsedNotification) -> str:
    return build_entity_key_from_parts(source_code, item.notification_number, item.title)


def notification_snapshot(item: ParsedNotification | RecruitmentNotification) -> Dict[str, Optional[str]]:
    snap: Dict[str, Optional[str]] = {}
    for field in TRACKED_FIELDS:
        if field == "notification_status":
            raw_status = getattr(item, "notification_status", None) or getattr(item, "lifecycle_status", None)
            snap[field] = resolve_notification_status(
                raw_status,
                getattr(item, "title", None),
                getattr(item, "description", None),
            )
        else:
            val = getattr(item, field, None)
            snap[field] = normalize_str(val)
    return snap


def _save_version_snapshot(
    db: Session,
    notif_id: int,
    version_number: int,
    snap: Dict[str, Optional[str]],
    content_hash: str,
    run_id: Optional[str],
    captured_at: datetime,
    fallback_org: str,
    fallback_url: str,
) -> NotificationVersion:
    ver = NotificationVersion(
        notification_id=notif_id,
        version_number=version_number,
        title=snap.get("title") or "Untitled Notification",
        organization_name=snap.get("organization_name") or fallback_org,
        notification_number=snap.get("notification_number"),
        vacancy_count=snap.get("vacancy_count"),
        job_category=snap.get("job_category"),
        application_start_date=snap.get("application_start_date"),
        application_deadline=snap.get("application_deadline"),
        exam_date=snap.get("exam_date"),
        admit_card_date=snap.get("admit_card_date"),
        result_date=snap.get("result_date"),
        description=snap.get("description"),
        pdf_url=snap.get("pdf_url"),
        source_url=snap.get("source_url") or fallback_url,
        published_date=snap.get("published_date"),
        notification_status=snap.get("notification_status") or "active",
        content_hash=content_hash,
        snapshot_json=json.dumps(snap),
        run_id=run_id,
        captured_at=captured_at,
    )
    db.add(ver)
    return ver


def apply_scrape_results(
    db: Session,
    source: MonitoringSource,
    parsed_items: List[ParsedNotification],
    run_id: str,
    is_demo_data: bool,
    allow_removal_check: bool = True,
    removal_threshold: int = 2,
    suppress_history: bool = False,
) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """
    Safely apply parsed notifications to SQLite with duplicate suppression,
    versioned snapshots, separate field-level ChangeHistory rows per changed field,
    and safe missing-field / removal protection.
    """
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    counts = {
        "parsed_count": len(parsed_items),
        "new_count": 0,
        "updated_count": 0,
        "duplicate_count": 0,
        "removed_count": 0,
    }

    if len(parsed_items) == 0:
        return counts

    seen_keys = set()
    existing_rows = (
        db.query(RecruitmentNotification)
        .filter(RecruitmentNotification.source_id == source.id)
        .all()
    )
    existing_by_key = {row.entity_key: row for row in existing_rows}

    for item in parsed_items:
        entity_key = build_entity_key(source.code, item)
        if entity_key in seen_keys:
            counts["duplicate_count"] += 1
            continue
        seen_keys.add(entity_key)

        raw_snap = notification_snapshot(item)
        existing = existing_by_key.get(entity_key)

        if existing is None:
            new_hash = compute_content_hash(raw_snap)
            notif = RecruitmentNotification(
                source_id=source.id,
                entity_key=entity_key,
                content_hash=new_hash,
                title=raw_snap["title"] or "Untitled Notification",
                organization_name=raw_snap["organization_name"] or source.organization_name,
                notification_number=raw_snap["notification_number"],
                vacancy_count=raw_snap["vacancy_count"],
                job_category=raw_snap["job_category"],
                application_start_date=raw_snap["application_start_date"],
                application_deadline=raw_snap["application_deadline"],
                exam_date=raw_snap["exam_date"],
                admit_card_date=raw_snap["admit_card_date"],
                result_date=raw_snap["result_date"],
                description=raw_snap["description"],
                pdf_url=raw_snap["pdf_url"],
                source_url=raw_snap["source_url"] or source.base_url,
                published_date=raw_snap["published_date"],
                lifecycle_status=raw_snap["notification_status"] or "active",
                is_removed=False,
                missing_count=0,
                is_demo_data=is_demo_data,
                first_seen_at=now,
                last_seen_at=now,
                source_updated_at=now,
                last_run_id=run_id,
            )
            db.add(notif)
            db.flush()

            _save_version_snapshot(
                db=db,
                notif_id=notif.id,
                version_number=1,
                snap=raw_snap,
                content_hash=new_hash,
                run_id=run_id,
                captured_at=now,
                fallback_org=source.organization_name,
                fallback_url=source.base_url,
            )

            if not suppress_history:
                new_summary_diffs = [{"field": "title", "old_value": None, "new_value": notif.title}]
                for extra_field in ("published_date", "application_start_date", "application_deadline", "exam_date", "result_date"):
                    if raw_snap.get(extra_field):
                        new_summary_diffs.append({"field": extra_field, "old_value": None, "new_value": raw_snap[extra_field]})

                change = ChangeHistory(
                    run_id=run_id,
                    source_id=source.id,
                    notification_id=notif.id,
                    entity_key=entity_key,
                    notification_title=notif.title,
                    change_type="NEW",
                    changed_field="title",
                    change_label="NEW NOTIFICATION PUBLISHED",
                    previous_value=None,
                    new_value=notif.title,
                    changed_fields_json=json.dumps(new_summary_diffs),
                    before_snapshot_json=None,
                    after_snapshot_json=json.dumps(raw_snap),
                    source_url=notif.source_url,
                    is_verified=False,
                    detected_at=now,
                )
                db.add(change)
                db.flush()

                db.add(
                    Alert(
                        change_id=change.id,
                        source_id=source.id,
                        notification_id=notif.id,
                        title=f"New Recruitment: {notif.title}",
                        message=f"{source.code.upper()} published a new notification ({notif.notification_number or 'Unavailable'}).",
                        alert_type="NEW",
                        severity="info",
                    )
                )
            counts["new_count"] += 1
        else:
            existing.last_seen_at = now
            existing.last_run_id = run_id
            existing.missing_count = 0
            existing.is_demo_data = is_demo_data

            was_removed = existing.is_removed
            if was_removed:
                existing.is_removed = False
                existing.lifecycle_status = "active"

            old_snap = notification_snapshot(existing)
            new_snap = merge_preserving_missing_fields(old_snap, raw_snap)
            new_hash = compute_content_hash(new_snap)

            if existing.content_hash == new_hash and not was_removed:
                counts["duplicate_count"] += 1
                continue

            diffs = compute_field_diffs(old_snap, new_snap)
            if not diffs and not was_removed:
                counts["duplicate_count"] += 1
                continue

            if was_removed and not diffs:
                diffs = [{"field": "notification_status", "old_value": "removed", "new_value": "active"}]

            # Preserve baseline version 1 if missing before recording new version
            max_ver = (
                db.query(func.max(NotificationVersion.version_number))
                .filter(NotificationVersion.notification_id == existing.id)
                .scalar()
            ) or 0
            if max_ver == 0:
                _save_version_snapshot(
                    db=db,
                    notif_id=existing.id,
                    version_number=1,
                    snap=old_snap,
                    content_hash=existing.content_hash,
                    run_id=existing.last_run_id,
                    captured_at=existing.first_seen_at or now,
                    fallback_org=existing.organization_name,
                    fallback_url=existing.source_url,
                )
                max_ver = 1

            for field in TRACKED_FIELDS:
                if field == "notification_status":
                    continue
                if field in ("title", "organization_name", "source_url") and not new_snap[field]:
                    continue
                setattr(existing, field, new_snap[field])

            existing.content_hash = new_hash
            existing.lifecycle_status = "cancelled" if new_snap.get("notification_status") == "cancelled" else "updated"
            existing.source_updated_at = now

            _save_version_snapshot(
                db=db,
                notif_id=existing.id,
                version_number=max_ver + 1,
                snap=new_snap,
                content_hash=new_hash,
                run_id=run_id,
                captured_at=now,
                fallback_org=existing.organization_name,
                fallback_url=existing.source_url,
            )

            # Separate change-history record for EACH high-signal changed field + targeted alert
            history_diffs = [d for d in diffs if (d["field"] or "") in HISTORY_WORTHY_FIELDS]
            for diff in history_diffs:
                field_name = diff["field"] or "details"
                old_val = diff["old_value"]
                new_val = diff["new_value"]
                label = classify_field_change(field_name, old_val, new_val)

                change = ChangeHistory(
                    run_id=run_id,
                    source_id=source.id,
                    notification_id=existing.id,
                    entity_key=entity_key,
                    notification_title=existing.title,
                    change_type="UPDATED",
                    changed_field=field_name,
                    change_label=label,
                    previous_value=old_val,
                    new_value=new_val,
                    changed_fields_json=json.dumps([diff]),
                    before_snapshot_json=json.dumps(old_snap),
                    after_snapshot_json=json.dumps(new_snap),
                    source_url=existing.source_url,
                    is_verified=False,
                    detected_at=now,
                )
                db.add(change)
                db.flush()

                if field_name in IMPORTANT_ALERT_FIELDS:
                    severity = (
                        "critical"
                        if new_val == "cancelled"
                        else (
                            "warning"
                            if field_name in ("exam_date", "application_deadline", "admit_card_date", "result_date", "vacancy_count", "notification_status")
                            else "info"
                        )
                    )
                    db.add(
                        Alert(
                            change_id=change.id,
                            source_id=source.id,
                            notification_id=existing.id,
                            title=f"{label}: {existing.title}",
                            message=(
                                f"{source.code.upper()} — Previous: {old_val or 'Unavailable'} → New: {new_val or 'Unavailable'}"
                            ),
                            alert_type="UPDATED",
                            severity=severity,
                        )
                    )

            if history_diffs:
                counts["updated_count"] += 1
            else:
                counts["duplicate_count"] += 1

    if allow_removal_check:
        for existing in existing_rows:
            if existing.entity_key not in seen_keys and not existing.is_removed:
                existing.missing_count += 1
                if existing.missing_count >= removal_threshold:
                    old_snap = notification_snapshot(existing)
                    existing.is_removed = True
                    existing.lifecycle_status = "removed"
                    existing.source_updated_at = now
                    removed_snap = dict(old_snap)
                    removed_snap["notification_status"] = "removed"
                    max_ver = (
                        db.query(func.max(NotificationVersion.version_number))
                        .filter(NotificationVersion.notification_id == existing.id)
                        .scalar()
                    ) or 0
                    _save_version_snapshot(
                        db=db,
                        notif_id=existing.id,
                        version_number=max_ver + 1,
                        snap=removed_snap,
                        content_hash=compute_content_hash(removed_snap),
                        run_id=run_id,
                        captured_at=now,
                        fallback_org=existing.organization_name,
                        fallback_url=existing.source_url,
                    )
                    change = ChangeHistory(
                        run_id=run_id,
                        source_id=source.id,
                        notification_id=existing.id,
                        entity_key=existing.entity_key,
                        notification_title=existing.title,
                        change_type="REMOVED",
                        changed_field="notification_status",
                        change_label="NOTIFICATION REMOVED FROM PORTAL",
                        previous_value="active",
                        new_value="removed",
                        changed_fields_json=json.dumps(
                            [{"field": "notification_status", "old_value": "active", "new_value": "removed"}]
                        ),
                        before_snapshot_json=json.dumps(old_snap),
                        after_snapshot_json=None,
                        source_url=existing.source_url,
                        is_verified=False,
                        detected_at=now,
                    )
                    db.add(change)
                    db.flush()
                    db.add(
                        Alert(
                            change_id=change.id,
                            source_id=source.id,
                            notification_id=existing.id,
                            title=f"Removed from Portal: {existing.title}",
                            message=f"{source.code.upper()} listing absent across {removal_threshold} consecutive healthy checks.",
                            alert_type="REMOVED",
                            severity="warning",
                        )
                    )
                    counts["removed_count"] += 1

    return counts


def record_failed_scrape(
    db: Session,
    source: MonitoringSource,
    run_id: str,
    trigger_type: str,
    mode_used: str,
    http_status: Optional[int],
    error_message: str,
    duration_ms: int,
    started_at: datetime,
) -> ScrapeRun:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    is_rate_limited = http_status in (403, 429)
    source.last_attempted_at = now
    source.last_http_status = http_status
    source.last_error = error_message
    source.freshness_status = "Rate Limited" if is_rate_limited else "Error"

    run = ScrapeRun(
        run_id=run_id,
        source_id=source.id,
        trigger_type=trigger_type,
        mode_used=mode_used,
        status="rate_limited" if is_rate_limited else "error",
        http_status=http_status,
        parsed_count=0,
        new_count=0,
        updated_count=0,
        duplicate_count=0,
        removed_count=0,
        error_count=1,
        duration_ms=duration_ms,
        error_message=error_message,
        started_at=started_at,
        completed_at=now,
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.add(
        Alert(
            source_id=source.id,
            title=f"Source Check Failed: {source.code.upper()}",
            message=f"{error_message} Existing records preserved safely (stale-data protection).",
            alert_type="SOURCE_ERROR",
            severity="critical" if is_rate_limited else "warning",
        )
    )
    return run

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base, sessionmaker
from .config import DATABASE_URL

connect_args = {"check_same_thread": False} if DATABASE_URL.startswith("sqlite") else {}

engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL, connect_args=connect_args, future=True)

if DATABASE_URL.startswith("sqlite"):
    @event.listens_for(engine, "connect")
    def _set_sqlite_pragma(dbapi_connection, _connection_record):
        cursor = dbapi_connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;")
        cursor.close()

SessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=engine, future=True)
Base = declarative_base()


def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()

===== FILE: backend/app/diff_core.py =====

import hashlib
import json
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

TRACKED_FIELDS = [
    "title",
    "organization_name",
    "notification_number",
    "vacancy_count",
    "job_category",
    "application_start_date",
    "application_deadline",
    "exam_date",
    "admit_card_date",
    "result_date",
    "description",
    "pdf_url",
    "source_url",
    "published_date",
    "notification_status",
]

PRESERVABLE_FIELDS = (
    "notification_number",
    "vacancy_count",
    "job_category",
    "application_start_date",
    "application_deadline",
    "exam_date",
    "admit_card_date",
    "result_date",
    "description",
    "pdf_url",
    "published_date",
)

IMPORTANT_ALERT_FIELDS = {
    "exam_date",
    "application_deadline",
    "application_start_date",
    "admit_card_date",
    "result_date",
    "vacancy_count",
    "notification_status",
    "title",
    "pdf_url",
    "source_url",
}


def normalize_str(value: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    if value is None:
        return None
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", str(value)).strip()
    return cleaned if cleaned else None


def resolve_notification_status(raw_status: Optional[str], title: Optional[str], description: Optional[str]) -> str:
    status_norm = (normalize_str(raw_status) or "").lower()
    if status_norm in ("cancelled", "canceled", "withdrawn", "removed"):
        return "cancelled" if status_norm in ("cancelled", "canceled", "withdrawn") else "removed"
    combined = f"{title or ''} {description or ''}"
    if re.search(r"\b(?:cancelled|canceled|withdrawn|annulled)\b", combined, re.I):
        return "cancelled"
    return "active"


def build_entity_key_from_parts(
    source_code: str,
    notification_number: Optional[str],
    title: Optional[str],
) -> str:
    notif_no = normalize_str(notification_number)
    if notif_no:
        slug = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", notif_no.lower()).strip("-")
        return f"{source_code}::{slug}"
    title_norm = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", (normalize_str(title) or "untitled").lower()).strip("-")
    return f"{source_code}::{title_norm[:120]}"


def normalize_snapshot(raw: Dict[str, Optional[str]]) -> Dict[str, Optional[str]]:
    snap: Dict[str, Optional[str]] = {}
    for field in TRACKED_FIELDS:
        if field == "notification_status":
            snap[field] = resolve_notification_status(
                raw.get("notification_status") or raw.get("lifecycle_status"),
                raw.get("title"),
                raw.get("description"),
            )
        else:
            snap[field] = normalize_str(raw.get(field))
    return snap


def merge_preserving_missing_fields(
    old_snap: Dict[str, Optional[str]],
    new_snap: Dict[str, Optional[str]],
) -> Dict[str, Optional[str]]:
    """Never interpret a partial scrape missing optional dates/counts as a deletion."""
    merged = dict(new_snap)
    if merged.get("notification_status") == "cancelled":
        return merged
    for field in PRESERVABLE_FIELDS:
        if merged.get(field) is None and old_snap.get(field) is not None:
            merged[field] = old_snap[field]
    return merged


def compute_content_hash(snapshot: Dict[str, Optional[str]]) -> str:
    canonical = json.dumps(snapshot, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def compute_field_diffs(
    old_snap: Dict[str, Optional[str]],
    new_snap: Dict[str, Optional[str]],
) -> List[Dict[str, Optional[str]]]:
    diffs: List[Dict[str, Optional[str]]] = []
    for field in TRACKED_FIELDS:
        old_val = old_snap.get(field)
        new_val = new_snap.get(field)
        if old_val != new_val:
            diffs.append({"field": field, "old_value": old_val, "new_value": new_val})
    return diffs


def classify_field_change(field: str, old_val: Optional[str], new_val: Optional[str]) -> str:
    if field == "exam_date":
        return "EXAM DATE CHANGED"
    if field == "application_deadline":
        if old_val and new_val and re.match(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$", old_val) and re.match(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$", new_val):
            if new_val > old_val:
                return "APPLICATION DEADLINE EXTENDED"
        return "APPLICATION DEADLINE CHANGED"
    if field == "application_start_date":
        return "APPLICATION START DATE CHANGED"
    if field == "admit_card_date":
        return "ADMIT CARD DATE UPDATED"
    if field == "result_date":
        return "RESULT DATE UPDATED"
    if field == "vacancy_count":
        return "VACANCY COUNT UPDATED"
    if field in ("notification_status", "lifecycle_status"):
        if new_val == "cancelled":
            return "NOTIFICATION CANCELLED"
        if new_val == "removed":
            return "NOTIFICATION REMOVED FROM PORTAL"
        return "NOTIFICATION STATUS CHANGED"
    if field == "pdf_url":
        return "OFFICIAL PDF UPDATED"
    if field == "source_url":
        return "OFFICIAL NOTIFICATION URL UPDATED"
    if field == "title":
        return "NOTIFICATION TITLE UPDATED"
    if field == "published_date":
        return "PUBLISHED DATE UPDATED"
    if field == "notification_number":
        return "NOTIFICATION NUMBER UPDATED"
    return "NOTIFICATION DETAILS UPDATED"


===== END FILE =====

import json
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Query, Response, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from sqlalchemy import desc, or_
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .auth import (
    create_access_token,
    get_current_user,
    get_optional_user,
    hash_password,
    require_admin,
    verify_password,
)
from .database import Base, SessionLocal, engine, get_db
from .models import (
    Alert,
    ChangeHistory,
    MonitoringSource,
    NotificationVersion,
    RecruitmentNotification,
    SavedNotification,
    ScrapeRun,
    User,
)
from .scheduler import get_scheduler_status, start_scheduler, stop_scheduler, update_scheduler_config
from .schemas import (
    AdminUserUpdateRequest,
    LoginRequest,
    PasswordChangeRequest,
    ProfileUpdateRequest,
    RegisterRequest,
    SaveNoteRequest,
    SchedulerConfigRequest,
    ScrapeTriggerRequest,
    SourceCreateRequest,
    SourceUpdateRequest,
)
from .scraper_engine import run_source_scrape
from .seed import seed_database
from .pdf_generator import build_notification_pdf


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(_app: FastAPI):
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        seed_database(db)
    finally:
        db.close()
    start_scheduler()
    yield
    stop_scheduler()


app = FastAPI(
    title="GovTrack API",
    description="Intelligent Government Recruitment and Examination Notification Change Monitoring System",
    version="1.0.0",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)


def serialize_user(user: User) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": user.id,
        "full_name": user.full_name,
        "email": user.email,
        "role": user.role,
        "target_exams": user.target_exams,
        "email_alerts_enabled": user.email_alerts_enabled,
        "is_active": user.is_active,
        "created_at": user.created_at.isoformat() if user.created_at else None,
    }


def serialize_source(src: MonitoringSource) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": src.id,
        "code": src.code,
        "name": src.name,
        "organization_name": src.organization_name,
        "base_url": src.base_url,
        "parser_key": src.parser_key,
        "fixture_filename": src.fixture_filename,
        "scrape_mode": src.scrape_mode,
        "crawl_interval_minutes": src.crawl_interval_minutes,
        "rate_limit_seconds": src.rate_limit_seconds,
        "robots_policy": src.robots_policy,
        "permission_note": src.permission_note,
        "is_active": src.is_active,
        "is_demo_data": src.is_demo_data,
        "freshness_status": src.freshness_status,
        "last_attempted_at": src.last_attempted_at.isoformat() if src.last_attempted_at else None,
        "last_successful_at": src.last_successful_at.isoformat() if src.last_successful_at else None,
        "last_http_status": src.last_http_status,
        "last_error": src.last_error,
        "last_parsed_count": src.last_parsed_count,
        "last_duplicate_count": src.last_duplicate_count,
    }


def serialize_notification(n: RecruitmentNotification, saved_ids: set[int] | None = None, source_code: str = "") -> dict:
    return {
        "id": n.id,
        "source_id": n.source_id,
        "source_code": source_code or (n.source.code if n.source else ""),
        "entity_key": n.entity_key,
        "content_hash": n.content_hash,
        "title": n.title,
        "organization_name": n.organization_name,
        "notification_number": n.notification_number,
        "vacancy_count": n.vacancy_count,
        "job_category": n.job_category,
        "application_start_date": n.application_start_date,
        "application_deadline": n.application_deadline,
        "exam_date": n.exam_date,
        "admit_card_date": n.admit_card_date,
        "result_date": n.result_date,
        "description": n.description,
        "pdf_url": n.pdf_url,
        "source_url": n.source_url,
        "published_date": n.published_date,
        "lifecycle_status": n.lifecycle_status,
        "is_removed": n.is_removed,
        "is_demo_data": n.is_demo_data,
        "first_seen_at": n.first_seen_at.isoformat() if n.first_seen_at else None,
        "last_seen_at": n.last_seen_at.isoformat() if n.last_seen_at else None,
        "source_updated_at": n.source_updated_at.isoformat() if n.source_updated_at else None,
        "last_run_id": n.last_run_id,
        "is_saved": n.id in saved_ids if saved_ids is not None else False,
    }


def serialize_version(v: NotificationVersion) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": v.id,
        "notification_id": v.notification_id,
        "version_number": v.version_number,
        "title": v.title,
        "organization_name": v.organization_name,
        "notification_number": v.notification_number,
        "vacancy_count": v.vacancy_count,
        "job_category": v.job_category,
        "application_start_date": v.application_start_date,
        "application_deadline": v.application_deadline,
        "exam_date": v.exam_date,
        "admit_card_date": v.admit_card_date,
        "result_date": v.result_date,
        "description": v.description,
        "pdf_url": v.pdf_url,
        "source_url": v.source_url,
        "published_date": v.published_date,
        "notification_status": v.notification_status,
        "content_hash": v.content_hash,
        "run_id": v.run_id,
        "captured_at": v.captured_at.isoformat() if v.captured_at else None,
    }


def serialize_change(c: ChangeHistory, source_obj: MonitoringSource | None = None, source_code: str = "") -> dict:
    changed_fields = json.loads(c.changed_fields_json or "[]")
    first_diff = changed_fields[0] if changed_fields else {}
    return {
        "id": c.id,
        "run_id": c.run_id,
        "source_id": c.source_id,
        "source_code": source_obj.code if source_obj else source_code,
        "source_name": source_obj.name if source_obj else "",
        "organization_name": source_obj.organization_name if source_obj else "",
        "notification_id": c.notification_id,
        "entity_key": c.entity_key,
        "notification_title": c.notification_title,
        "change_type": c.change_type,
        "changed_field": c.changed_field or first_diff.get("field"),
        "change_label": c.change_label,
        "previous_value": c.previous_value if c.previous_value is not None else first_diff.get("old_value"),
        "new_value": c.new_value if c.new_value is not None else first_diff.get("new_value"),
        "changed_fields": changed_fields,
        "before_snapshot": json.loads(c.before_snapshot_json) if c.before_snapshot_json else None,
        "after_snapshot": json.loads(c.after_snapshot_json) if c.after_snapshot_json else None,
        "source_url": c.source_url,
        "is_verified": c.is_verified,
        "verified_by": c.verified_by,
        "detected_at": c.detected_at.isoformat() if c.detected_at else None,
    }


@app.get("/api/health")
def health_check():
    return {"status": "ok", "service": "GovTrack FastAPI"}


@app.post("/api/auth/register", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def register(payload: RegisterRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    if db.query(User).filter(User.email == payload.email.lower()).first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Email is already registered")
    user = User(
        full_name=payload.full_name.strip(),
        email=payload.email.lower().strip(),
        password_hash=hash_password(payload.password),
        role="user",
        target_exams=payload.target_exams,
    )
    db.add(user)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)
    token = create_access_token(user)
    return {"access_token": token, "token_type": "bearer", "user": serialize_user(user)}


@app.post("/api/auth/login")
def login(payload: LoginRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == payload.email.lower().strip()).first()
    if not user or not verify_password(payload.password, user.password_hash):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid email or password")
    if not user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Account is deactivated")
    token = create_access_token(user)
    return {"access_token": token, "token_type": "bearer", "user": serialize_user(user)}


@app.get("/api/auth/me")
def me(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return {"user": serialize_user(current_user)}


@app.put("/api/profile")
def update_profile(
    payload: ProfileUpdateRequest,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    current_user.full_name = payload.full_name.strip()
    current_user.target_exams = payload.target_exams
    current_user.email_alerts_enabled = payload.email_alerts_enabled
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(current_user)
    return {"user": serialize_user(current_user)}


@app.put("/api/profile/password")
def change_password(
    payload: PasswordChangeRequest,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    if not verify_password(payload.current_password, current_user.password_hash):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Current password is incorrect")
    current_user.password_hash = hash_password(payload.new_password)
    db.commit()
    return {"message": "Password updated successfully"}


@app.get("/api/dashboard")
def get_dashboard(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    sources = db.query(MonitoringSource).order_by(MonitoringSource.id.asc()).all()
    source_map = {s.id: s.code for s in sources}

    total_notifications = db.query(RecruitmentNotification).filter(RecruitmentNotification.is_removed == False).count()  # noqa: E712
    unread_alerts = db.query(Alert).filter(Alert.is_read == False).count()  # noqa: E712
    total_changes = db.query(ChangeHistory).filter(ChangeHistory.change_type.in_(["UPDATED", "REMOVED"])).count()
    healthy_sources = sum(1 for s in sources if s.freshness_status == "Fresh")

    saved_ids = {
        s.notification_id
        for s in db.query(SavedNotification).filter(SavedNotification.user_id == current_user.id).all()
    }

    today_str = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    upcoming = (
        db.query(RecruitmentNotification)
        .filter(
            RecruitmentNotification.is_removed == False,  # noqa: E712
            RecruitmentNotification.application_deadline != None,  # noqa: E711
            RecruitmentNotification.application_deadline >= today_str,
        )
        .order_by(RecruitmentNotification.application_deadline.asc())
        .limit(6)
        .all()
    )

    upcoming_exams = (
        db.query(RecruitmentNotification)
        .filter(
            RecruitmentNotification.is_removed == False,  # noqa: E712
            RecruitmentNotification.exam_date != None,  # noqa: E711
            RecruitmentNotification.exam_date >= today_str,
        )
        .order_by(RecruitmentNotification.exam_date.asc())
        .limit(6)
        .all()
    )

    recent_changes = (
        db.query(ChangeHistory)
        .filter(ChangeHistory.change_type.in_(["UPDATED", "REMOVED"]))
        .order_by(desc(ChangeHistory.detected_at), desc(ChangeHistory.id))
        .limit(8)
        .all()
    )
    recent_runs = db.query(ScrapeRun).order_by(desc(ScrapeRun.completed_at)).limit(6).all()

    source_obj_map = {s.id: s for s in sources}
    return {
        "stats": {
            "active_notifications": total_notifications,
            "monitored_sources": len(sources),
            "healthy_sources": healthy_sources,
            "unread_alerts": unread_alerts,
            "total_changes": total_changes,
            "saved_count": len(saved_ids),
        },
        "upcoming_deadlines": [serialize_notification(n, saved_ids, source_map.get(n.source_id, "")) for n in upcoming],
        "upcoming_exams": [serialize_notification(n, saved_ids, source_map.get(n.source_id, "")) for n in upcoming_exams],
        "recent_changes": [serialize_change(c, source_obj_map.get(c.source_id)) for c in recent_changes],
        "sources": [serialize_source(s) for s in sources],
        "recent_runs": [
            {
                "run_id": r.run_id,
                "source_code": source_map.get(r.source_id, ""),
                "status": r.status,
                "mode_used": r.mode_used,
                "parsed_count": r.parsed_count,
                "new_count": r.new_count,
                "updated_count": r.updated_count,
                "duplicate_count": r.duplicate_count,
                "completed_at": r.completed_at.isoformat() if r.completed_at else None,
            }
            for r in recent_runs
        ],
    }


@app.get("/api/notifications")
def list_notifications(
    q: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    source_code: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    category: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    status_filter: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    sort: str = Query("deadline"),
    current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_optional_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    sources = db.query(MonitoringSource).all()
    source_map = {s.id: s.code for s in sources}
    code_to_id = {s.code: s.id for s in sources}

    query = db.query(RecruitmentNotification)
    if q:
        like = f"%{q.strip()}%"
        query = query.filter(
            or_(
                RecruitmentNotification.title.ilike(like),
                RecruitmentNotification.organization_name.ilike(like),
                RecruitmentNotification.notification_number.ilike(like),
                RecruitmentNotification.description.ilike(like),
            )
        )
    if source_code and source_code != "all" and source_code in code_to_id:
        query = query.filter(RecruitmentNotification.source_id == code_to_id[source_code])
    if category and category != "all":
        query = query.filter(RecruitmentNotification.job_category == category)
    if status_filter and status_filter != "all":
        query = query.filter(RecruitmentNotification.lifecycle_status == status_filter)

    if sort == "newest":
        query = query.order_by(desc(RecruitmentNotification.first_seen_at))
    else:
        query = query.order_by(RecruitmentNotification.application_deadline.asc().nullslast())

    items = query.all()
    saved_ids = set()
    if current_user:
        saved_ids = {
            s.notification_id
            for s in db.query(SavedNotification).filter(SavedNotification.user_id == current_user.id).all()
        }

    categories = sorted(
        {n.job_category for n in db.query(RecruitmentNotification).all() if n.job_category}
    )
    return {
        "items": [serialize_notification(n, saved_ids, source_map.get(n.source_id, "")) for n in items],
        "categories": categories,
    }


@app.get("/api/notifications/{notification_id}")
def get_notification_detail(
    notification_id: int,
    current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_optional_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    notif = db.query(RecruitmentNotification).filter(RecruitmentNotification.id == notification_id).first()
    if not notif:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Notification not found")
    source = db.query(MonitoringSource).filter(MonitoringSource.id == notif.source_id).first()
    changes = (
        db.query(ChangeHistory)
        .filter(ChangeHistory.notification_id == notif.id)
        .order_by(desc(ChangeHistory.detected_at))
        .all()
    )
    versions = (
        db.query(NotificationVersion)
        .filter(NotificationVersion.notification_id == notif.id)
        .order_by(desc(NotificationVersion.version_number))
        .all()
    )
    saved_ids = set()
    if current_user:
        saved = (
            db.query(SavedNotification)
            .filter(SavedNotification.user_id == current_user.id, SavedNotification.notification_id == notif.id)
            .first()
        )
        if saved:
            saved_ids.add(notif.id)

    return {
        "notification": serialize_notification(notif, saved_ids, source.code if source else ""),
        "source": serialize_source(source) if source else None,
        "changes": [serialize_change(c, source) for c in changes],
        "versions": [serialize_version(v) for v in versions],
    }


@app.get("/api/notifications/{notification_id}/pdf")
def download_notification_summary_pdf(notification_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    notif = db.query(RecruitmentNotification).filter(RecruitmentNotification.id == notification_id).first()
    if not notif:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Notification not found")
    pdf_bytes = build_notification_pdf(serialize_notification(notif))
    return Response(
        content=pdf_bytes,
        media_type="application/pdf",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": f'inline; filename="govtrack-notification-{notification_id}.pdf"'},
    )


@app.get("/api/changes")
def list_changes(
    change_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    source_code: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    changed_field: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    q: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    sources = db.query(MonitoringSource).all()
    source_obj_map = {s.id: s for s in sources}
    code_to_id = {s.code: s.id for s in sources}

    query = db.query(ChangeHistory)
    if change_type == "UPDATES_ONLY":
        query = query.filter(ChangeHistory.change_type.in_(["UPDATED", "REMOVED"]))
    elif change_type and change_type != "all":
        query = query.filter(ChangeHistory.change_type == change_type.upper())
    if source_code and source_code != "all" and source_code in code_to_id:
        query = query.filter(ChangeHistory.source_id == code_to_id[source_code])
    if changed_field and changed_field != "all":
        query = query.filter(ChangeHistory.changed_field == changed_field)
    if q and q.strip():
        like = f"%{q.strip()}%"
        query = query.filter(
            or_(
                ChangeHistory.notification_title.ilike(like),
                ChangeHistory.change_label.ilike(like),
                ChangeHistory.previous_value.ilike(like),
                ChangeHistory.new_value.ilike(like),
                ChangeHistory.entity_key.ilike(like),
            )
        )

    changes = query.order_by(desc(ChangeHistory.detected_at), desc(ChangeHistory.id)).limit(250).all()
    return {"items": [serialize_change(c, source_obj_map.get(c.source_id)) for c in changes]}


@app.post("/api/changes/{change_id}/verify")
def verify_change(
    change_id: int,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    change = db.query(ChangeHistory).filter(ChangeHistory.id == change_id).first()
    if not change:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Change record not found")
    change.is_verified = True
    change.verified_by = current_user.full_name
    linked_alert = db.query(Alert).filter(Alert.change_id == change.id).first()
    if linked_alert:
        linked_alert.is_verified = True
        linked_alert.is_read = True
    db.commit()
    return {"message": "Change verified", "verified_by": current_user.full_name}


@app.get("/api/sources")
def list_sources(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    sources = db.query(MonitoringSource).order_by(MonitoringSource.id.asc()).all()
    return {"items": [serialize_source(s) for s in sources]}


@app.post("/api/sources/scrape-all-live")
def scrape_all_sources_live(
    _current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    sources = db.query(MonitoringSource).filter(MonitoringSource.is_active == True).order_by(MonitoringSource.id.asc()).all()  # noqa: E712
    runs = []
    for src in sources:
        run = run_source_scrape(db, src, trigger_type="manual", mode_override="auto")
        runs.append({
            "source_code": src.code,
            "run_id": run.run_id,
            "status": run.status,
            "mode_used": run.mode_used,
            "parsed_count": run.parsed_count,
            "new_count": run.new_count,
            "updated_count": run.updated_count,
            "duplicate_count": run.duplicate_count,
        })
    return {"runs": runs}


@app.post("/api/sources", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def create_source(
    payload: SourceCreateRequest,
    _admin: User = Depends(require_admin),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    if db.query(MonitoringSource).filter(MonitoringSource.code == payload.code.lower()).first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Source code already exists")
    src = MonitoringSource(
        code=payload.code.lower().strip(),
        name=payload.name.strip(),
        organization_name=payload.organization_name.strip(),
        base_url=payload.base_url.strip(),
        parser_key=payload.parser_key.strip(),
        fixture_filename=payload.fixture_filename.strip(),
        scrape_mode=payload.scrape_mode,
        crawl_interval_minutes=payload.crawl_interval_minutes,
        rate_limit_seconds=payload.rate_limit_seconds,
        robots_policy=payload.robots_policy,
        permission_note=payload.permission_note,
    )
    db.add(src)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(src)
    return {"source": serialize_source(src)}


@app.patch("/api/sources/{source_id}")
def update_source(
    source_id: int,
    payload: SourceUpdateRequest,
    _admin: User = Depends(require_admin),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    src = db.query(MonitoringSource).filter(MonitoringSource.id == source_id).first()
    if not src:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Source not found")
    if payload.scrape_mode is not None:
        src.scrape_mode = payload.scrape_mode
    if payload.crawl_interval_minutes is not None:
        src.crawl_interval_minutes = payload.crawl_interval_minutes
    if payload.is_active is not None:
        src.is_active = payload.is_active
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(src)
    return {"source": serialize_source(src)}


@app.post("/api/sources/{source_id}/scrape")
def trigger_source_scrape(
    source_id: int,
    payload: ScrapeTriggerRequest = ScrapeTriggerRequest(),
    _current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    src = db.query(MonitoringSource).filter(MonitoringSource.id == source_id).first()
    if not src:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Source not found")

    trigger_type = "fixture_replay" if payload.fixture_override else "manual"
    run = run_source_scrape(
        db=db,
        source=src,
        trigger_type=trigger_type,
        mode_override=payload.mode_override,
        fixture_override=payload.fixture_override,
        simulate_http_status=payload.simulate_http_status,
    )
    db.refresh(src)
    return {
        "source": serialize_source(src),
        "run": {
            "run_id": run.run_id,
            "status": run.status,
            "mode_used": run.mode_used,
            "http_status": run.http_status,
            "parsed_count": run.parsed_count,
            "new_count": run.new_count,
            "updated_count": run.updated_count,
            "duplicate_count": run.duplicate_count,
            "removed_count": run.removed_count,
            "error_message": run.error_message,
            "duration_ms": run.duration_ms,
        },
    }


@app.get("/api/alerts")
def list_alerts(
    unread_only: bool = Query(False),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    sources = {s.id: s.code for s in db.query(MonitoringSource).all()}
    query = db.query(Alert)
    if unread_only:
        query = query.filter(Alert.is_read == False)  # noqa: E712
    alerts = query.order_by(desc(Alert.created_at)).limit(150).all()
    return {
        "items": [
            {
                "id": a.id,
                "change_id": a.change_id,
                "source_id": a.source_id,
                "source_code": sources.get(a.source_id, ""),
                "notification_id": a.notification_id,
                "title": a.title,
                "message": a.message,
                "alert_type": a.alert_type,
                "severity": a.severity,
                "is_read": a.is_read,
                "is_verified": a.is_verified,
                "created_at": a.created_at.isoformat() if a.created_at else None,
            }
            for a in alerts
        ]
    }

@app.get("/api/saved")
def list_saved_notifications(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    sources = {s.id: s.code for s in db.query(MonitoringSource).all()}
    saved = (
        db.query(SavedNotification)
        .filter(SavedNotification.user_id == current_user.id)
        .order_by(desc(SavedNotification.saved_at))
        .all()
    )
    items = []
    for entry in saved:
        notif = db.query(RecruitmentNotification).filter(RecruitmentNotification.id == entry.notification_id).first()
        if notif:
            data = serialize_notification(notif, {notif.id}, sources.get(notif.source_id, ""))
            data["saved_notes"] = entry.notes
            data["saved_at"] = entry.saved_at.isoformat() if entry.saved_at else None
            items.append(data)
    return {"items": items}


@app.post("/api/saved/{notification_id}")
def save_notification(
    notification_id: int,
    payload: SaveNoteRequest = SaveNoteRequest(),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    notif = db.query(RecruitmentNotification).filter(RecruitmentNotification.id == notification_id).first()
    if not notif:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Notification not found")
    existing = (
        db.query(SavedNotification)
        .filter(SavedNotification.user_id == current_user.id, SavedNotification.notification_id == notification_id)
        .first()
    )
    if existing:
        existing.notes = payload.notes
    else:
        db.add(SavedNotification(user_id=current_user.id, notification_id=notification_id, notes=payload.notes))
    db.commit()
    return {"saved": True}


@app.delete("/api/saved/{notification_id}")
def remove_saved_notification(
    notification_id: int,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    db.query(SavedNotification).filter(
        SavedNotification.user_id == current_user.id,
        SavedNotification.notification_id == notification_id,
    ).delete()
    db.commit()
    return {"saved": False}

        ]
    }

@app.patch("/api/admin/users/{user_id}")
def admin_update_user(
    user_id: int,
    payload: AdminUserUpdateRequest,
    _admin: User = Depends(require_admin),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
    if payload.role in ("user", "admin"):
        user.role = payload.role
    if payload.is_active is not None:
        user.is_active = payload.is_active
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)
    return {"user": serialize_user(user)}


@app.get("/api/admin/scheduler")
def admin_scheduler_status(_admin: User = Depends(require_admin)):
    return get_scheduler_status()


@app.put("/api/admin/scheduler")
def admin_scheduler_update(
    payload: SchedulerConfigRequest,
    _admin: User = Depends(require_admin),
):
    return update_scheduler_config(paused=payload.paused, interval_minutes=payload.interval_minutes)


@app.post("/api/admin/scrape-all")
def admin_scrape_all(
    _admin: User = Depends(require_admin),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    sources = db.query(MonitoringSource).filter(MonitoringSource.is_active == True).all()  # noqa: E712
    results = []
    for src in sources:
        run = run_source_scrape(db, src, trigger_type="manual")
        results.append({"source_code": src.code, "run_id": run.run_id, "status": run.status})
    return {"runs": results}

===== FILE: backend/app/scheduler.py =====

from apscheduler.schedulers.background import BackgroundScheduler
from .config import SCHEDULER_INTERVAL_MINUTES
from .database import SessionLocal
from .models import MonitoringSource
from .scraper_engine import run_source_scrape

scheduler = BackgroundScheduler(timezone="UTC")
_scheduler_state = {
    "paused": False,
    "interval_minutes": SCHEDULER_INTERVAL_MINUTES,
}


def scheduled_scrape_all_job():
    if _scheduler_state["paused"]:
        return
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        sources = db.query(MonitoringSource).filter(MonitoringSource.is_active == True).all()  # noqa: E712
        for source in sources:
            run_source_scrape(db, source, trigger_type="scheduled")
    finally:
        db.close()


def start_scheduler():
    if not scheduler.running:
        scheduler.add_job(
            scheduled_scrape_all_job,
            "interval",
            minutes=_scheduler_state["interval_minutes"],
            id="govtrack_periodic_monitor",
            replace_existing=True,
        )
        scheduler.start()


def stop_scheduler():
    if scheduler.running:
        scheduler.shutdown(wait=False)


def get_scheduler_status() -> dict:
    job = scheduler.get_job("govtrack_periodic_monitor") if scheduler.running else None
    return {
        "running": scheduler.running,
        "paused": _scheduler_state["paused"],
        "interval_minutes": _scheduler_state["interval_minutes"],
        "next_run_at": job.next_run_time.isoformat() if job and job.next_run_time else None,
    }


def update_scheduler_config(paused: bool | None = None, interval_minutes: int | None = None) -> dict:
    if paused is not None:
        _scheduler_state["paused"] = paused
    if interval_minutes is not None and interval_minutes >= 5:
        _scheduler_state["interval_minutes"] = interval_minutes
        if scheduler.running:
            scheduler.reschedule_job(
                "govtrack_periodic_monitor",
                trigger="interval",
                minutes=interval_minutes,
            )
    return get_scheduler_status()

===== FILE: backend/app/scraper_engine.py =====

import re
import time
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional
from urllib.parse import quote, urlparse
from urllib.robotparser import RobotFileParser

import requests
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .change_detector import apply_scrape_results, record_failed_scrape
from .config import FIXTURES_DIR, SCRAPER_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, SCRAPER_USER_AGENT
from .models import MonitoringSource, ScrapeRun
from .scrapers import get_parser
from .scrapers.base import ParsedNotification

CAPTCHA_MARKERS = ("captcha", "cf-challenge", "g-recaptcha", "hcaptcha", "access denied")


def check_robots_allowed(base_url: str, user_agent: str) -> bool:
    """Checks robots.txt politely; defaults to True if robots.txt is unreachable."""
    try:
        parsed = urlparse(base_url)
        robots_url = f"{parsed.scheme}://{parsed.netloc}/robots.txt"
        rp = RobotFileParser()
        resp = requests.get(robots_url, headers={"User-Agent": user_agent}, timeout=4)
        if resp.status_code == 200:
            rp.parse(resp.text.splitlines())
            return rp.can_fetch(user_agent, base_url)
    except Exception:
        return True
    return True


def enrich_live_items(parser_key: str, base_url: str, items):
    if not items:
        return items
    headers = {"User-Agent": SCRAPER_USER_AGENT, "Accept": "text/html,application/xhtml+xml"}
    if parser_key == "upsc":
        from .scrapers.upsc import parse_upsc_detail_html
        for idx, item in enumerate(items[:10]):
            if item.source_url and item.source_url != base_url and "upsc.gov.in/active-examinations/" in item.source_url:
                try:
                    resp = requests.get(item.source_url, headers=headers, timeout=9)
                    if resp.status_code == 200:
                        items[idx] = parse_upsc_detail_html(item, resp.text)
                except requests.RequestException:
                    pass
    elif parser_key == "rbi":
        from .scrapers.rbi import parse_rbi_detail_html
        for idx, item in enumerate(items[:6]):
            if item.source_url and "bs_viewcontent.aspx" in item.source_url:
                try:
                    resp = requests.get(item.source_url, headers=headers, timeout=9)
                    if resp.status_code == 200:
                        items[idx] = parse_rbi_detail_html(item, resp.text, item.source_url)
                except requests.RequestException:
                    pass
    elif parser_key == "ibps":
        from .scrapers.ibps import extract_ibps_subpage_dates
        sub_urls = list(
            dict.fromkeys(
                it.source_url
                for it in items
                if it.source_url and it.source_url != base_url and "ibps.in/index.php/" in it.source_url and not it.source_url.lower().endswith(".pdf")
            )
        )[:5]
        for sub_url in sub_urls:
            try:
                resp = requests.get(sub_url, headers=headers, timeout=9)
                if resp.status_code == 200:
                    extract_ibps_subpage_dates(items, resp.text, sub_url)
            except requests.RequestException:
                pass

    from .pdf_extractor import enrich_notification_from_pdf
    for idx in range(min(8, len(items))):
        items[idx] = enrich_notification_from_pdf(items[idx])

    return items


def _fetch_ssc_live_items() -> List[ParsedNotification]:
    headers = {
        "User-Agent": SCRAPER_USER_AGENT,
        "Accept": "application/json",
        "Referer": "https://ssc.gov.in/",
    }
    notice_url = (
        "https://ssc.gov.in/api/general-website/portal/notice-boards"
        "?page=1&limit=10&contentType=notice-boards&key=createdAt&order=DESC"
        "&isPaginationRequired=true&isAttachment=true&language=english"
        "&attributes=id,headline,examId,contentType,redirectUrl,createdAt"
    )
    calendar_url = (
        "https://ssc.gov.in/api/general-website/portal/ssc-calendar"
        "?page=1&limit=8&contentType=ssc-calendar&key=createdAt&order=DESC"
        "&isPaginationRequired=false&isAttachment=true&language=english"
        "&attributes=id,headline,examId,examYear,desc,content,contentType,startDate,endDate,language,createdAt"
    )

    def safe_get_json(url: str) -> list:
        try:
            resp = requests.get(url, headers=headers, timeout=14)
            if resp.status_code == 200:
                data = resp.json().get("data")
                return data if isinstance(data, list) else []
        except Exception:
            return []
        return []

    notices = safe_get_json(notice_url)
    calendar = safe_get_json(calendar_url)
    results: List[ParsedNotification] = []
    seen = set()

    for row in notices:
        title = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", str(row.get("headline") or "")).strip()
        if not title or title.lower() in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(title.lower())
        attachments = row.get("attachments") or []
        att = next((a for a in attachments if a.get("path") and str(a.get("path")).lower().endswith(".pdf")), None) or (
            attachments[0] if attachments else None
        )
        clean_path = str(att["path"]).replace("\\", "/").lstrip("/") if att and att.get("path") else None
        pdf_url = f"https://ssc.gov.in/api/attachment/{quote(clean_path, safe='/')}" if clean_path else None
        pub_date = str(row.get("createdAt"))[:10] if row.get("createdAt") else None
        start_date = str(row.get("startDate"))[:10] if row.get("startDate") else None
        deadline = str(row.get("endDate"))[:10] if row.get("endDate") else None
        is_admit = bool(re.search(r"admit|admission\s*certificate|city\s*of\s*examination", title, re.I))
        is_result = bool(re.search(r"result|answer\s*key|marks|cut-?off", title, re.I))

        results.append(
            ParsedNotification(
                title=title,
                organization_name="Staff Selection Commission (SSC)",
                notification_number=f"SSC-{row['examId']}" if row.get("examId") else None,
                job_category="Admit Card / City Intimation" if is_admit else ("Result / Answer Key" if is_result else "SSC Notice Board"),
                application_start_date=start_date,
                application_deadline=deadline,
                admit_card_date=pub_date if is_admit else None,
                result_date=pub_date if is_result else None,
                description=f"Official SSC Notice Board update released on {pub_date}." if pub_date else None,
                pdf_url=pdf_url,
                source_url="https://ssc.gov.in/",
                published_date=pub_date,
            )
        )

    for row in calendar:
        title = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", str(row.get("headline") or "")).strip()
        if not title or title.lower() in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(title.lower())
        start_date = str(row.get("startDate"))[:10] if row.get("startDate") else None
        deadline = str(row.get("endDate"))[:10] if row.get("endDate") else None
        pub_date = str(row.get("createdAt"))[:10] if row.get("createdAt") else None
        content_val = str(row.get("content") or "").strip() or None
        desc_parts = [p for p in (row.get("desc"), f"Exam Month: {content_val}" if content_val else None) if p]
        results.append(
            ParsedNotification(
                title=title,
                organization_name="Staff Selection Commission (SSC)",
                notification_number=f"SSC-CAL-{row['examId']}" if row.get("examId") else None,
                job_category="SSC Examination Calendar",
                application_start_date=start_date,
                application_deadline=deadline,
                exam_date=content_val,
                description=" · ".join(desc_parts) if desc_parts else None,
                pdf_url=None,
                source_url="https://ssc.gov.in/",
                published_date=pub_date,
            )
        )

    return results[:14]


def run_source_scrape(
    db: Session,
    source: MonitoringSource,
    trigger_type: str = "manual",
    mode_override: Optional[str] = None,
    fixture_override: Optional[str] = None,
    simulate_http_status: Optional[int] = None,
    suppress_history: bool = False,
) -> ScrapeRun:
    started_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    start_perf = time.perf_counter()
    run_id = f"RUN-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10].upper()}"
    effective_mode = mode_override or source.scrape_mode

    # Simulated HTTP failure path for CP-5 mentor live testing
    if simulate_http_status and simulate_http_status >= 400:
        duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_perf) * 1000)
        run = record_failed_scrape(
            db=db,
            source=source,
            run_id=run_id,
            trigger_type=trigger_type,
            mode_used="simulated_error",
            http_status=simulate_http_status,
            error_message=f"Simulated HTTP {simulate_http_status} error from source endpoint.",
            duration_ms=duration_ms,
            started_at=started_at,
        )
        db.commit()
        return run

    html_content: Optional[str] = None
    http_status: int = 200
    is_demo_data = True
    mode_used = "fixture"

    if fixture_override:
        fixture_path = FIXTURES_DIR / Path(fixture_override).name
        if not fixture_path.exists():
            duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_perf) * 1000)
            run = record_failed_scrape(
                db, source, run_id, trigger_type, "fixture", 404, f"Fixture not found: {fixture_override}", duration_ms, started_at
            )
            db.commit()
            return run
        if "404" in fixture_path.name:
            duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_perf) * 1000)
            run = record_failed_scrape(
                db, source, run_id, trigger_type, "fixture", 404, f"HTTP 404 Not Found replayed from {fixture_path.name}", duration_ms, started_at
            )
            db.commit()
            return run
        html_content = fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        mode_used = "fixture"
        is_demo_data = True
    elif effective_mode in ("live", "auto"):
        # Polite live request with robots.txt check and backoff
        if not check_robots_allowed(source.base_url, SCRAPER_USER_AGENT):
            duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_perf) * 1000)
            run = record_failed_scrape(
                db, source, run_id, trigger_type, "live", 403, "Disallowed by robots.txt policy.", duration_ms, started_at
            )
            db.commit()
            return run

        live_error: Optional[str] = None
        for attempt in range(2):
            try:
                resp = requests.get(
                    source.base_url,
                    headers={"User-Agent": SCRAPER_USER_AGENT, "Accept": "text/html,application/xhtml+xml"},
                    timeout=SCRAPER_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
                )
                http_status = resp.status_code
                if http_status in (403, 429):
                    live_error = f"HTTP {http_status} received; stopped immediately per respectful crawling policy."
                    break
                if http_status >= 500:
                    live_error = f"HTTP {http_status} transient server error."
                    time.sleep(0.5 * (attempt + 1))
                    continue
                if http_status == 200:
                    lower_text = resp.text[:4000].lower()
                    if any(marker in lower_text for marker in CAPTCHA_MARKERS):
                        live_error = "Anti-bot / CAPTCHA challenge detected; never bypassing."
                        http_status = 403
                        break
                    html_content = resp.text
                    mode_used = "live"
                    is_demo_data = False
                    break
                live_error = f"Unexpected HTTP {http_status}."
                break
            except requests.RequestException as exc:
                live_error = f"Network error: {str(exc)[:180]}"
                time.sleep(0.3 * (attempt + 1))

        if html_content is None:
            if effective_mode == "live":
                duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_perf) * 1000)
                run = record_failed_scrape(
                    db, source, run_id, trigger_type, "live", http_status or 503, live_error or "Live fetch failed", duration_ms, started_at
                )
                db.commit()
                return run
            # Auto mode falls back cleanly to verified offline fixture
            fixture_path = FIXTURES_DIR / source.fixture_filename
            if fixture_path.exists():
                html_content = fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                mode_used = "fixture"
                is_demo_data = True
                http_status = 200
            else:
                duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_perf) * 1000)
                run = record_failed_scrape(
                    db, source, run_id, trigger_type, "auto", http_status or 503, live_error or "Fetch and fallback failed", duration_ms, started_at
                )
                db.commit()
                return run
    else:
        fixture_path = FIXTURES_DIR / source.fixture_filename
        if not fixture_path.exists():
            duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_perf) * 1000)
            run = record_failed_scrape(
                db, source, run_id, trigger_type, "fixture", 404, f"Missing fixture {source.fixture_filename}", duration_ms, started_at
            )
            db.commit()
            return run
        html_content = fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        mode_used = "fixture"
        is_demo_data = True

    parser = get_parser(source.parser_key)
    if mode_used == "live" and source.parser_key == "ssc":
        parsed_items = _fetch_ssc_live_items()
        if not parsed_items:
            parsed_items = parser.parse(html_content, source_url=source.base_url)
    else:
        parsed_items = parser.parse(html_content, source_url=source.base_url)

    if mode_used == "live" and parsed_items:
        parsed_items = enrich_live_items(source.parser_key, source.base_url, parsed_items)

    # If live page returned 0 items due to JS-rendered SPA shell in auto mode, safely fallback to fixture
    if len(parsed_items) == 0 and mode_used == "live" and effective_mode == "auto":
        fixture_path = FIXTURES_DIR / source.fixture_filename
        if fixture_path.exists():
            html_content = fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            parsed_items = parser.parse(html_content, source_url=source.base_url)
            mode_used = "fixture"
            is_demo_data = True

    duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start_perf) * 1000)
    if len(parsed_items) == 0:
        run = record_failed_scrape(
            db,
            source,
            run_id,
            trigger_type,
            mode_used,
            http_status,
            "Parser returned 0 records (selector/schema mismatch guard). Existing data preserved.",
            duration_ms,
            started_at,
        )
        db.commit()
        return run

    counts = apply_scrape_results(
        db=db,
        source=source,
        parsed_items=parsed_items,
        run_id=run_id,
        is_demo_data=is_demo_data,
        allow_removal_check=True,
        suppress_history=suppress_history,
    )

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    source.last_attempted_at = now
    source.last_successful_at = now
    source.last_http_status = http_status
    source.last_error = None
    source.is_demo_data = is_demo_data
    source.freshness_status = "Fresh"
    source.last_parsed_count = counts["parsed_count"]
    source.last_duplicate_count = counts["duplicate_count"]

    run = ScrapeRun(
        run_id=run_id,
        source_id=source.id,
        trigger_type=trigger_type,
        mode_used=mode_used,
        status="success",
        http_status=http_status,
        parsed_count=counts["parsed_count"],
        new_count=counts["new_count"],
        updated_count=counts["updated_count"],
        duplicate_count=counts["duplicate_count"],
        removed_count=counts["removed_count"],
        error_count=0,
        duration_ms=duration_ms,
        error_message=None,
        started_at=started_at,
        completed_at=now,
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.commit()
    return run

from typing import Dict
from .base import BasePortalParser
from .upsc import UpscParser
from .ssc import SscParser
from .tnpsc import TnpscParser
from .tnusrb import TnusrbParser
from .rrbcdg import RrbcdgParser
from .ibps import IbpsParser
from .sbi import SbiParser
from .rbi import RbiParser
from .nta import NtaParser
from .indiapost import IndiaPostParser

PARSER_REGISTRY: Dict[str, BasePortalParser] = {
    "upsc": UpscParser(),
    "ssc": SscParser(),
    "tnpsc": TnpscParser(),
    "tnusrb": TnusrbParser(),
    "rrbcdg": RrbcdgParser(),
    "ibps": IbpsParser(),
    "sbi": SbiParser(),
    "rbi": RbiParser(),
    "nta": NtaParser(),
    "indiapost": IndiaPostParser(),
}


def get_parser(parser_key: str) -> BasePortalParser:
    if parser_key not in PARSER_REGISTRY:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported parser_key: {parser_key}")
    return PARSER_REGISTRY[parser_key]
