===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/base.py =====

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from typing import List, Optional
from urllib.parse import urljoin
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

MONTHS = {
    "jan": "01", "january": "01",
    "feb": "02", "february": "02",
    "mar": "03", "march": "03",
    "apr": "04", "april": "04",
    "may": "05",
    "jun": "06", "june": "06",
    "jul": "07", "july": "07",
    "aug": "08", "august": "08",
    "sep": "09", "sept": "09", "september": "09",
    "oct": "10", "october": "10",
    "nov": "11", "november": "11",
    "dec": "12", "december": "12",
}


def clean_text(value: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    if value is None:
        return None
    normalized = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", value).strip()
    return normalized if normalized else None


def resolve_url(base_url: str, href: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    cleaned = clean_text(href)
    if not cleaned:
        return None
    return urljoin(base_url, cleaned)


def is_public_http_url(url: Optional[str]) -> bool:
    if not url:
        return False
    return bool(re.match(r"^https?://(?!10\.|127\.|192\.168\.|172\.(?:1[6-9]|2\d|3[01])\.)", url, re.I))


def normalize_human_date(raw: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    cleaned = clean_text(raw)
    if not cleaned or re.match(r"^(-+|—+|n/?a|nil|tba|not\s+applicable)$", cleaned, re.I):
        return None
    if re.match(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$", cleaned):
        return cleaned

    compact = re.search(r"(?:^|_)(\d{4})(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])(?:\d{6})?(?:\.pdf|$)", cleaned, re.I)
    if compact:
        return f"{compact.group(1)}-{compact.group(2)}-{compact.group(3)}"

    dmy = re.search(r"(\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{4})", cleaned)
    if dmy:
        day, mon = int(dmy.group(1)), int(dmy.group(2))
        if 1 <= day <= 31 and 1 <= mon <= 12:
            return f"{dmy.group(3)}-{str(mon).zfill(2)}-{str(day).zfill(2)}"

    d_mon_y = re.search(r"(\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+([A-Za-z]{3,9}),?\s+(\d{4})", cleaned)
    if d_mon_y:
        mon = MONTHS.get(d_mon_y.group(2).lower()) or MONTHS.get(d_mon_y.group(2)[:3].lower())
        if mon:
            return f"{d_mon_y.group(3)}-{mon}-{d_mon_y.group(1).zfill(2)}"

    mon_d_y = re.search(r"\b([A-Za-z]{3,9})\s+(\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?,\s*(\d{4})\b", cleaned)
    if mon_d_y:
        mon = MONTHS.get(mon_d_y.group(1).lower()) or MONTHS.get(mon_d_y.group(1)[:3].lower())
        if mon:
            return f"{mon_d_y.group(3)}-{mon}-{mon_d_y.group(2).zfill(2)}"

    return None


@dataclass
class ParsedNotification:
    title: str
    organization_name: str
    notification_number: Optional[str] = None
    vacancy_count: Optional[str] = None
    job_category: Optional[str] = None
    application_start_date: Optional[str] = None
    application_deadline: Optional[str] = None
    exam_date: Optional[str] = None
    admit_card_date: Optional[str] = None
    result_date: Optional[str] = None
    description: Optional[str] = None
    pdf_url: Optional[str] = None
    source_url: str = ""
    published_date: Optional[str] = None
    notification_status: Optional[str] = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)


class BasePortalParser:
    parser_key: str = "base"
    organization_name: str = ""
    base_url: str = ""

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        raise NotImplementedError("Subclasses must implement parse()")

    def soup(self, html: str) -> BeautifulSoup:
        return BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/ibps.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, is_public_http_url, normalize_human_date, resolve_url


def extract_ibps_subpage_dates(items: List[ParsedNotification], html: str, page_url: str) -> None:
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
    entries = []
    for a in soup.select("a"):
        href = resolve_url(page_url, a.get("href"))
        text = clean_text(a.get_text())
        if not href or not text or len(text) < 15:
            continue
        if not href.lower().endswith(".pdf") and not re.search(r"notification|result|allotment|call\s*letter|score", text, re.I):
            continue
        parent = a.find_parent(["li", "tr", "p", "div"])
        parent_text = clean_text(parent.get_text()) if parent else text
        dm = re.search(r"(\d{1,2}\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9}\s+\d{4}|\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})", parent_text or "")
        entries.append({
            "date": normalize_human_date(dm.group(1)) if dm else None,
            "title": text,
            "pdf_url": href if href.lower().endswith(".pdf") and is_public_http_url(href) else None,
        })
    if not entries:
        return
    for item in items:
        tl = item.title.lower()
        match = (
            next((e for e in entries if e["title"].lower() == tl), None)
            or next(
                (
                    e
                    for e in entries
                    if (item.notification_number and item.notification_number.lower() in e["title"].lower())
                    or ("notification" in e["title"].lower() and item.source_url == page_url)
                ),
                None,
            )
            or next((e for e in entries if item.source_url == page_url), None)
        )
        if match:
            if not item.published_date and match["date"]:
                item.published_date = match["date"]
            if not item.pdf_url and match["pdf_url"]:
                item.pdf_url = match["pdf_url"]
            if re.search(r"result|provisional\s+allotment|score", item.title, re.I) and match["date"]:
                item.result_date = match["date"]
            if re.search(r"call\s*letter|admit\s*card", item.title, re.I) and match["date"]:
                item.admit_card_date = match["date"]


class IbpsParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "ibps"
    organization_name = "Institute of Banking Personnel Selection (IBPS)"
    base_url = "https://www.ibps.in/"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        items = soup.select(".crp-notification-list .crp-item")
        if items:
            for item in items:
                title_el = item.select_one("h3 a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                kv = {dd.get("data-key"): clean_text(dd.get_text()) for dd in item.select("dl.crp-dates dd[data-key]")}
                pdf_el = item.select_one("a.crp-pdf")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(item.select_one(".crp-ref").get_text() if item.select_one(".crp-ref") else None),
                        vacancy_count=kv.get("vacancies"),
                        job_category=clean_text(item.get("data-stream")) or "Public Sector Banking",
                        application_start_date=kv.get("start"),
                        application_deadline=kv.get("deadline"),
                        exam_date=kv.get("exam"),
                        admit_card_date=kv.get("admit"),
                        result_date=kv.get("result"),
                        description=clean_text(item.select_one("p.crp-desc").get_text() if item.select_one("p.crp-desc") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href")) or effective_base,
                        published_date=kv.get("published"),
                    )
                )
            return results

        seen = set()
        for a in soup.select("a"):
            title = clean_text(a.get_text())
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, a.get("href"))
            if not title or not href or len(title) < 18:
                continue
            if re.match(r"^CRP\s*[-\s]?(?:RRBs|CSA.*|PO/MTs|Clerks|Specialist Officers)$", title, re.I):
                continue
            if not re.search(r"CRP|Notification for Common Recruitment|Provisional Allotment|Result", title, re.I):
                continue
            if title.lower() in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(title.lower())
            crp_m = re.search(r"CRP[-\sA-Z/]+(?:XVII|XVI|XV|XIV)", title, re.I)
            is_pdf = ".pdf" in href.lower()
            file_date_m = re.search(r"(\d{2}\.\d{2}\.\d{4})\.pdf$", href, re.I) if is_pdf else None
            parent = a.find_parent(["li", "p", "div"])
            parent_text = clean_text(parent.get_text()) if parent else ""
            parent_date_m = re.search(r"(\d{1,2}\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9}\s+\d{4})", parent_text or "")
            pub_date = (
                normalize_human_date(file_date_m.group(1))
                if file_date_m
                else (normalize_human_date(parent_date_m.group(1)) if parent_date_m else None)
            )
            results.append(
                ParsedNotification(
                    title=title,
                    organization_name=self.organization_name,
                    notification_number=crp_m.group(0) if crp_m else None,
                    job_category="Public Sector Banking",
                    result_date=pub_date if pub_date and re.search(r"provisional allotment|result", title, re.I) else None,
                    pdf_url=href if is_pdf and is_public_http_url(href) else None,
                    source_url=effective_base if is_pdf else href,
                    published_date=pub_date,
                )
            )
        return results[:12]

===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/indiapost.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from .base import (
    BasePortalParser,
    ParsedNotification,
    clean_text,
    is_public_http_url,
    normalize_human_date,
    resolve_url,
)


class IndiaPostParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "indiapost"
    organization_name = "India Post — Department of Posts"
    base_url = "https://www.indiapost.gov.in/vacancies"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        entries = soup.select(".gds-engagement-list .indiapost-entry")
        if entries:
            for entry in entries:
                title_el = entry.select_one("h4.ip-title a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                pdf_el = entry.select_one("a.ip-pdf")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(entry.select_one(".ip-notif-no").get_text() if entry.select_one(".ip-notif-no") else None),
                        vacancy_count=clean_text(entry.select_one(".ip-vac").get_text() if entry.select_one(".ip-vac") else None),
                        job_category=clean_text(entry.get("data-category")) or "Postal Services",
                        application_start_date=clean_text(entry.select_one(".ip-start").get_text() if entry.select_one(".ip-start") else None),
                        application_deadline=clean_text(entry.select_one(".ip-deadline").get_text() if entry.select_one(".ip-deadline") else None),
                        exam_date=clean_text(entry.select_one(".ip-exam").get_text() if entry.select_one(".ip-exam") else None),
                        admit_card_date=clean_text(entry.select_one(".ip-admit").get_text() if entry.select_one(".ip-admit") else None),
                        result_date=clean_text(entry.select_one(".ip-result").get_text() if entry.select_one(".ip-result") else None),
                        description=clean_text(entry.select_one("p.ip-desc").get_text() if entry.select_one("p.ip-desc") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href")) or effective_base,
                        published_date=clean_text(entry.select_one(".ip-pub").get_text() if entry.select_one(".ip-pub") else None),
                    )
                )
            return results

        unescaped = html.replace('\\"', '"')
        seen = set()
        english_first: List[ParsedNotification] = []
        other_langs: List[ParsedNotification] = []
        rsc_pattern = re.compile(
            r'"title"\s*:\s*"([^"]{15,300})"\s*,\s*"category"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"\s*,\s*"date"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"[^}]{0,220}?"version"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"[^}]{0,120}?"url"\s*:\s*"(/api/documents/file/[^"]+)"'
        )
        for m in rsc_pattern.finditer(unescaped):
            title = clean_text(m.group(1))
            raw_cat = clean_text(m.group(2)) or "Postal Services"
            pub_date = normalize_human_date(m.group(3))
            version = clean_text(m.group(4)) or ""
            pdf_url = resolve_url(effective_base, m.group(5))
            if not title or not pdf_url or not is_public_http_url(pdf_url):
                continue
            if re.match(r"^hindi version$", version, re.I) and any(r.title == title for r in english_first):
                continue
            dedupe_key = title.lower()
            if dedupe_key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(dedupe_key)

            vac_m = re.search(r"\b(\d{1,4})\s+(?:vacant\s+)?posts?\b", title, re.I)
            notif_m = re.search(r"\bNo\.?\s*([A-Z0-9/-]+(?:-\d{4})?)\b", title, re.I)
            parsed = ParsedNotification(
                title=title,
                organization_name=self.organization_name,
                notification_number=notif_m.group(1) if notif_m else None,
                vacancy_count=vac_m.group(1) if vac_m else None,
                job_category="Postal Recruitment" if re.search(r"recruit", raw_cat, re.I) else raw_cat,
                description=f"India Post {raw_cat} ({version})" if version else f"India Post {raw_cat}",
                pdf_url=pdf_url,
                source_url=effective_base,
                published_date=pub_date,
            )
            if re.search(r"english", version, re.I):
                english_first.append(parsed)
            else:
                other_langs.append(parsed)

        rsc_combined = english_first + other_langs
        if rsc_combined:
            return rsc_combined[:12]

        for a in soup.select("a"):
            title = clean_text(a.get_text())
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, a.get("href"))
            if (
                title
                and href
                and len(title) > 22
                and not re.match(r"^(Vacancies|Recruitment Rules|Recruitments)$", title, re.I)
                and not re.search(r"recruitment\s*rules", title, re.I)
                and re.search(r"gds|gramin dak|recruitment|engagement|vacancy", title + href, re.I)
            ):
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        job_category="Postal Services",
                        description="Live India Post recruitment link.",
                        pdf_url=href if ".pdf" in href.lower() and is_public_http_url(href) else None,
                        source_url=href,
                    )
                )
        return results[:12]


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/nta.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, is_public_http_url, normalize_human_date, resolve_url


class NtaParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "nta"
    organization_name = "National Testing Agency (NTA)"
    base_url = "https://nta.ac.in/"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        rows = soup.select("section.public-notices-grid .nta-notice-row")
        if rows:
            for row in rows:
                title_el = row.select_one(".nta-title a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                meta = {span.get("data-m"): clean_text(span.get_text()) for span in row.select(".nta-meta span[data-m]")}
                pdf_el = row.select_one("a.nta-pdf")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(row.select_one(".nta-ref").get_text() if row.select_one(".nta-ref") else None),
                        vacancy_count=meta.get("vacancies"),
                        job_category=clean_text(row.get("data-category")) or "National Examination",
                        application_start_date=meta.get("start"),
                        application_deadline=meta.get("deadline"),
                        exam_date=meta.get("exam"),
                        admit_card_date=meta.get("admit"),
                        result_date=meta.get("result"),
                        description=clean_text(row.select_one("p.nta-desc").get_text() if row.select_one("p.nta-desc") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href")) or effective_base,
                        published_date=meta.get("published"),
                    )
                )
            return results

        seen = set()
        dated_results: List[ParsedNotification] = []
        general_results: List[ParsedNotification] = []
        for a in soup.select('a[href*="/Download/Notice/"]'):
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, a.get("href"))
            if not href or not is_public_http_url(href):
                continue
            parent = a.find_parent(["p", "li", "div"])
            content_el = parent.select_one("content") if parent else None
            title = clean_text(content_el.get_text()) if content_el else None
            if not title and parent:
                link_txt = clean_text(a.get_text()) or ""
                parent_txt = clean_text(parent.get_text()) or ""
                if link_txt:
                    parent_txt = clean_text(parent_txt.replace(link_txt, "")) or ""
                title = parent_txt if len(parent_txt) > 15 else None
            if not title or len(title) < 12 or title in seen:
                continue
            if re.match(r"^procedure for duplicate score card$", title, re.I):
                continue
            seen.add(title)

            pub_date = normalize_human_date(href)
            inline_date = normalize_human_date(title)
            is_admit = bool(re.search(r"admit\s*card|city\s*intimation", title, re.I))
            is_result = bool(re.search(r"result|score\s*card|answer\s*key", title, re.I))
            category = (
                "Admit Card / Intimation"
                if is_admit
                else ("Examination Result" if is_result else "National Examination Notice")
            )
            entry = ParsedNotification(
                title=title,
                organization_name=self.organization_name,
                job_category=category,
                exam_date=inline_date if inline_date and re.search(r"schedule|exam", title, re.I) else None,
                admit_card_date=pub_date if is_admit else None,
                result_date=pub_date if is_result else None,
                description=f"Official NTA Public Notice released on {pub_date}." if pub_date else None,
                pdf_url=href,
                source_url=effective_base,
                published_date=pub_date,
            )
            if pub_date:
                dated_results.append(entry)
            else:
                general_results.append(entry)
        combined = dated_results + general_results
        if combined:
            return combined[:12]

        for a in soup.select("a"):
            title = clean_text(a.get_text())
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, a.get("href"))
            if not title or not href or len(title) < 15 or title in seen:
                continue
            if re.search(r"nta\.nic\.in|Notice|Examination|CUET|JEE|NEET|UGC|AISSEE|NCET|SWAYAM", title + href, re.I):
                seen.add(title)
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        job_category="National Examination",
                        pdf_url=href if ".pdf" in href.lower() else None,
                        source_url=href,
                    )
                )
        return results[:12]


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/rbi.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, is_public_http_url, normalize_human_date, resolve_url


def parse_rbi_detail_html(item: ParsedNotification, html: str, detail_url: str) -> ParsedNotification:
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
    container = soup.select_one("#pnlContent, .tablebg, main") or soup
    txt = clean_text(container.get_text()) or ""

    pub_m = re.search(r"Date\s*:\s*([A-Za-z]+\s+\d{1,2},\s*\d{4}|\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})", txt, re.I)
    if pub_m:
        item.published_date = normalize_human_date(pub_m.group(1))

    last_m = re.search(
        r"(?:last\s+date[^.]{0,80}?|on\s+or\s+before|reach[^.]{0,40}?by)\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|[A-Za-z]+\s+\d{1,2},\s*\d{4}|\d{1,2}\s+[A-Za-z]+\s*,?\s*\d{4})",
        txt,
        re.I,
    )
    if last_m:
        item.application_deadline = normalize_human_date(last_m.group(1))

    vac_m = re.search(r"\b(?:fill|total|engage)\s+(?:[a-z]+\s+)?\((\d{1,4})\)\s*(?:posts?|vacanc(?:y|ies))", txt, re.I) or re.search(
        r"\b(\d{1,4})\s+(?:posts?|vacanc(?:y|ies))\b", txt, re.I
    )
    if vac_m:
        item.vacancy_count = vac_m.group(1)

    pdfs = []
    for a in soup.select('a[href*=".pdf"]'):
        resolved = resolve_url(detail_url, a.get("href"))
        if resolved and is_public_http_url(resolved) and not re.search(r"Annual_Recruitment_Calendar|Utkarsh2029", resolved, re.I):
            pdfs.append(resolved)
    if pdfs:
        item.pdf_url = pdfs[0]

    para_el = soup.select_one("#pnlContent p, .tablebg p")
    para = clean_text(para_el.get_text()) if para_el else None
    if para and len(para) > 30:
        item.description = para[:260]
    return item


class RbiParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "rbi"
    organization_name = "Reserve Bank of India (RBI Opportunities)"
    base_url = "https://opportunities.rbi.org.in/Scripts/Vacancies.aspx"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        notices = soup.select(".rbi-vacancies-list .rbi-notice")
        if notices:
            for notice in notices:
                title_el = notice.select_one("a.rbi-link")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                pdf_el = notice.select_one("a.rbi-pdf")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(notice.select_one(".rbi-advt").get_text() if notice.select_one(".rbi-advt") else None),
                        vacancy_count=clean_text(notice.select_one(".f-vac").get_text() if notice.select_one(".f-vac") else None),
                        job_category=clean_text(notice.get("data-category")) or "Central Banking",
                        application_start_date=clean_text(notice.select_one(".f-start").get_text() if notice.select_one(".f-start") else None),
                        application_deadline=clean_text(notice.select_one(".f-deadline").get_text() if notice.select_one(".f-deadline") else None),
                        exam_date=clean_text(notice.select_one(".f-exam").get_text() if notice.select_one(".f-exam") else None),
                        admit_card_date=clean_text(notice.select_one(".f-admit").get_text() if notice.select_one(".f-admit") else None),
                        result_date=clean_text(notice.select_one(".f-result").get_text() if notice.select_one(".f-result") else None),
                        description=clean_text(notice.select_one("p.rbi-summary").get_text() if notice.select_one("p.rbi-summary") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href")) or effective_base,
                        published_date=clean_text(notice.select_one(".f-pub").get_text() if notice.select_one(".f-pub") else None),
                    )
                )
            return results

        seen = set()
        for a in soup.select('a[href*="bs_viewcontent"]'):
            title = clean_text(a.get_text())
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, a.get("href"))
            if not title or not href or len(title) < 20:
                continue
            if re.match(r"^(Recruitment|Promotion|Rosters at ROs)$", title, re.I):
                continue
            if href in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(href)
            id_m = re.search(r"[?&]Id=(\d+)", href, re.I)
            results.append(
                ParsedNotification(
                    title=title,
                    organization_name=self.organization_name,
                    notification_number=f"RBI-VAC-{id_m.group(1)}" if id_m else None,
                    job_category="Central Banking",
                    source_url=href,
                )
            )
        return results[:10]


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/rrbcdg.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, resolve_url


class RrbcdgParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "rrbcdg"
    organization_name = "Railway Recruitment Boards (RRB Chandigarh)"
    base_url = "https://www.rrbcdg.gov.in/"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        rows = soup.select("table.cen-notice-table tbody tr")
        if rows:
            for row in rows:
                title_el = row.select_one("td.cen-title a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                pdf_el = row.select_one("td.cen-doc a")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-id").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-id") else None),
                        vacancy_count=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-posts").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-posts") else None),
                        job_category=clean_text(row.get("data-cat")) or "Indian Railways",
                        application_start_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-open").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-open") else None),
                        application_deadline=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-close").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-close") else None),
                        exam_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-cbt").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-cbt") else None),
                        admit_card_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-ecall").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-ecall") else None),
                        result_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-res").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-res") else None),
                        description=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-summary").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-summary") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href")) or effective_base,
                        published_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.cen-pub").get_text() if row.select_one("td.cen-pub") else None),
                    )
                )
            return results

        for a in soup.select('a[href*=".pdf"], table a'):
            title = clean_text(a.get_text())
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, a.get("href"))
            if title and href and len(title) > 18 and re.search(r"cen|alp|ntpc|technician|admit|result", title, re.I):
                cen_m = re.search(r"CEN\s*\d+/\d{4}", title, re.I)
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=cen_m.group(0) if cen_m else None,
                        job_category="Indian Railways",
                        description="Live RRB notice.",
                        pdf_url=href if ".pdf" in href.lower() else None,
                        source_url=href,
                    )
                )
        return results[:12]

===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/sbi.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, is_public_http_url, normalize_human_date, resolve_url


class SbiParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "sbi"
    organization_name = "State Bank of India (SBI Careers)"
    base_url = "https://sbi.co.in/web/careers/current-openings"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        rows = soup.select("table.sbi-careers-table tbody tr.sbi-opening")
        if rows:
            for row in rows:
                title_el = row.select_one("td.title-col a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                pdf_el = row.select_one("td.pdf-col a")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(row.select_one("td.advt-col").get_text() if row.select_one("td.advt-col") else None),
                        vacancy_count=clean_text(row.select_one("td.vac-col").get_text() if row.select_one("td.vac-col") else None),
                        job_category=clean_text(row.get("data-category")) or "SBI Banking Recruitment",
                        application_start_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.start-col").get_text() if row.select_one("td.start-col") else None),
                        application_deadline=clean_text(row.select_one("td.end-col").get_text() if row.select_one("td.end-col") else None),
                        exam_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.exam-col").get_text() if row.select_one("td.exam-col") else None),
                        admit_card_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.admit-col").get_text() if row.select_one("td.admit-col") else None),
                        result_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.res-col").get_text() if row.select_one("td.res-col") else None),
                        description=clean_text(row.select_one("td.title-col .opening-note").get_text() if row.select_one("td.title-col .opening-note") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href")) or effective_base,
                        published_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.pub-col").get_text() if row.select_one("td.pub-col") else None),
                    )
                )
            return results

        seen = set()
        for a in soup.select('li.icon-touch a[href*=".pdf"]'):
            raw_href = a.get("href") or ""
            link_text = clean_text(a.get_text()) or ""
            if not re.search(r"english|adv|detailed", link_text + raw_href, re.I):
                continue
            resolved_pdf = resolve_url(effective_base, raw_href)
            if not is_public_http_url(resolved_pdf):
                continue
            collapse_div = a.find_parent(class_=re.compile(r"panel-collapse|accordion-collapse|collapse|card-body"))
            prev_title = clean_text(collapse_div.find_previous_sibling().get_text()) if collapse_div and collapse_div.find_previous_sibling() else None
            card = a.find_parent(class_=re.compile(r"panel|card|accordion-item"))
            card_meta = clean_text(card.select_one("h3, h4, h5, button, .panel-title, .accordion-button").get_text()) if card and card.select_one("h3, h4, h5, button, .panel-title, .accordion-button") else ""
            raw_title = prev_title or card_meta
            if not raw_title or len(raw_title) < 15:
                continue

            full_card_text = clean_text(card.get_text()) if card else card_meta
            clean_title = clean_text(re.sub(r"\(Apply Online from[^)]+\)", "", raw_title, flags=re.I)) or raw_title
            if clean_title in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(clean_title)

            win_m = re.search(r"Apply Online from\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})\s*TO\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})", full_card_text, re.I)
            start_date = normalize_human_date(win_m.group(1)) if win_m else None
            win_end = normalize_human_date(win_m.group(2)) if win_m else None
            dl_m = re.search(r"LAST DATE TO APPLY\s*:\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})", full_card_text, re.I)
            deadline = (normalize_human_date(dl_m.group(1)) if dl_m else None) or win_end

            advt_m = re.search(r"ADVERTISEMENT NO:\s*([A-Z0-9/_-]+)", full_card_text, re.I)
            advt_no = advt_m.group(1) if advt_m else None
            if not advt_no:
                href_m = re.search(r"CRPD[-_][A-Z0-9_-]+|SCO-\d+", raw_href, re.I)
                advt_no = href_m.group(0) if href_m else None

            results.append(
                ParsedNotification(
                    title=clean_title,
                    organization_name=self.organization_name,
                    notification_number=advt_no,
                    job_category="SBI Banking Recruitment",
                    application_start_date=start_date,
                    application_deadline=deadline,
                    description=f"SBI Current Opening ({advt_no})" if advt_no else None,
                    pdf_url=resolved_pdf,
                    source_url=effective_base,
                )
            )
        return results[:12]


===== END FILE =====

from typing import List, Optional
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, resolve_url


class SscParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "ssc"
    organization_name = "Staff Selection Commission (SSC)"
    base_url = "https://ssc.gov.in/"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        cards = soup.select("section.ssc-notice-board article.notice-board-card")
        if cards:
            for card in cards:
                title_el = card.select_one(".notice-title a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                notice_url = resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href") if title_el else None) or effective_base
                pdf_el = card.select_one("a.pdf-link")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(card.select_one(".notice-num").get_text() if card.select_one(".notice-num") else None),
                        vacancy_count=clean_text(card.select_one(".vacancies").get_text() if card.select_one(".vacancies") else None),
                        job_category=clean_text(card.get("data-category")) or "Central Group B & C",
                        application_start_date=clean_text(card.select_one(".start-date").get_text() if card.select_one(".start-date") else None),
                        application_deadline=clean_text(card.select_one(".deadline").get_text() if card.select_one(".deadline") else None),
                        exam_date=clean_text(card.select_one(".exam-date").get_text() if card.select_one(".exam-date") else None),
                        admit_card_date=clean_text(card.select_one(".admit-date").get_text() if card.select_one(".admit-date") else None),
                        result_date=clean_text(card.select_one(".result-date").get_text() if card.select_one(".result-date") else None),
                        description=clean_text(card.select_one(".notice-desc").get_text() if card.select_one(".notice-desc") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=notice_url,
                        published_date=clean_text(card.select_one(".published-date").get_text() if card.select_one(".published-date") else None),
                    )
                )
            return results

        for anchor in soup.select('a[href*=".pdf"], .latest-news-list a, .notice-board a')[:12]:
            title = clean_text(anchor.get_text())
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, anchor.get("href"))
            if title and href and len(title) > 18:
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        job_category="SSC Notice",
                        description="Live SSC public notice link.",
                        pdf_url=href if ".pdf" in href.lower() else None,
                        source_url=href,
                    )
                )
        return results

===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/tnpsc.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, is_public_http_url, normalize_human_date, resolve_url


class TnpscParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "tnpsc"
    organization_name = "Tamil Nadu Public Service Commission (TNPSC)"
    base_url = "https://www.tnpsc.gov.in/English/Notification.aspx"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        rows = soup.select("table#notification-grid tbody tr.tnpsc-item")
        if rows:
            for row in rows:
                title_el = row.select_one("td.post-title a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                notice_url = resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href") if title_el else None) or effective_base
                pdf_el = row.select_one("td.pdf a")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(row.select_one("td.advt-no").get_text() if row.select_one("td.advt-no") else None),
                        vacancy_count=clean_text(row.select_one("td.vacancies").get_text() if row.select_one("td.vacancies") else None),
                        job_category=clean_text(row.select_one("td.category").get_text() if row.select_one("td.category") else None) or "State Civil Services",
                        application_start_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.start").get_text() if row.select_one("td.start") else None),
                        application_deadline=clean_text(row.select_one("td.deadline").get_text() if row.select_one("td.deadline") else None),
                        exam_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.exam").get_text() if row.select_one("td.exam") else None),
                        admit_card_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.admit").get_text() if row.select_one("td.admit") else None),
                        result_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.result").get_text() if row.select_one("td.result") else None),
                        description=clean_text(row.select_one("td.desc").get_text() if row.select_one("td.desc") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=notice_url,
                        published_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.published").get_text() if row.select_one("td.published") else None),
                    )
                )
            return results

        for tr in soup.select("table tr"):
            tds = tr.select("td")
            if len(tds) < 5:
                continue
            notif_no = clean_text(tds[1].get_text())
            if not notif_no or not re.search(r"\d{1,3}/\d{4}", notif_no):
                continue

            pdf_anchors = [a for a in tds[2].select('a[href*=".pdf"]') if not re.search(r"addendum", a.get_text(), re.I)]
            pdf_anchor = pdf_anchors[0] if pdf_anchors else tds[2].select_one('a[href*=".pdf"]')
            title = clean_text(pdf_anchor.get_text()) if pdf_anchor else None
            if not title:
                raw_txt = clean_text(tds[2].get_text()) or ""
                title = clean_text(re.sub(r"\b(APPLY ONLINE|Download Hall Ticket|ADDENDUM_[A-Za-z0-9]+)\b", "", raw_txt, flags=re.I))
            if not title:
                continue

            pdf_url = resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_anchor.get("href") if pdf_anchor else None)
            start_date = normalize_human_date(tds[3].get_text())
            deadline = normalize_human_date(tds[4].get_text())
            raw_exam_text = clean_text(tds[5].get_text()) if len(tds) >= 6 else None
            exam_date = normalize_human_date(raw_exam_text)

            desc_parts = [f"TNPSC Notification {notif_no}"]
            if raw_exam_text:
                desc_parts.append(f"Exam Schedule: {raw_exam_text}")
            if any(
                re.search(r"hall ticket", a.get_text(), re.I)
                and not re.search(r"display:\s*none", str(a.get("style") or ""), re.I)
                for a in tds[2].select("a")
            ):
                desc_parts.append("Hall Ticket download link active on portal")

            results.append(
                ParsedNotification(
                    title=title,
                    organization_name=self.organization_name,
                    notification_number=notif_no,
                    job_category="State Civil Services",
                    application_start_date=start_date,
                    application_deadline=deadline,
                    exam_date=exam_date,
                    description=" · ".join(desc_parts),
                    pdf_url=pdf_url if is_public_http_url(pdf_url) else None,
                    source_url=effective_base,
                    published_date=start_date,
                )
            )
        return results[:12]


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/tnusrb.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, resolve_url


class TnusrbParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "tnusrb"
    organization_name = "Tamil Nadu Uniformed Services Recruitment Board (TNUSRB)"
    base_url = "https://www.tnusrb.tn.gov.in/"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        cards = soup.select(".recruitment-panel .tnusrb-card")
        if cards:
            for card in cards:
                title_el = card.select_one("h4 a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                fields = {li.get("data-field"): clean_text(li.get_text()) for li in card.select("ul.dates-list li[data-field]")}
                pdf_el = card.select_one("a.btn-pdf")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(card.select_one(".notif-code").get_text() if card.select_one(".notif-code") else None),
                        vacancy_count=fields.get("vacancies"),
                        job_category=clean_text(card.get("data-category")) or "Police & Uniformed Services",
                        application_start_date=fields.get("start"),
                        application_deadline=fields.get("deadline"),
                        exam_date=fields.get("exam"),
                        admit_card_date=fields.get("admit"),
                        result_date=fields.get("result"),
                        description=clean_text(card.select_one("p.details").get_text() if card.select_one("p.details") else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href")) or effective_base,
                        published_date=fields.get("published"),
                    )
                )
            return results

        for a in soup.select("a"):
            title = clean_text(a.get_text())
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, a.get("href"))
            if title and href and len(title) > 18 and re.search(r"sub-inspector|constable|recruitment|notification|brochure", title, re.I):
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        job_category="Police & Uniformed Services",
                        description="Live TNUSRB recruitment notice.",
                        pdf_url=href if ".pdf" in href.lower() else None,
                        source_url=href,
                    )
                )
        return results[:10]


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: backend/app/scrapers/upsc.py =====

import re
from typing import List, Optional
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from .base import BasePortalParser, ParsedNotification, clean_text, is_public_http_url, normalize_human_date, resolve_url


def parse_upsc_detail_html(item: ParsedNotification, html: str) -> ParsedNotification:
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
    details: List[str] = []

    if not item.notification_number:
        m = re.search(r"Examination Notice No:\s*([A-Z0-9/()-]+)", soup.get_text(" "), re.I)
        if m and m.group(1) != "—":
            item.notification_number = clean_text(m.group(1))

    for col in soup.select(".exam-detail-card .col-lg, .exam-detail-card > div"):
        children = [c for c in col.find_all(recursive=False)]
        if len(children) < 2:
            continue
        label = clean_text(children[0].get_text()) or ""
        value = clean_text(children[1].get_text()) or ""
        if not label or not value:
            continue

        if re.search(r"date of notification", label, re.I):
            iso = normalize_human_date(value)
            if iso:
                item.published_date = iso
                if not item.application_start_date:
                    item.application_start_date = iso
        elif re.search(r"date of upload", label, re.I) and not item.published_date:
            iso = normalize_human_date(value)
            if iso:
                item.published_date = iso
        elif re.search(r"commencement of examination", label, re.I):
            iso = normalize_human_date(value)
            if iso:
                item.exam_date = iso
        elif re.search(r"last date for submission", label, re.I):
            iso = normalize_human_date(value)
            item.application_deadline = iso
            if not iso:
                item.application_start_date = None
        elif re.search(r"duration of examination", label, re.I):
            details.append(f"Duration: {value}")

    doc_labels: List[str] = []
    for tr in soup.select("table tr"):
        tds = tr.select("td")
        if len(tds) < 2:
            continue
        doc_type = clean_text(tds[0].get_text()) or ""
        upload_date = normalize_human_date(tds[1].get_text())
        if not doc_type or "document type" in doc_type.lower():
            continue
        doc_labels.append(re.sub(r"keyboard_arrow_down", "", doc_type, flags=re.I).strip())
        if re.search(r"admit\s*card", doc_type, re.I) and upload_date:
            item.admit_card_date = upload_date
        elif re.search(r"(?:written|final|recommended).*result|^\s*result", doc_type, re.I) and upload_date:
            item.result_date = upload_date

    pdf_matches = list(
        dict.fromkeys(
            u
            for u in re.findall(r"https?://(?:www\.)?upsc\.gov\.in/uploads/[^\"'\s\\]+\.pdf", html, re.I)
            if is_public_http_url(u)
        )
    )
    if pdf_matches:
        eng_notif = next((u for u in pdf_matches if re.search(r"notif", u, re.I) and re.search(r"engl", u, re.I)), None)
        any_notif = next((u for u in pdf_matches if re.search(r"notif", u, re.I)), None)
        any_eng = next((u for u in pdf_matches if re.search(r"engl", u, re.I)), None)
        item.pdf_url = eng_notif or any_notif or any_eng or pdf_matches[0]

    fact_parts: List[str] = []
    if item.notification_number:
        fact_parts.append(f"Notice No: {item.notification_number}")
    fact_parts.extend(details)
    if doc_labels:
        fact_parts.append("Documents: " + ", ".join(doc_labels[:5]))
    if fact_parts:
        item.description = " · ".join(fact_parts)

    return item


class UpscParser(BasePortalParser):
    parser_key = "upsc"
    organization_name = "Union Public Service Commission (UPSC)"
    base_url = "https://upsc.gov.in/active-examinations/"

    def parse(self, html: str, source_url: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ParsedNotification]:
        soup = self.soup(html)
        effective_base = source_url or self.base_url
        results: List[ParsedNotification] = []

        rows = soup.select("table.upsc-notice-table tbody tr.upsc-row")
        if rows:
            for row in rows:
                title_el = row.select_one("td.col-title a")
                title = clean_text(title_el.get_text()) if title_el else None
                if not title:
                    continue
                notice_url = resolve_url(effective_base, title_el.get("href") if title_el else None) or effective_base
                summary_el = row.select_one("td.col-title p.summary")
                pdf_el = row.select_one("td.col-pdf a")
                results.append(
                    ParsedNotification(
                        title=title,
                        organization_name=self.organization_name,
                        notification_number=clean_text(row.select_one("td.col-advt").get_text() if row.select_one("td.col-advt") else None),
                        vacancy_count=clean_text(row.select_one("td.col-vacancy").get_text() if row.select_one("td.col-vacancy") else None),
                        job_category=clean_text(row.get("data-category")) or "Central Civil Services",
                        application_start_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.col-start").get_text() if row.select_one("td.col-start") else None),
                        application_deadline=clean_text(row.select_one("td.col-deadline").get_text() if row.select_one("td.col-deadline") else None),
                        exam_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.col-exam").get_text() if row.select_one("td.col-exam") else None),
                        admit_card_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.col-admit").get_text() if row.select_one("td.col-admit") else None),
                        result_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.col-result").get_text() if row.select_one("td.col-result") else None),
                        description=clean_text(summary_el.get_text() if summary_el else None),
                        pdf_url=resolve_url(effective_base, pdf_el.get("href") if pdf_el else None),
                        source_url=notice_url,
                        published_date=clean_text(row.select_one("td.col-published").get_text() if row.select_one("td.col-published") else None),
                    )
                )
            return results

        for tr in soup.select("table tr"):
            tds = tr.select("td")
            if len(tds) < 3:
                continue
            anchor = tds[0].select_one("a")
            title = clean_text(anchor.get_text() if anchor else tds[0].get_text())
            if not title or "examination name" in title.lower():
                continue
            notice_no = clean_text(tds[1].get_text())
            exam_date = normalize_human_date(tds[2].get_text())
            deadline = normalize_human_date(tds[3].get_text()) if len(tds) >= 4 else None
            href = resolve_url(effective_base, anchor.get("href") if anchor else None) or effective_base
            results.append(
                ParsedNotification(
                    title=title,
                    organization_name=self.organization_name,
                    notification_number=notice_no if notice_no and notice_no != "—" else None,
                    job_category="UPSC Active Examination",
                    application_deadline=deadline,
                    exam_date=exam_date,
                    description=f"Examination Notice No: {notice_no}" if notice_no and notice_no != "—" else None,
                    pdf_url=href if href.lower().endswith(".pdf") else None,
                    source_url=href,
                )
            )
        return results[:12]


===== END FILE =====

def seed_database(db: Session):
    # Seed default Admin & Aspirant users if none exist
    if db.query(User).count() == 0:
        db.add_all(
            [
                User(
                    full_name="Arjun Krishnan (Admin)",
                    email="admin@govtrack.in",
                    password_hash=hash_password("Admin@2026"),
                    role="admin",
                    target_exams="All 10 Official Portals",
                    email_alerts_enabled=True,
                ),
                User(
                    full_name="Priya Sharma",
                    email="aspirant@govtrack.in",
                    password_hash=hash_password("Aspirant@2026"),
                    role="user",
                    target_exams="UPSC, SSC, IBPS, TNPSC",
                    email_alerts_enabled=True,
                ),
            ]
        )
        db.commit()

    # Seed all 10 official government recruitment portals
    created_sources = []
    for src_data in DEFAULT_SOURCES:
        existing = db.query(MonitoringSource).filter(MonitoringSource.code == src_data["code"]).first()
        if not existing:
            src = MonitoringSource(**src_data)
            db.add(src)
            created_sources.append(src)
    if created_sources:
        db.commit()
        for src in created_sources:
            db.refresh(src)
            run_source_scrape(db, src, trigger_type="scheduled", mode_override="auto", suppress_history=True)

import json
import re
import sqlite3
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT_DIR))

from backend.app.diff_core import (  # noqa: E402
    build_entity_key_from_parts,
    classify_field_change,
    compute_content_hash,
    compute_field_diffs,
    merge_preserving_missing_fields,
    normalize_snapshot,
)

FIXTURES_DIR = ROOT_DIR / "backend" / "fixtures"

try:
    from bs4 import BeautifulSoup  # type: ignore
    HAS_BS4 = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_BS4 = False


def extract_upsc_rows_stdlib(html: str) -> list[dict]:
    """Minimal stdlib fallback extractor for environments without bs4 installed."""
    rows = []
    for tr_match in re.finditer(r'<tr class="upsc-row"[^>]*data-category="([^"]*)"[^>]*>(.*?)</tr>', html, re.S):
        cat, body = tr_match.group(1), tr_match.group(2)

        def cell(cls: str):
            m = re.search(rf'<td class="{cls}"[^>]*>(.*?)</td>', body, re.S)
            if not m:
                return None
            text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", m.group(1))
            text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
            return text or None

        title_m = re.search(r'<td class="col-title">.*?<a href="([^"]+)">(.*?)</a>', body, re.S)
        sum_m = re.search(r'<p class="summary">(.*?)</p>', body, re.S)
        pdf_m = re.search(r'<td class="col-pdf"><a href="([^"]+)">', body, re.S)
        rows.append(
            normalize_snapshot(
                {
                    "title": title_m.group(2) if title_m else None,
                    "organization_name": "Union Public Service Commission (UPSC)",
                    "notification_number": cell("col-advt"),
                    "vacancy_count": cell("col-vacancy"),
                    "job_category": cat,
                    "application_start_date": cell("col-start"),
                    "application_deadline": cell("col-deadline"),
                    "exam_date": cell("col-exam"),
                    "admit_card_date": cell("col-admit"),
                    "result_date": cell("col-result"),
                    "description": sum_m.group(1) if sum_m else None,
                    "pdf_url": pdf_m.group(1) if pdf_m else None,
                    "source_url": title_m.group(1) if title_m else "https://upsc.gov.in/",
                    "published_date": cell("col-published"),
                }
            )
        )
    return rows


class InMemoryMonitorPipeline:
    """Deterministic change-detection pipeline mirror for reproducible offline unit testing."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.records: dict[str, dict] = {}
        self.versions: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
        self.changes: list[dict] = []
        self.alerts: list[dict] = []
        self.missing_counts: dict[str, int] = {}

    def ingest(self, source_code: str, items: list[dict], http_status: int = 200) -> dict:
        counts = {"new": 0, "updated": 0, "duplicate": 0, "removed": 0, "error": 0}
        if http_status >= 400 or len(items) == 0:
            # SRS FR-05 & CP-5: Never treat 404/429/403 or 0-result parses as deletions
            counts["error"] = 1
            return counts

        seen = set()
        for raw in items:
            raw_snap = normalize_snapshot(raw)
            key = build_entity_key_from_parts(source_code, raw_snap["notification_number"], raw_snap["title"])
            if key in seen:
                counts["duplicate"] += 1
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            self.missing_counts[key] = 0

            if key not in self.records:
                new_hash = compute_content_hash(raw_snap)
                self.records[key] = {"hash": new_hash, "snap": raw_snap, "removed": False}
                self.versions[key] = [dict(raw_snap)]
                self.changes.append({
                    "key": key,
                    "type": "NEW",
                    "changed_field": "title",
                    "change_label": "NEW NOTIFICATION PUBLISHED",
                    "previous_value": None,
                    "new_value": raw_snap["title"],
                    "diffs": [{"field": "title", "old_value": None, "new_value": raw_snap["title"]}],
                })
                self.alerts.append({"key": key, "type": "NEW"})
                counts["new"] += 1
            else:
                prev = self.records[key]
                if prev["removed"]:
                    prev["removed"] = False
                snap = merge_preserving_missing_fields(prev["snap"], raw_snap)
                new_hash = compute_content_hash(snap)
                if prev["hash"] == new_hash:
                    counts["duplicate"] += 1
                    continue
                diffs = compute_field_diffs(prev["snap"], snap)
                if not diffs:
                    counts["duplicate"] += 1
                    continue
                self.records[key] = {"hash": new_hash, "snap": snap, "removed": False}
                self.versions[key].append(dict(snap))
                for diff in diffs:
                    label = classify_field_change(diff["field"], diff["old_value"], diff["new_value"])
                    self.changes.append({
                        "key": key,
                        "type": "UPDATED",
                        "changed_field": diff["field"],
                        "change_label": label,
                        "previous_value": diff["old_value"],
                        "new_value": diff["new_value"],
                        "diffs": [diff],
                    })
                    self.alerts.append({
                        "key": key,
                        "type": "UPDATED",
                        "label": label,
                        "previous_value": diff["old_value"],
                        "new_value": diff["new_value"],
                    })
                counts["updated"] += 1

        for key, rec in self.records.items():
            if key not in seen and not rec["removed"]:
                self.missing_counts[key] = self.missing_counts.get(key, 0) + 1
                if self.missing_counts[key] >= 2:
                    rec["removed"] = True
                    self.changes.append({
                        "key": key,
                        "type": "REMOVED",
                        "changed_field": "notification_status",
                        "change_label": "NOTIFICATION REMOVED FROM PORTAL",
                        "previous_value": "active",
                        "new_value": "removed",
                        "diffs": [{"field": "notification_status", "old_value": "active", "new_value": "removed"}],
                    })
                    self.alerts.append({"key": key, "type": "REMOVED"})
                    counts["removed"] += 1
        return counts


class TestChangeDetectionSuite(unittest.TestCase):
    """20 labelled change-detection, provenance, version-history, and failure-safety test cases."""

    def test_01_new_notification_detection(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        rows = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        res = pipeline.ingest("upsc", rows)
        self.assertEqual(res["new"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(res["updated"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(pipeline.alerts), 3)

    def test_02_exact_rerun_duplicate_suppression(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        rows = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", rows)
        res2 = pipeline.ingest("upsc", rows)
        self.assertEqual(res2["new"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(res2["updated"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(res2["duplicate"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(len(pipeline.alerts), 3)

    def test_03_formatting_only_html_edit_causes_zero_false_alerts(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        fmt = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc_format_only.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1)
        res = pipeline.ingest("upsc", fmt)
        self.assertEqual(res["updated"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(res["duplicate"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(len(pipeline.alerts), 3)

    def test_04_deadline_extension_and_vacancy_update_diff(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        v2 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc_v2_updated.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1)
        res = pipeline.ingest("upsc", v2)
        self.assertEqual(res["new"], 1)  # 07/2026-CMS added
        self.assertEqual(res["updated"], 1)  # 05/2026-CSP updated
        updated_events = [c for c in pipeline.changes if c["type"] == "UPDATED"]
        by_field = {c["changed_field"]: c for c in updated_events}
        self.assertIn("application_deadline", by_field)
        self.assertEqual(by_field["application_deadline"]["change_label"], "APPLICATION DEADLINE EXTENDED")
        self.assertEqual(by_field["application_deadline"]["previous_value"], "2026-03-05")
        self.assertEqual(by_field["application_deadline"]["new_value"], "2026-03-15")
        self.assertEqual(by_field["vacancy_count"]["change_label"], "VACANCY COUNT UPDATED")
        self.assertEqual(by_field["vacancy_count"]["previous_value"], "1056")
        self.assertEqual(by_field["vacancy_count"]["new_value"], "1105")

    def test_05_http_404_never_deletes_existing_notifications(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1)
        res_404 = pipeline.ingest("upsc", [], http_status=404)
        self.assertEqual(res_404["error"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(res_404["removed"], 0)
        self.assertTrue(all(not rec["removed"] for rec in pipeline.records.values()))

    def test_06_http_404_followed_by_restored_page_no_false_change(self):
        """SRS CP-5: Mentor supplies 404 followed by restored identical page -> zero false changes."""
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1)
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", [], http_status=404)
        res_restored = pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1, http_status=200)
        self.assertEqual(res_restored["new"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(res_restored["updated"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(res_restored["removed"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(res_restored["duplicate"], 3)

    def test_07_http_429_rate_limit_preserves_state(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1)
        res_429 = pipeline.ingest("upsc", [], http_status=429)
        self.assertEqual(res_429["error"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(pipeline.records), 3)

    def test_08_zero_result_parse_failure_guard(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1)
        broken_html = (FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc_404_error.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        parsed_broken = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib(broken_html)
        self.assertEqual(len(parsed_broken), 0)
        res = pipeline.ingest("upsc", parsed_broken, http_status=200)
        self.assertEqual(res["error"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(res["removed"], 0)

    def test_09_in_batch_duplicate_rows_suppressed(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        rows = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        doubled = rows + [rows[0]]
        res = pipeline.ingest("upsc", doubled)
        self.assertEqual(res["new"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(res["duplicate"], 1)

    def test_10_missing_fields_stay_none_never_invented(self):
        rows = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        csp = rows[0]
        self.assertIsNone(csp["admit_card_date"])
        self.assertIsNone(csp["result_date"])

    def test_11_safe_removal_requires_consecutive_healthy_parses(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        rows = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", rows)
        subset = rows[:2]  # 3rd item missing once -> should NOT immediately mark removed
        res1 = pipeline.ingest("upsc", subset)
        self.assertEqual(res1["removed"], 0)
        res2 = pipeline.ingest("upsc", subset)  # Absent across 2nd consecutive healthy parse -> REMOVED
        self.assertEqual(res2["removed"], 1)

    def test_12_pdf_link_update_detected_with_before_after(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        v2 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc_v2_updated.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1)
        pipeline.ingest("upsc", v2)
        pdf_events = [c for c in pipeline.changes if c["type"] == "UPDATED" and c["changed_field"] == "pdf_url"]
        self.assertEqual(len(pdf_events), 1)
        self.assertIsNone(pdf_events[0]["previous_value"])
        self.assertIn("upsc.gov.in/active-examinations", pdf_events[0]["new_value"])

    def test_13_entity_key_falls_back_to_title_when_number_missing(self):
        key = build_entity_key_from_parts("nta", None, "UGC NET December 2026 Examination")
        self.assertEqual(key, "nta::ugc-net-december-2026-examination")

    def test_14_all_10_offline_html_fixtures_exist_and_non_empty(self):
        expected = [
            "upsc.html", "ssc.html", "tnpsc.html", "tnusrb.html", "rrbcdg.html",
            "ibps.html", "sbi.html", "rbi.html", "nta.html", "indiapost.html",
        ]
        for fname in expected:
            fpath = FIXTURES_DIR / fname
            self.assertTrue(fpath.exists(), f"Missing fixture: {fname}")
            self.assertGreater(len(fpath.read_text(encoding="utf-8")), 200)

    def test_15_three_cycle_timestamped_refresh_consistency(self):
        """SRS Section 9: Simulate 3 refresh cycles (baseline -> update -> steady rerun)."""
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        v2 = extract_upsc_rows_stdlib((FIXTURES_DIR / "upsc_v2_updated.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        r1 = pipeline.ingest("upsc", v1)
        r2 = pipeline.ingest("upsc", v2)
        r3 = pipeline.ingest("upsc", v2)
        self.assertEqual((r1["new"], r1["updated"], r1["duplicate"]), (3, 0, 0))
        self.assertEqual((r2["new"], r2["updated"], r2["duplicate"]), (1, 1, 2))
        self.assertEqual((r3["new"], r3["updated"], r3["duplicate"]), (0, 0, 4))

    def test_16_exam_date_change_creates_separate_field_record_and_alert(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        base = {
            "title": "IBPS – CRP Recruitment",
            "organization_name": "Institute of Banking Personnel Selection (IBPS)",
            "notification_number": "CRP-PO-XVI",
            "application_start_date": "2026-07-01",
            "application_deadline": "2026-07-21",
            "exam_date": "2026-08-10",
            "source_url": "https://www.ibps.in/",
        }
        updated = dict(base, exam_date="2026-08-25")
        pipeline.ingest("ibps", [base])
        res = pipeline.ingest("ibps", [updated])

        self.assertEqual(res["updated"], 1)
        exam_changes = [c for c in pipeline.changes if c["changed_field"] == "exam_date"]
        self.assertEqual(len(exam_changes), 1)
        self.assertEqual(exam_changes[0]["change_label"], "EXAM DATE CHANGED")
        self.assertEqual(exam_changes[0]["previous_value"], "2026-08-10")
        self.assertEqual(exam_changes[0]["new_value"], "2026-08-25")

        exam_alerts = [a for a in pipeline.alerts if a.get("label") == "EXAM DATE CHANGED"]
        self.assertEqual(len(exam_alerts), 1)
        self.assertEqual(exam_alerts[0]["previous_value"], "2026-08-10")
        self.assertEqual(exam_alerts[0]["new_value"], "2026-08-25")

    def test_17_multi_field_update_creates_separate_change_records_per_field(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = {
            "title": "IBPS – CRP Recruitment",
            "organization_name": "IBPS",
            "notification_number": "CRP-CLERK-XVI",
            "application_deadline": "2026-07-21",
            "exam_date": "2026-08-10",
            "vacancy_count": "6128",
            "source_url": "https://www.ibps.in/",

        v2 = dict(v1, exam_date="2026-08-25", application_deadline="2026-07-31", vacancy_count="6500")
        pipeline.ingest("ibps", [v1])
        pipeline.ingest("ibps", [v2])

        updated = [c for c in pipeline.changes if c["type"] == "UPDATED"]
        self.assertEqual(len(updated), 3)
        labels = {c["changed_field"]: c["change_label"] for c in updated}
        self.assertEqual(labels["exam_date"], "EXAM DATE CHANGED")
        self.assertEqual(labels["application_deadline"], "APPLICATION DEADLINE EXTENDED")
        self.assertEqual(labels["vacancy_count"], "VACANCY COUNT UPDATED")

    def test_18_missing_exam_date_on_partial_scrape_never_deletes_existing_exam_date(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = {
            "title": "IBPS – CRP Recruitment",
            "organization_name": "IBPS",
            "notification_number": "CRP-RRB-XV",
            "exam_date": "2026-08-10",
            "source_url": "https://www.ibps.in/",
        }
        partial = dict(v1, exam_date=None)
        pipeline.ingest("ibps", [v1])
        res = pipeline.ingest("ibps", [partial])
        self.assertEqual(res["updated"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(res["duplicate"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(pipeline.records["ibps::crp-rrb-xv"]["snap"]["exam_date"], "2026-08-10")

    def test_19_version_history_preserves_previous_snapshots(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = {
            "title": "IBPS – CRP Recruitment",
            "organization_name": "IBPS",
            "notification_number": "CRP-SO-XVI",
            "exam_date": "2026-08-10",
            "source_url": "https://www.ibps.in/",
        }
        v2 = dict(v1, exam_date="2026-08-25")
        pipeline.ingest("ibps", [v1])
        pipeline.ingest("ibps", [v2])
        versions = pipeline.versions["ibps::crp-so-xvi"]
        self.assertEqual(len(versions), 2)
        self.assertEqual(versions[0]["exam_date"], "2026-08-10")
        self.assertEqual(versions[1]["exam_date"], "2026-08-25")

    def test_20_notification_cancellation_detected(self):
        pipeline = InMemoryMonitorPipeline()
        v1 = {
            "title": "SSC Special Recruitment Notification",
            "organization_name": "SSC",
            "notification_number": "SSC-SP-2026",
            "exam_date": "2026-09-12",
            "source_url": "https://ssc.gov.in/",
        }
        v2 = dict(v1, notification_status="cancelled")
        pipeline.ingest("ssc", [v1])
        pipeline.ingest("ssc", [v2])
        cancel_events = [c for c in pipeline.changes if c["change_label"] == "NOTIFICATION CANCELLED"]
        self.assertEqual(len(cancel_events), 1)
        self.assertEqual(cancel_events[0]["previous_value"], "active")
        self.assertEqual(cancel_events[0]["new_value"], "cancelled")
