===== FILE: backend/app/pdf_extractor.py =====

import re
import zlib
from typing import Dict, Optional

import requests

from .config import SCRAPER_USER_AGENT
from .scrapers.base import ParsedNotification, clean_text, is_public_http_url, normalize_human_date


def extract_text_from_pdf_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
    chunks = []
    stream_pattern = re.compile(rb"stream\r?\n(.*?)\r?\n?endstream", re.S)
    for idx, match in enumerate(stream_pattern.finditer(data)):
        if idx >= 160:
            break
        raw_stream = match.group(1)
        try:
            target = zlib.decompress(raw_stream).decode("latin1", errors="ignore")
        except Exception:
            try:
                target = zlib.decompress(raw_stream, -zlib.MAX_WBITS).decode("latin1", errors="ignore")
            except Exception:
                target = raw_stream.decode("latin1", errors="ignore")

        str_matches = re.findall(r"\((?:\\.|[^\\()])*\)", target)
        if str_matches:
            joined = " ".join(
                s[1:-1]
                .replace("\\n", " ")
                .replace("\\r", " ")
                .replace("\\t", " ")
                .replace("\\(", "(")
                .replace("\\)", ")")
                .replace("\\\\", "\\")
                for s in str_matches
            )
            joined = re.sub(r"[^\x20-\x7E]+", " ", joined)
            joined = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", joined).strip()
            if len(joined) > 8:
                chunks.append(joined)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", " ".join(chunks)).strip()


def extract_structured_fields_from_pdf_text(text: str) -> Dict[str, Optional[str]]:
    result: Dict[str, Optional[str]] = {
        "application_start_date": None,
        "application_deadline": None,
        "exam_date": None,
        "admit_card_date": None,
        "result_date": None,
        "vacancy_count": None,
        "notification_status": None,
    }
    if not text or len(text) < 30:
        return result

    if re.search(r"\b(?:notification|examination|recruitment)\s+(?:stands\s+)?(?:cancelled|canceled|withdrawn|annulled)\b", text, re.I):
        result["notification_status"] = "cancelled"

    reg_window = re.search(
        r"(?:On-?line\s+Registration(?:\s+of\s+Applications)?|Registration\s+Window)[^0-9]{0,55}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})\s*(?:to|TO|–|-|&)\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})",
        text,
        re.I,
    )
    if reg_window:
        result["application_start_date"] = normalize_human_date(reg_window.group(1))
        result["application_deadline"] = normalize_human_date(reg_window.group(2))

    if not result["application_deadline"]:
        deadline_m = re.search(
            r"(?:Last\s+Date\s+(?:for|of)\s+(?:Online\s+)?(?:Submission|Application|Registration)|Closing\s+Date)[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,25}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9},?\s+\d{4})",
            text,
            re.I,
        )
        if deadline_m:
            result["application_deadline"] = normalize_human_date(deadline_m.group(1))

    exam_m = re.search(
        r"(?:Date\s+of\s+(?:Online\s+)?(?:Preliminary\s+|Main\s+|Written\s+|CBT\s+)?Examination|Online\s+Examination\s*\(?(?:Preliminary|Mains|CBT)?\)?)[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,30}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9},?\s+\d{4})",
        text,
        re.I,
    )
    if exam_m:
        result["exam_date"] = normalize_human_date(exam_m.group(1))

    admit_m = re.search(
        r"(?:Download\s+of\s+)?(?:Call\s+Letters?|Admit\s+Card|Hall\s+Ticket)[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,35}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9},?\s+\d{4})",
        text,
        re.I,
    )
    if admit_m:
        result["admit_card_date"] = normalize_human_date(admit_m.group(1))

    result_m = re.search(
        r"Declaration\s+of\s+(?:Preliminary|Main|Final|Written\s+)?Result[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,35}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9},?\s+\d{4})",
        text,
        re.I,
    )
    if result_m:
        result["result_date"] = normalize_human_date(result_m.group(1))

    vac_m = re.search(r"\bTotal\s+(?:Tentative\s+)?Vacancies\s*[:\-–]?\s*(\d{1,6})\b", text, re.I)
    if vac_m:
        num = int(vac_m.group(1))
        if 1 <= num <= 500000:
            result["vacancy_count"] = str(num)

    return result


def enrich_notification_from_pdf(item: ParsedNotification) -> ParsedNotification:
    if not item.pdf_url or not is_public_http_url(item.pdf_url):
        return item
    if item.exam_date and item.application_deadline and item.application_start_date and item.vacancy_count:
        return item

    try:
        resp = requests.get(
            item.pdf_url,
            headers={"User-Agent": SCRAPER_USER_AGENT, "Accept": "application/pdf,*/*;q=0.8"},
            timeout=9,
        )
        if resp.status_code != 200 or len(resp.content) > 4_000_000 or not resp.content[:5].startswith(b"%PDF"):
            return item
        pdf_text = extract_text_from_pdf_bytes(resp.content)
        if not pdf_text:
            return item
        extracted = extract_structured_fields_from_pdf_text(pdf_text)
        if not item.application_start_date and extracted["application_start_date"]:
            item.application_start_date = extracted["application_start_date"]
        if not item.application_deadline and extracted["application_deadline"]:
            item.application_deadline = extracted["application_deadline"]
        if not item.exam_date and extracted["exam_date"]:
            item.exam_date = extracted["exam_date"]
        if not item.admit_card_date and extracted["admit_card_date"]:
            item.admit_card_date = extracted["admit_card_date"]
        if not item.result_date and extracted["result_date"]:
            item.result_date = extracted["result_date"]
        if not item.vacancy_count and extracted["vacancy_count"]:
            item.vacancy_count = extracted["vacancy_count"]
        if extracted["notification_status"] == "cancelled":
            item.notification_status = "cancelled"
        if not item.description and extracted["exam_date"]:
            item.description = clean_text(f"Exam Date: {extracted['exam_date']}")
    except Exception:
        return item
    return item


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: backend/app/pdf_generator.py =====

def _escape_pdf_text(text: str) -> str:
    ascii_only = "".join(ch if 32 <= ord(ch) <= 126 else " " for ch in text)
    return ascii_only.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("(", "\\(").replace(")", "\\)")


def _wrap_line(text: str, max_len: int = 84) -> list[str]:
    words = text.split()
    lines: list[str] = []
    cur = ""
    for w in words:
        if len((cur + " " + w).strip()) > max_len:
            if cur:
                lines.append(cur)
            cur = w
        else:
            cur = (cur + " " + w).strip()
    if cur:
        lines.append(cur)
    return lines or [""]


def build_notification_pdf(notif: dict) -> bytes:
    lines: list[tuple[str, bool, int]] = [
        ("GOVTRACK - OFFICIAL RECRUITMENT NOTIFICATION SUMMARY", True, 13),
        ("==========================================================================", False, 9),
        ("", False, 10),
        (str(notif.get("title") or "Untitled Notification"), True, 12),
        ("", False, 10),
    ]

    fields = [
        ("Organization", notif.get("organization_name")),
        ("Notification No.", notif.get("notification_number")),
        ("Job Category", notif.get("job_category")),
        ("Vacancy Count", notif.get("vacancy_count")),
        ("Published Date", notif.get("published_date")),
        ("Application Start", notif.get("application_start_date")),
        ("Application Deadline", notif.get("application_deadline")),
        ("Examination Date", notif.get("exam_date")),
        ("Admit Card Date", notif.get("admit_card_date")),
        ("Result Date", notif.get("result_date")),
        ("Source Mode", "Offline HTML Fixture Snapshot (Demo)" if notif.get("is_demo_data") else "Live Extracted"),
        ("Original Source URL", notif.get("source_url")),
        ("Official PDF URL", notif.get("pdf_url")),
        ("First Seen At", notif.get("first_seen_at")),
        ("Last Checked At", notif.get("last_seen_at")),
    ]

    for label, val in fields:
        val_str = str(val) if val else "Not specified on source"
        for w in _wrap_line(f"{label}: {val_str}", 84):
            lines.append((w, False, 10))

    lines.append(("", False, 10))
    lines.append(("Description:", True, 10))
    for w in _wrap_line(str(notif.get("description") or "Not specified on source."), 84):
        lines.append((w, False, 10))

    ops = ["BT"]
    y = 760
    for text, bold, size in lines:
        if y < 50:
            break
        font = "/F2" if bold else "/F1"
        ops.append(f"1 0 0 1 50 {y} Tm {font} {size} Tf ({_escape_pdf_text(text)}) Tj")
        y -= size + 5
    ops.append("ET")
    stream = "\n".join(ops)

    objects = [
        "1 0 obj\n<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>\nendobj\n",
        "2 0 obj\n<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>\nendobj\n",
        "3 0 obj\n<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 612 792] /Contents 4 0 R /Resources << /Font << /F1 5 0 R /F2 6 0 R >> >> >>\nendobj\n",
        f"4 0 obj\n<< /Length {len(stream.encode('utf-8'))} >>\nstream\n{stream}\nendstream\nendobj\n",
        "5 0 obj\n<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica >>\nendobj\n",
        "6 0 obj\n<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica-Bold >>\nendobj\n",
    ]

    pdf = "%PDF-1.4\n"
    offsets = [0]
    for obj in objects:
        offsets.append(len(pdf.encode("utf-8")))
        pdf += obj
    xref_pos = len(pdf.encode("utf-8"))
    pdf += f"xref\n0 {len(objects) + 1}\n0000000000 65535 f \n"
    for i in range(1, len(objects) + 1):
        pdf += f"{offsets[i]:010d} 00000 n \n"
    pdf += f"trailer\n<< /Size {len(objects) + 1} /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n{xref_pos}\n%%EOF\n"
    return pdf.encode("utf-8")


===== END FILE =====

import path from 'node:path';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';

const DB_PATH = process.env.SQLITE_DB_PATH
  ? path.resolve(process.cwd(), process.env.SQLITE_DB_PATH)
  : path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'govtrack.db');

export const db = new DatabaseSync(DB_PATH);

db.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;');
db.exec('PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;');

export function initSchema(): void {
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
      password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
      role TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user',
      target_exams TEXT DEFAULT 'UPSC, SSC, Banking',
      email_alerts_enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
      is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
      created_at TEXT NOT NULL
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS monitoring_sources (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      organization_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      base_url TEXT NOT NULL,
      parser_key TEXT NOT NULL,
      fixture_filename TEXT NOT NULL,
      scrape_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'fixture',
      crawl_interval_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 360,
      rate_limit_seconds INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 2,
      robots_policy TEXT NOT NULL,
      permission_note TEXT NOT NULL,
      is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
      is_demo_data INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
      freshness_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Pending',
      last_attempted_at TEXT,
      last_successful_at TEXT,
      last_http_status INTEGER,
      last_error TEXT,
      last_parsed_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      last_duplicate_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      created_at TEXT NOT NULL
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recruitment_notifications (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      source_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES monitoring_sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      entity_key TEXT NOT NULL,
      content_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
      title TEXT NOT NULL,
      organization_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      notification_number TEXT,
      vacancy_count TEXT,
      job_category TEXT,
      application_start_date TEXT,
      application_deadline TEXT,
      exam_date TEXT,
      admit_card_date TEXT,
      result_date TEXT,
      description TEXT,
      pdf_url TEXT,
      source_url TEXT NOT NULL,
      published_date TEXT,
      lifecycle_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
      is_removed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      missing_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      is_demo_data INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
      first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      last_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      source_updated_at TEXT,
      last_run_id TEXT,
      UNIQUE(source_id, entity_key)
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notification_versions (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      notification_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES recruitment_notifications(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      version_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
      title TEXT NOT NULL,
      organization_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      notification_number TEXT,
      vacancy_count TEXT,
      job_category TEXT,
      application_start_date TEXT,
      application_deadline TEXT,
      exam_date TEXT,
      admit_card_date TEXT,
      result_date TEXT,
      description TEXT,
      pdf_url TEXT,
      source_url TEXT NOT NULL,
      published_date TEXT,
      notification_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
      content_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
      snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL,
      run_id TEXT,
      captured_at TEXT NOT NULL
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS change_history (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      source_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES monitoring_sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      notification_id INTEGER REFERENCES recruitment_notifications(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      entity_key TEXT NOT NULL,
      notification_title TEXT NOT NULL,
      change_type TEXT NOT NULL,
      changed_field TEXT,
      change_label TEXT,
      previous_value TEXT,
      new_value TEXT,
      changed_fields_json TEXT NOT NULL,
      before_snapshot_json TEXT,
      after_snapshot_json TEXT,
      source_url TEXT NOT NULL,
      is_verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      verified_by TEXT,
      detected_at TEXT NOT NULL
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS alerts (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      change_id INTEGER REFERENCES change_history(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      source_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES monitoring_sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      notification_id INTEGER REFERENCES recruitment_notifications(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      title TEXT NOT NULL,
      message TEXT NOT NULL,
      alert_type TEXT NOT NULL,
      severity TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'info',
      is_read INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      is_verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      created_at TEXT NOT NULL
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS saved_notifications (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      notification_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES recruitment_notifications(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      notes TEXT,
      saved_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      UNIQUE(user_id, notification_id)
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scrape_runs (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      run_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
      source_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES monitoring_sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      trigger_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'manual',
      mode_used TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'fixture',
      status TEXT NOT NULL,
      http_status INTEGER,
      parsed_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      new_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      updated_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      duplicate_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      removed_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      error_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      duration_ms INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      error_message TEXT,
      started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      completed_at TEXT NOT NULL
    );
  `);

  // Safe additive migration for pre-existing databases
  const chgCols = db.prepare('PRAGMA table_info(change_history)').all() as { name: string }[];
  const existingColNames = new Set(chgCols.map((c) => c.name));
  for (const [col, def] of [
    ['changed_field', 'TEXT'],
    ['change_label', 'TEXT'],
    ['previous_value', 'TEXT'],
    ['new_value', 'TEXT'],
  ] as const) {
    if (!existingColNames.has(col)) {
      db.exec(`ALTER TABLE change_history ADD COLUMN ${col} ${def};`);
    }
  }
}

import { Router } from 'express';

export const schedulerState = {
  running: true,
  paused: false,
  interval_minutes: Number(process.env.SCHEDULER_INTERVAL_MINUTES || 360),
  next_run_at: new Date(Date.now() + 360 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),

function serializeSource(row: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return {
    id: Number(row.id),
    code: String(row.code),
    name: String(row.name),
    organization_name: String(row.organization_name),
    base_url: String(row.base_url),
    parser_key: String(row.parser_key),
    fixture_filename: String(row.fixture_filename),
    scrape_mode: String(row.scrape_mode),
    crawl_interval_minutes: Number(row.crawl_interval_minutes),
    rate_limit_seconds: Number(row.rate_limit_seconds),
    robots_policy: String(row.robots_policy),
    permission_note: String(row.permission_note),
    is_active: Boolean(row.is_active),
    is_demo_data: Boolean(row.is_demo_data),
    freshness_status: String(row.freshness_status),
    last_attempted_at: row.last_attempted_at ? String(row.last_attempted_at) : null,
    last_successful_at: row.last_successful_at ? String(row.last_successful_at) : null,
    last_http_status: row.last_http_status !== null && row.last_http_status !== undefined ? Number(row.last_http_status) : null,
    last_error: row.last_error ? String(row.last_error) : null,
    last_parsed_count: Number(row.last_parsed_count || 0),
    last_duplicate_count: Number(row.last_duplicate_count || 0),
  };
}

function serializeNotification(
  row: Record<string, unknown>,
  savedIds: Set<number>,
  sourceCode: string
) {
  const id = Number(row.id);
  return {
    id,
    source_id: Number(row.source_id),
    source_code: sourceCode,
    entity_key: String(row.entity_key),
    content_hash: String(row.content_hash),
    title: String(row.title),
    organization_name: String(row.organization_name),
    notification_number: row.notification_number ? String(row.notification_number) : null,
    vacancy_count: row.vacancy_count ? String(row.vacancy_count) : null,
    job_category: row.job_category ? String(row.job_category) : null,
    application_start_date: row.application_start_date ? String(row.application_start_date) : null,
    application_deadline: row.application_deadline ? String(row.application_deadline) : null,
    exam_date: row.exam_date ? String(row.exam_date) : null,
    admit_card_date: row.admit_card_date ? String(row.admit_card_date) : null,
    result_date: row.result_date ? String(row.result_date) : null,
    description: row.description ? String(row.description) : null,
    pdf_url: row.pdf_url ? String(row.pdf_url) : null,
    source_url: String(row.source_url),
    published_date: row.published_date ? String(row.published_date) : null,
    lifecycle_status: String(row.lifecycle_status),
    is_removed: Boolean(row.is_removed),
    is_demo_data: Boolean(row.is_demo_data),
    first_seen_at: String(row.first_seen_at),
    last_seen_at: String(row.last_seen_at),
    source_updated_at: row.source_updated_at ? String(row.source_updated_at) : null,
    last_run_id: row.last_run_id ? String(row.last_run_id) : null,
    is_saved: savedIds.has(id),
  };
}

    source_url: String(row.source_url),
    published_date: row.published_date ? String(row.published_date) : null,
    notification_status: String(row.notification_status || 'active'),
    content_hash: String(row.content_hash),
    run_id: row.run_id ? String(row.run_id) : null,
    captured_at: String(row.captured_at),
  };
}

function serializeChange(
  row: Record<string, unknown>,
  sourceInfo?: { code: string; name: string; organization_name: string }
) {
  const changedFields = JSON.parse(String(row.changed_fields_json || '[]')) as {
    field?: string;
    old_value?: string | null;
    new_value?: string | null;
  }[];
  const firstDiff = changedFields[0] || {};
  return {
    id: Number(row.id),
    run_id: String(row.run_id),
    source_id: Number(row.source_id),
    source_code: sourceInfo?.code || '',
    source_name: sourceInfo?.name || '',
    organization_name: sourceInfo?.organization_name || '',
    notification_id: row.notification_id !== null ? Number(row.notification_id) : null,
    entity_key: String(row.entity_key),
    notification_title: String(row.notification_title),
    change_type: String(row.change_type),
    changed_field: row.changed_field ? String(row.changed_field) : firstDiff.field || null,
    change_label: row.change_label ? String(row.change_label) : null,
    previous_value:
      row.previous_value !== null && row.previous_value !== undefined
        ? String(row.previous_value)
        : firstDiff.old_value ?? null,
    new_value:
      row.new_value !== null && row.new_value !== undefined
        ? String(row.new_value)
        : firstDiff.new_value ?? null,
    changed_fields: changedFields,
    before_snapshot: row.before_snapshot_json ? JSON.parse(String(row.before_snapshot_json)) : null,
    after_snapshot: row.after_snapshot_json ? JSON.parse(String(row.after_snapshot_json)) : null,
    source_url: String(row.source_url),
    is_verified: Boolean(row.is_verified),
    verified_by: row.verified_by ? String(row.verified_by) : null,
    detected_at: String(row.detected_at),
  };
}

function getSourceInfoMap(): Map<number, { code: string; name: string; organization_name: string }> {
  const rows = db
    .prepare('SELECT id, code, name, organization_name FROM monitoring_sources')
    .all() as { id: number; code: string; name: string; organization_name: string }[];
  return new Map(
    rows.map((r) => [
      Number(r.id),
      { code: String(r.code), name: String(r.name), organization_name: String(r.organization_name) },
    ])
  );
}

function getSourceMap(): Map<number, string> {
  const rows = db.prepare('SELECT id, code FROM monitoring_sources').all() as { id: number; code: string }[];
  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [Number(r.id), String(r.code)]));
}

export const apiRouter = Router();

apiRouter.get('/health', (_req, res) => {
  res.json({ status: 'ok', service: 'GovTrack API' });
});

apiRouter.post('/auth/register', (req, res) => {
  const { full_name, email, password, target_exams } = req.body || {};
  if (!full_name || !email || !password || String(password).length < 6) {
    res.status(400).json({ detail: 'Full name, valid email, and password (min 6 chars) are required' });
    return;
  }
  const normalizedEmail = String(email).toLowerCase().trim();
  const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?').get(normalizedEmail);
  if (existing) {
    res.status(400).json({ detail: 'Email is already registered' });
    return;
  }
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const info = db
    .prepare(
      `INSERT INTO users (full_name, email, password_hash, role, target_exams, email_alerts_enabled, is_active, created_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'user', ?, 1, 1, ?)`
    )
    .run(String(full_name).trim(), normalizedEmail, hashPassword(String(password)), target_exams || 'UPSC, SSC, Banking', now);

  const userRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(Number(info.lastInsertRowid)) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const user = mapUserRow(userRow);
  const token = createAccessToken(user);
  res.status(201).json({ access_token: token, token_type: 'bearer', user });
});

apiRouter.post('/auth/login', (req, res) => {
  const { email, password } = req.body || {};
  const normalizedEmail = String(email || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const row = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?').get(normalizedEmail) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  if (!row || !verifyPassword(String(password || ''), String(row.password_hash))) {
    res.status(401).json({ detail: 'Invalid email or password' });
    return;
  }
  if (!row.is_active) {
    res.status(403).json({ detail: 'Account is deactivated' });
    return;
  }
  const user = mapUserRow(row);
  const token = createAccessToken(user);
  res.json({ access_token: token, token_type: 'bearer', user });
});

apiRouter.get('/auth/me', requireAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  res.json({ user: req.user });
});

apiRouter.put('/profile', requireAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const { full_name, target_exams, email_alerts_enabled } = req.body || {};
  if (!full_name || String(full_name).trim().length < 2) {
    res.status(400).json({ detail: 'Full name is required' });
    return;
  }
  db.prepare('UPDATE users SET full_name = ?, target_exams = ?, email_alerts_enabled = ? WHERE id = ?').run(
    String(full_name).trim(),
    target_exams ?? null,
    email_alerts_enabled ? 1 : 0,
    req.user!.id
  );
  const updated = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(req.user!.id) as Record<string, unknown>;
  res.json({ user: mapUserRow(updated) });
});

apiRouter.put('/profile/password', requireAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const { current_password, new_password } = req.body || {};
  const row = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(req.user!.id) as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!verifyPassword(String(current_password || ''), String(row.password_hash))) {
    res.status(400).json({ detail: 'Current password is incorrect' });
    return;
  }
  if (!new_password || String(new_password).length < 6) {
    res.status(400).json({ detail: 'New password must be at least 6 characters' });
    return;
  }
  db.prepare('UPDATE users SET password_hash = ? WHERE id = ?').run(hashPassword(String(new_password)), req.user!.id);
  res.json({ message: 'Password updated successfully' });
});

apiRouter.get('/dashboard', requireAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const sources = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources ORDER BY id ASC').all() as Record<string, unknown>[];
  const sourceMap = new Map(sources.map((s) => [Number(s.id), String(s.code)]));

  const activeCount = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM recruitment_notifications WHERE is_removed = 0').get() as { c: number }).c;
  const unreadAlerts = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM alerts WHERE is_read = 0').get() as { c: number }).c;
  const totalChanges = (
    db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM change_history WHERE change_type IN ('UPDATED', 'REMOVED')").get() as { c: number }
  ).c;
  const healthySources = sources.filter((s) => s.freshness_status === 'Fresh').length;

  const savedRows = db
    .prepare('SELECT notification_id FROM saved_notifications WHERE user_id = ?')
    .all(req.user!.id) as { notification_id: number }[];
  const savedIds = new Set(savedRows.map((r) => Number(r.notification_id)));

  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const upcoming = db
    .prepare(
      `SELECT * FROM recruitment_notifications
       WHERE is_removed = 0 AND application_deadline IS NOT NULL AND application_deadline >= ?
       ORDER BY application_deadline ASC LIMIT 6`
    )
    .all(todayStr) as Record<string, unknown>[];

  const upcomingExams = db
    .prepare(
      `SELECT * FROM recruitment_notifications
       WHERE is_removed = 0 AND exam_date IS NOT NULL AND exam_date >= ?
       ORDER BY exam_date ASC LIMIT 6`
    )
    .all(todayStr) as Record<string, unknown>[];

  const recentChanges = db
    .prepare("SELECT * FROM change_history WHERE change_type IN ('UPDATED', 'REMOVED') ORDER BY detected_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 8")
    .all() as Record<string, unknown>[];

  const recentRuns = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM scrape_runs ORDER BY completed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 6')
    .all() as Record<string, unknown>[];

  const sourceInfoMap = new Map(
    sources.map((s) => [
      Number(s.id),
      {
        code: String(s.code),
        name: String(s.name),
        organization_name: String(s.organization_name),
      },
    ])
  );

  res.json({
    stats: {
      active_notifications: activeCount,
      monitored_sources: sources.length,
      healthy_sources: healthySources,
      unread_alerts: unreadAlerts,
      total_changes: totalChanges,
      saved_count: savedIds.size,
    },
    upcoming_deadlines: upcoming.map((n) => serializeNotification(n, savedIds, sourceMap.get(Number(n.source_id)) || '')),
    upcoming_exams: upcomingExams.map((n) => serializeNotification(n, savedIds, sourceMap.get(Number(n.source_id)) || '')),
    recent_changes: recentChanges.map((c) => serializeChange(c, sourceInfoMap.get(Number(c.source_id)))),
    sources: sources.map(serializeSource),
    recent_runs: recentRuns.map((r) => ({
      run_id: String(r.run_id),
      source_code: sourceMap.get(Number(r.source_id)) || '',
      status: String(r.status),
      mode_used: String(r.mode_used),
      parsed_count: Number(r.parsed_count),
      new_count: Number(r.new_count),
      updated_count: Number(r.updated_count),
      duplicate_count: Number(r.duplicate_count),
      completed_at: String(r.completed_at),
    })),
  });
});

apiRouter.get('/notifications', optionalAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const sources = db.prepare('SELECT id, code FROM monitoring_sources').all() as { id: number; code: string }[];
  const sourceMap = new Map(sources.map((s) => [Number(s.id), String(s.code)]));
  const codeToId = new Map(sources.map((s) => [String(s.code), Number(s.id)]));

  const q = typeof req.query.q === 'string' ? req.query.q.trim() : '';
  const sourceCode = typeof req.query.source_code === 'string' ? req.query.source_code : '';
  const category = typeof req.query.category === 'string' ? req.query.category : '';
  const statusFilter = typeof req.query.status_filter === 'string' ? req.query.status_filter : '';
  const sort = typeof req.query.sort === 'string' ? req.query.sort : 'deadline';

  const conditions: string[] = [];
  const params: (string | number)[] = [];

  if (q) {
    conditions.push('(title LIKE ? OR organization_name LIKE ? OR notification_number LIKE ? OR description LIKE ?)');
    const like = `%${q}%`;
    params.push(like, like, like, like);
  }
  if (sourceCode && sourceCode !== 'all' && codeToId.has(sourceCode)) {
    conditions.push('source_id = ?');
    params.push(codeToId.get(sourceCode)!);
  }
  if (category && category !== 'all') {
    conditions.push('job_category = ?');
    params.push(category);
  }
  if (statusFilter && statusFilter !== 'all') {
    conditions.push('lifecycle_status = ?');
    params.push(statusFilter);
  }

  const whereSql = conditions.length > 0 ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}` : '';
  const orderSql =
    sort === 'newest'
      ? 'ORDER BY first_seen_at DESC, id DESC'
      : 'ORDER BY CASE WHEN application_deadline IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END, application_deadline ASC';

  const rows = db
    .prepare(`SELECT * FROM recruitment_notifications ${whereSql} ${orderSql}`)
    .all(...params) as Record<string, unknown>[];

  const savedIds = new Set<number>();
  if (req.user) {
    const savedRows = db
      .prepare('SELECT notification_id FROM saved_notifications WHERE user_id = ?')
      .all(req.user.id) as { notification_id: number }[];
    for (const s of savedRows) savedIds.add(Number(s.notification_id));
  }

  const catRows = db
    .prepare('SELECT DISTINCT job_category FROM recruitment_notifications WHERE job_category IS NOT NULL')
    .all() as { job_category: string }[];

  res.json({
    items: rows.map((n) => serializeNotification(n, savedIds, sourceMap.get(Number(n.source_id)) || '')),
    categories: catRows.map((c) => c.job_category).sort(),
  });
});

apiRouter.get('/notifications/:id', optionalAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const notifId = Number(req.params.id);
  const notif = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM recruitment_notifications WHERE id = ?').get(notifId) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  if (!notif) {
    res.status(404).json({ detail: 'Notification not found' });
    return;
  }
  const source = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE id = ?').get(Number(notif.source_id)) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  const changes = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM change_history WHERE notification_id = ? ORDER BY detected_at DESC, id DESC')
    .all(notifId) as Record<string, unknown>[];

  const versions = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM notification_versions WHERE notification_id = ? ORDER BY version_number DESC')
    .all(notifId) as Record<string, unknown>[];

  const savedIds = new Set<number>();
  if (req.user) {
    const saved = db
      .prepare('SELECT id FROM saved_notifications WHERE user_id = ? AND notification_id = ?')
      .get(req.user.id, notifId);
    if (saved) savedIds.add(notifId);
  }

  const srcCode = source ? String(source.code) : '';
  const srcInfo = source
    ? {
        code: srcCode,
        name: String(source.name),
        organization_name: String(source.organization_name),
      }
    : undefined;

  res.json({
    notification: serializeNotification(notif, savedIds, srcCode),
    source: source ? serializeSource(source) : null,
    changes: changes.map((c) => serializeChange(c, srcInfo)),
    versions: versions.map(serializeVersion),
  });
});

apiRouter.get('/notifications/:id/pdf', (req, res) => {
  const notifId = Number(req.params.id);
  const notif = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM recruitment_notifications WHERE id = ?').get(notifId) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  if (!notif) {
    res.status(404).json({ detail: 'Notification not found' });
    return;
  }
  const pdfBuffer = buildNotificationPdf(notif);
  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `inline; filename="govtrack-notification-${notifId}.pdf"`);
  res.send(pdfBuffer);
});

apiRouter.get('/changes', (req, res) => {
  const sourceInfoMap = getSourceInfoMap();
  const sources = db.prepare('SELECT id, code FROM monitoring_sources').all() as { id: number; code: string }[];
  const codeToId = new Map(sources.map((s) => [String(s.code), Number(s.id)]));

  const changeType = typeof req.query.change_type === 'string' ? req.query.change_type : '';
  const sourceCode = typeof req.query.source_code === 'string' ? req.query.source_code : '';
  const changedField = typeof req.query.changed_field === 'string' ? req.query.changed_field : '';
  const q = typeof req.query.q === 'string' ? req.query.q.trim() : '';

  const conditions: string[] = [];
  const params: (string | number)[] = [];

  if (changeType === 'UPDATES_ONLY') {
    conditions.push("change_type IN ('UPDATED', 'REMOVED')");
  } else if (changeType && changeType !== 'all') {
    conditions.push('change_type = ?');
    params.push(changeType.toUpperCase());
  }
  if (sourceCode && sourceCode !== 'all' && codeToId.has(sourceCode)) {
    conditions.push('source_id = ?');
    params.push(codeToId.get(sourceCode)!);
  }
  if (changedField && changedField !== 'all') {
    conditions.push('changed_field = ?');
    params.push(changedField);
  }
  if (q) {
    conditions.push(
      '(notification_title LIKE ? OR change_label LIKE ? OR previous_value LIKE ? OR new_value LIKE ? OR entity_key LIKE ?)'
    );
    const like = `%${q}%`;
    params.push(like, like, like, like, like);
  }

  const whereSql = conditions.length > 0 ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}` : '';
  const rows = db
    .prepare(`SELECT * FROM change_history ${whereSql} ORDER BY detected_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 250`)
    .all(...params) as Record<string, unknown>[];

  res.json({
    items: rows.map((c) => serializeChange(c, sourceInfoMap.get(Number(c.source_id)))),
  });
});

apiRouter.post('/changes/:id/verify', requireAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const changeId = Number(req.params.id);
  const change = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM change_history WHERE id = ?').get(changeId);
  if (!change) {
    res.status(404).json({ detail: 'Change record not found' });
    return;
  }
  db.prepare('UPDATE change_history SET is_verified = 1, verified_by = ? WHERE id = ?').run(
    req.user!.full_name,
    changeId
  );
  db.prepare('UPDATE alerts SET is_verified = 1, is_read = 1 WHERE change_id = ?').run(changeId);
  res.json({ message: 'Change verified', verified_by: req.user!.full_name });
});

apiRouter.post('/sources/scrape-all-live', requireAuth, async (_req, res) => {
  const sources = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE is_active = 1 ORDER BY id ASC').all() as Record<string, unknown>[];
  const runs = await Promise.all(
    sources.map(async (src) => {
      const run = await runSourceScrape(src, { triggerType: 'manual', modeOverride: 'auto' });
      return {
        source_code: String(src.code),
        run_id: String(run.run_id),
        status: String(run.status),
        mode_used: String(run.mode_used),
        parsed_count: Number(run.parsed_count),
        new_count: Number(run.new_count),
        updated_count: Number(run.updated_count),
        duplicate_count: Number(run.duplicate_count),
      };
    })
  );
  res.json({ runs });
});

apiRouter.post('/sources', requireAdmin, (req, res) => {
  const body = req.body || {};
  if (!body.code || !body.name || !body.base_url || !body.parser_key) {
    res.status(400).json({ detail: 'code, name, base_url, and parser_key are required' });
    return;
  }
  const code = String(body.code).toLowerCase().trim();
  if (db.prepare('SELECT id FROM monitoring_sources WHERE code = ?').get(code)) {
    res.status(400).json({ detail: 'Source code already exists' });
    return;
  }
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const info = db
    .prepare(
      `INSERT INTO monitoring_sources (
        code, name, organization_name, base_url, parser_key, fixture_filename,
        scrape_mode, crawl_interval_minutes, rate_limit_seconds, robots_policy,
        permission_note, is_active, is_demo_data, freshness_status, created_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 1, 'Pending', ?)`
    )
    .run(
      code,
      String(body.name).trim(),
      String(body.organization_name || body.name).trim(),
      String(body.base_url).trim(),
      String(body.parser_key).trim(),
      String(body.fixture_filename || `${code}.html`).trim(),
      String(body.scrape_mode || 'fixture'),
      Number(body.crawl_interval_minutes || 360),
      Number(body.rate_limit_seconds || 2),
      String(body.robots_policy || 'Public non-sensitive data only.'),
      String(body.permission_note || 'Admin approved source.'),
      now
    );

  const created = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE id = ?').get(Number(info.lastInsertRowid)) as Record<string, unknown>;
  res.status(201).json({ source: serializeSource(created) });
});

apiRouter.patch('/sources/:id', requireAdmin, (req, res) => {
  const sourceId = Number(req.params.id);
  const src = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE id = ?').get(sourceId);
  if (!src) {
    res.status(404).json({ detail: 'Source not found' });
    return;
  }
  const body = req.body || {};
  if (body.scrape_mode !== undefined) {
    db.prepare('UPDATE monitoring_sources SET scrape_mode = ? WHERE id = ?').run(String(body.scrape_mode), sourceId);
  }
  if (body.crawl_interval_minutes !== undefined) {
    db.prepare('UPDATE monitoring_sources SET crawl_interval_minutes = ? WHERE id = ?').run(
      Number(body.crawl_interval_minutes),
      sourceId
    );
  }
  if (body.is_active !== undefined) {
    db.prepare('UPDATE monitoring_sources SET is_active = ? WHERE id = ?').run(body.is_active ? 1 : 0, sourceId);
  }
  const updated = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE id = ?').get(sourceId) as Record<string, unknown>;
  res.json({ source: serializeSource(updated) });
});

apiRouter.post('/sources/:id/scrape', requireAuth, async (req, res) => {
  const sourceId = Number(req.params.id);
  const src = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE id = ?').get(sourceId) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  if (!src) {
    res.status(404).json({ detail: 'Source not found' });
    return;
  }
  const body = req.body || {};
  const triggerType = body.fixture_override ? 'fixture_replay' : 'manual';
  const run = await runSourceScrape(src, {
    triggerType,
    modeOverride: body.mode_override || null,
    fixtureOverride: body.fixture_override || null,
    simulateHttpStatus: body.simulate_http_status ? Number(body.simulate_http_status) : null,
  });
  const updatedSrc = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE id = ?').get(sourceId) as Record<string, unknown>;
  res.json({
    source: serializeSource(updatedSrc),
    run: {
      run_id: String(run.run_id),
      status: String(run.status),
      mode_used: String(run.mode_used),
      http_status: run.http_status !== null ? Number(run.http_status) : null,
      parsed_count: Number(run.parsed_count),
      new_count: Number(run.new_count),
      updated_count: Number(run.updated_count),
      duplicate_count: Number(run.duplicate_count),
      removed_count: Number(run.removed_count),
      error_message: run.error_message ? String(run.error_message) : null,
      duration_ms: Number(run.duration_ms),
    },
  });
});

apiRouter.get('/alerts', (req, res) => {
  const sourceMap = getSourceMap();
  const unreadOnly = req.query.unread_only === 'true';
  const sql = unreadOnly
    ? 'SELECT * FROM alerts WHERE is_read = 0 ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 150'
    : 'SELECT * FROM alerts ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 150';
  const rows = db.prepare(sql).all() as Record<string, unknown>[];
  res.json({
    items: rows.map((a) => ({
      id: Number(a.id),
      change_id: a.change_id !== null ? Number(a.change_id) : null,
      source_id: Number(a.source_id),
      source_code: sourceMap.get(Number(a.source_id)) || '',
      notification_id: a.notification_id !== null ? Number(a.notification_id) : null,
      title: String(a.title),
      message: String(a.message),
      alert_type: String(a.alert_type),
      severity: String(a.severity),
      is_read: Boolean(a.is_read),
      is_verified: Boolean(a.is_verified),
      created_at: String(a.created_at),
    })),
  });
});

apiRouter.get('/saved', requireAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const sourceMap = getSourceMap();
  const savedRows = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM saved_notifications WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY saved_at DESC')
    .all(req.user!.id) as Record<string, unknown>[];

  const items: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  for (const s of savedRows) {
    const notif = db
      .prepare('SELECT * FROM recruitment_notifications WHERE id = ?')
      .get(Number(s.notification_id)) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    if (notif) {
      items.push({
        ...serializeNotification(notif, new Set([Number(notif.id)]), sourceMap.get(Number(notif.source_id)) || ''),
        saved_notes: s.notes ? String(s.notes) : null,
        saved_at: String(s.saved_at),
      });
    }
  }
  res.json({ items });
});

apiRouter.post('/saved/:notificationId', requireAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const notifId = Number(req.params.notificationId);
  const notif = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM recruitment_notifications WHERE id = ?').get(notifId);
  if (!notif) {
    res.status(404).json({ detail: 'Notification not found' });
    return;
  }
  const notes = req.body?.notes ? String(req.body.notes) : '';
  const existing = db
    .prepare('SELECT id FROM saved_notifications WHERE user_id = ? AND notification_id = ?')
    .get(req.user!.id, notifId);
  if (existing) {
    db.prepare('UPDATE saved_notifications SET notes = ? WHERE user_id = ? AND notification_id = ?').run(
      notes,
      req.user!.id,
      notifId
    );
  } else {
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO saved_notifications (user_id, notification_id, notes, saved_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
      req.user!.id,
      notifId,
      notes,
      new Date().toISOString()
    );
  }
  res.json({ saved: true });
});

apiRouter.delete('/saved/:notificationId', requireAuth, (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
  const notifId = Number(req.params.notificationId);
  db.prepare('DELETE FROM saved_notifications WHERE user_id = ? AND notification_id = ?').run(req.user!.id, notifId);
  res.json({ saved: false });
});

apiRouter.patch('/admin/users/:userId', requireAdmin, (req, res) => {
  const userId = Number(req.params.userId);
  const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(userId);
  if (!existing) {
    res.status(404).json({ detail: 'User not found' });
    return;
  }
  const { role, is_active } = req.body || {};
  if (role === 'user' || role === 'admin') {
    db.prepare('UPDATE users SET role = ? WHERE id = ?').run(role, userId);
  }
  if (is_active !== undefined) {
    db.prepare('UPDATE users SET is_active = ? WHERE id = ?').run(is_active ? 1 : 0, userId);
  }
  const updated = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(userId) as Record<string, unknown>;
  res.json({ user: mapUserRow(updated) });
});

apiRouter.get('/admin/scheduler', requireAdmin, (_req, res) => {
  res.json(schedulerState);
});

apiRouter.put('/admin/scheduler', requireAdmin, (req, res) => {
  const { paused, interval_minutes } = req.body || {};
  if (paused !== undefined) schedulerState.paused = Boolean(paused);
  if (interval_minutes !== undefined && Number(interval_minutes) >= 5) {
    schedulerState.interval_minutes = Number(interval_minutes);
    schedulerState.next_run_at = new Date(Date.now() + schedulerState.interval_minutes * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
  }
  res.json(schedulerState);
});

apiRouter.post('/admin/scrape-all', requireAdmin, async (_req, res) => {
  const sources = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE is_active = 1 ORDER BY id ASC').all() as Record<string, unknown>[];
  const runs = [];
  for (const src of sources) {
    const run = await runSourceScrape(src, { triggerType: 'manual' });
    runs.push({ source_code: String(src.code), run_id: String(run.run_id), status: String(run.status) });
  }
  res.json({ runs });
});

===== FILE: server/scraperEngine.ts =====

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { db } from './db.ts';
import { applyScrapeResults, recordFailedScrape } from './changeDetector.ts';
import {
  extractIbpsSubpageDates,
  getParser,
  parseRbiDetailHtml,
  parseUpscDetailHtml,
} from './scrapers/index.ts';
import { enrichNotificationFromPdf } from './pdfExtractor.ts';
import type { ParsedNotification } from './scrapers/base.ts';

const FIXTURES_DIR = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'backend', 'fixtures');
const USER_AGENT =
  process.env.SCRAPER_USER_AGENT ||
  'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/126.0.0.0 Safari/537.36';
const CAPTCHA_MARKERS = ['captcha', 'cf-challenge', 'g-recaptcha', 'hcaptcha', 'access denied'];

async function fetchHtml(url: string, timeoutMs = 24000): Promise<{ status: number; text: string }> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(url, {
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': USER_AGENT,
        Accept: 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8',
      },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    const text = resp.status === 200 ? await resp.text() : '';
    return { status: resp.status, text };
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

interface SscApiRecord {
  id?: string;
  headline?: string;
  examId?: string;
  examYear?: string;
  desc?: string;
  content?: string;
  startDate?: string | null;
  endDate?: string | null;
  createdAt?: string | null;
  redirectUrl?: string | null;
  attachments?: { path?: string; fileName?: string; type?: string }[];
}

async function fetchSscLiveItems(): Promise<ParsedNotification[]> {
  const headers = {
    'User-Agent': USER_AGENT,
    Accept: 'application/json',
    Referer: 'https://ssc.gov.in/',
  };
  const noticeUrl =
    'https://ssc.gov.in/api/general-website/portal/notice-boards?page=1&limit=10&contentType=notice-boards&key=createdAt&order=DESC&isPaginationRequired=true&isAttachment=true&language=english&attributes=id,headline,examId,contentType,redirectUrl,createdAt';
  const calendarUrl =
    'https://ssc.gov.in/api/general-website/portal/ssc-calendar?page=1&limit=8&contentType=ssc-calendar&key=createdAt&order=DESC&isPaginationRequired=false&isAttachment=true&language=english&attributes=id,headline,examId,examYear,desc,content,contentType,startDate,endDate,language,createdAt';

  const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();

  const safeFetchJson = async (url: string): Promise<SscApiRecord[]> => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 14000);
    try {
      const resp = await fetch(url, { headers, signal: controller.signal });
      if (!resp.ok) return [];
      const payload = (await resp.json()) as { statusCode?: string; data?: SscApiRecord[] };
      return Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  };

  const [notices, calendar] = await Promise.all([safeFetchJson(noticeUrl), safeFetchJson(calendarUrl)]);

  for (const row of notices) {
    const title = row.headline ? row.headline.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : '';
    if (!title || seen.has(title.toLowerCase())) continue;
    seen.add(title.toLowerCase());

    const att = row.attachments?.find((a) => a.path && /\.pdf$/i.test(a.path)) || row.attachments?.[0];
    const cleanPath = att?.path ? att.path.replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/^\/+/, '') : null;
    const pdfUrl = cleanPath ? `https://ssc.gov.in/api/attachment/${encodeURI(cleanPath)}` : null;
    const pubDate = row.createdAt ? row.createdAt.slice(0, 10) : null;
    const startDate = row.startDate ? row.startDate.slice(0, 10) : null;
    const deadline = row.endDate ? row.endDate.slice(0, 10) : null;
    const isAdmit = /admit|admission\s*certificate|city\s*of\s*examination/i.test(title);
    const isResult = /result|answer\s*key|marks|cut-?off/i.test(title);

    results.push({
      title,
      organization_name: 'Staff Selection Commission (SSC)',
      notification_number: row.examId ? `SSC-${row.examId}` : null,
      vacancy_count: null,
      job_category: isAdmit ? 'Admit Card / City Intimation' : isResult ? 'Result / Answer Key' : 'SSC Notice Board',
      application_start_date: startDate,
      application_deadline: deadline,
      exam_date: null,
      admit_card_date: isAdmit ? pubDate : null,
      result_date: isResult ? pubDate : null,
      description: pubDate ? `Official SSC Notice Board update released on ${pubDate}.` : null,
      pdf_url: pdfUrl,
      source_url: 'https://ssc.gov.in/',
      published_date: pubDate,
    });
  }

  for (const row of calendar) {
    const title = row.headline ? row.headline.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : '';
    if (!title || seen.has(title.toLowerCase())) continue;
    seen.add(title.toLowerCase());

    const startDate = row.startDate ? row.startDate.slice(0, 10) : null;
    const deadline = row.endDate ? row.endDate.slice(0, 10) : null;
    const pubDate = row.createdAt ? row.createdAt.slice(0, 10) : null;
    const descParts = [row.desc, row.content ? `Exam Month: ${row.content}` : null].filter(Boolean);

    results.push({
      title,
      organization_name: 'Staff Selection Commission (SSC)',
      notification_number: row.examId ? `SSC-CAL-${row.examId}` : null,
      vacancy_count: null,
      job_category: 'SSC Examination Calendar',
      application_start_date: startDate,
      application_deadline: deadline,
      exam_date: row.content ? row.content.trim() : null,
      admit_card_date: null,
      result_date: null,
      description: descParts.length > 0 ? descParts.join(' · ') : null,
      pdf_url: null,
      source_url: 'https://ssc.gov.in/',
      published_date: pubDate,
    });
  }

  return results.slice(0, 14);
}

async function checkRobotsAllowed(baseUrl: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(baseUrl);
    const robotsUrl = `${parsed.protocol}//${parsed.host}/robots.txt`;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3500);
    const resp = await fetch(robotsUrl, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': USER_AGENT },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if (!resp.ok) return true;
    const text = await resp.text();
    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);
    let inStar = false;
    for (const rawLine of lines) {
      const line = rawLine.split('#')[0].trim();
      if (!line) continue;
      if (/^user-agent:\s*\*/i.test(line)) {
        inStar = true;
      } else if (/^user-agent:/i.test(line)) {
        inStar = false;
      } else if (inStar && /^disallow:\s*\/\s*$/i.test(line)) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  } catch {
    return true;
  }
}

async function enrichLiveItems(
  parserKey: string,
  baseUrl: string,
  items: ParsedNotification[]
): Promise<ParsedNotification[]> {
  if (items.length === 0) return items;

  if (parserKey === 'upsc') {
    const enriched = [...items];
    const batch = enriched.slice(0, 10);
    const updatedBatch = await Promise.all(
      batch.map(async (item) => {
        if (!item.source_url || item.source_url === baseUrl || !item.source_url.includes('upsc.gov.in/active-examinations/')) {
          return item;
        }
        try {
          const { status, text } = await fetchHtml(item.source_url, 9000);
          if (status === 200 && text) {
            return parseUpscDetailHtml(item, text);
          }
        } catch {
          // Keep listing fields intact if detail page times out
        }
        return item;
      })
    );
    for (let i = 0; i < updatedBatch.length; i += 1) {
      enriched[i] = updatedBatch[i];
    }
    return enriched;
  }

  if (parserKey === 'rbi') {
    const enriched = [...items];
    const batch = enriched.slice(0, 6);
    const updatedBatch = await Promise.all(
      batch.map(async (item) => {
        if (!item.source_url || !item.source_url.includes('bs_viewcontent.aspx')) {
          return item;
        }
        try {
          const { status, text } = await fetchHtml(item.source_url, 9000);
          if (status === 200 && text) {
            return parseRbiDetailHtml(item, text, item.source_url);
          }
        } catch {
          // Keep base row
        }
        return item;
      })
    );
    for (let i = 0; i < updatedBatch.length; i += 1) {
      enriched[i] = updatedBatch[i];
    }
    return enriched;
  }

  if (parserKey === 'ibps') {
    const subUrls = Array.from(
      new Set(
        items
          .map((it) => it.source_url)
          .filter((u) => u && u !== baseUrl && u.includes('ibps.in/index.php/') && !u.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf'))
      )
    ).slice(0, 5);

    await Promise.all(
      subUrls.map(async (subUrl) => {
        try {
          const { status, text } = await fetchHtml(subUrl, 9000);
          if (status === 200 && text) {
            extractIbpsSubpageDates(items, text, subUrl);
          }
        } catch {
          // Non-fatal
        }
      })
    );
  }

  // Extract additional labeled dates & vacancy counts from linked official PDFs (top 8 items missing key fields)
  const pdfTargets = items.slice(0, 8);
  const pdfEnriched = await Promise.all(pdfTargets.map((item) => enrichNotificationFromPdf(item)));
  for (let i = 0; i < pdfEnriched.length; i += 1) {
    items[i] = pdfEnriched[i];
  }

  return items;
}

export async function runSourceScrape(
  source: Record<string, unknown>,
  options: {
    triggerType?: string;
    modeOverride?: string | null;
    fixtureOverride?: string | null;
    simulateHttpStatus?: number | null;
    suppressHistory?: boolean;
  } = {}
): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const startMs = Date.now();
  const runId = `RUN-${crypto.randomBytes(5).toString('hex').toUpperCase()}`;
  const triggerType = options.triggerType || 'manual';
  const effectiveMode = options.modeOverride || String(source.scrape_mode || 'auto');
  const sourceId = Number(source.id);

  if (options.simulateHttpStatus && options.simulateHttpStatus >= 400) {
    return recordFailedScrape(
      source,
      runId,
      triggerType,
      'simulated_error',
      options.simulateHttpStatus,
      `Simulated HTTP ${options.simulateHttpStatus} error from source endpoint.`,
      Date.now() - startMs,
      startedAt
    );
  }

  let htmlContent: string | null = null;
  let httpStatus = 200;
  let isDemoData = true;
  let modeUsed = 'fixture';

  if (options.fixtureOverride) {
    const safeName = path.basename(options.fixtureOverride);
    const fixturePath = path.join(FIXTURES_DIR, safeName);
    if (!fs.existsSync(fixturePath)) {
      return recordFailedScrape(
        source,
        runId,
        triggerType,
        'fixture',
        404,
        `Fixture not found: ${safeName}`,
        Date.now() - startMs,
        startedAt
      );
    }
    if (safeName.includes('404')) {
      return recordFailedScrape(
        source,
        runId,
        triggerType,
        'fixture',
        404,
        `HTTP 404 Not Found replayed from ${safeName}`,
        Date.now() - startMs,
        startedAt
      );
    }
    htmlContent = fs.readFileSync(fixturePath, 'utf-8');
    modeUsed = 'fixture';
    isDemoData = true;
  } else if (effectiveMode === 'live' || effectiveMode === 'auto') {
    const allowed = await checkRobotsAllowed(String(source.base_url));
    if (!allowed) {
      return recordFailedScrape(
        source,
        runId,
        triggerType,
        'live',
        403,
        'Disallowed by robots.txt policy.',
        Date.now() - startMs,
        startedAt
      );
    }

    let liveError: string | null = null;
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt += 1) {
      try {
        const resp = await fetchHtml(String(source.base_url), 24000);
        httpStatus = resp.status;
        if (httpStatus === 403 || httpStatus === 429) {
          liveError = `HTTP ${httpStatus} received; stopped immediately per respectful crawling policy.`;
          break;
        }
        if (httpStatus >= 500) {
          liveError = `HTTP ${httpStatus} transient server error.`;
          continue;
        }
        if (httpStatus === 200) {
          const text = resp.text;
          const lower = text.slice(0, 4000).toLowerCase();
          if (CAPTCHA_MARKERS.some((m) => lower.includes(m))) {
            liveError = 'Anti-bot / CAPTCHA challenge detected; never bypassing.';
            httpStatus = 403;
            break;
          }
          htmlContent = text;
          modeUsed = 'live';
          isDemoData = false;
          break;
        }
        liveError = `Unexpected HTTP ${httpStatus}.`;
        break;
      } catch (err) {
        liveError = `Network error: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message.slice(0, 140) : 'Timeout'}`;
      }
    }

    if (!htmlContent) {
      if (effectiveMode === 'live') {
        return recordFailedScrape(
          source,
          runId,
          triggerType,
          'live',
          httpStatus || 503,
          liveError || 'Live fetch failed',
          Date.now() - startMs,
          startedAt
        );
      }
      const fallbackPath = path.join(FIXTURES_DIR, String(source.fixture_filename));
      if (fs.existsSync(fallbackPath)) {
        htmlContent = fs.readFileSync(fallbackPath, 'utf-8');
        modeUsed = 'fixture';
        isDemoData = true;
        httpStatus = 200;
      } else {
        return recordFailedScrape(
          source,
          runId,
          triggerType,
          'auto',
          httpStatus || 503,
          liveError || 'Fetch and fixture fallback failed',
          Date.now() - startMs,
          startedAt
        );
      }
    }
  } else {
    const fixturePath = path.join(FIXTURES_DIR, String(source.fixture_filename));
    if (!fs.existsSync(fixturePath)) {
      return recordFailedScrape(
        source,
        runId,
        triggerType,
        'fixture',
        404,
        `Missing fixture ${source.fixture_filename}`,
        Date.now() - startMs,
        startedAt
      );
    }
    htmlContent = fs.readFileSync(fixturePath, 'utf-8');
    modeUsed = 'fixture';
    isDemoData = true;
  }

  const parserKey = String(source.parser_key);
  const parser = getParser(parserKey);
  let parsedItems =
    modeUsed === 'live' && parserKey === 'ssc'
      ? await fetchSscLiveItems()
      : parser.parse(htmlContent, String(source.base_url));

  if (modeUsed === 'live' && parsedItems.length === 0 && parserKey !== 'ssc') {
    parsedItems = parser.parse(htmlContent, String(source.base_url));
  }

  if (modeUsed === 'live' && parsedItems.length > 0) {
    parsedItems = await enrichLiveItems(parserKey, String(source.base_url), parsedItems);
  }

  if (parsedItems.length === 0 && modeUsed === 'live' && effectiveMode === 'auto') {
    const fallbackPath = path.join(FIXTURES_DIR, String(source.fixture_filename));
    if (fs.existsSync(fallbackPath)) {
      htmlContent = fs.readFileSync(fallbackPath, 'utf-8');
      parsedItems = parser.parse(htmlContent, String(source.base_url));
      modeUsed = 'fixture';
      isDemoData = true;
    }
  }

  const durationMs = Date.now() - startMs;
  if (parsedItems.length === 0) {
    return recordFailedScrape(
      source,
      runId,
      triggerType,
      modeUsed,
      httpStatus,
      'Parser returned 0 records (selector/schema mismatch guard). Existing data preserved.',
      durationMs,
      startedAt
    );
  }

  const counts = applyScrapeResults(
    source,
    parsedItems,
    runId,
    isDemoData,
    true,
    2,
    Boolean(options.suppressHistory)
  );
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  db.prepare(
    `UPDATE monitoring_sources SET
      last_attempted_at = ?, last_successful_at = ?, last_http_status = ?,
      last_error = NULL, is_demo_data = ?, freshness_status = 'Fresh',
      last_parsed_count = ?, last_duplicate_count = ?
    WHERE id = ?`
  ).run(
    now,
    now,
    httpStatus,
    isDemoData ? 1 : 0,
    counts.parsed_count,
    counts.duplicate_count,
    sourceId
  );

  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO scrape_runs (
      run_id, source_id, trigger_type, mode_used, status, http_status,
      parsed_count, new_count, updated_count, duplicate_count, removed_count,
      error_count, duration_ms, error_message, started_at, completed_at
    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'success', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, NULL, ?, ?)`
  ).run(
    runId,
    sourceId,
    triggerType,
    modeUsed,
    httpStatus,
    counts.parsed_count,
    counts.new_count,
    counts.updated_count,
    counts.duplicate_count,
    counts.removed_count,
    durationMs,
    startedAt,
    now
  );

  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM scrape_runs WHERE run_id = ?').get(runId) as Record<string, unknown>;
}

===== FILE: server/scrapers/base.ts =====

export interface ParsedNotification {
  title: string;
  organization_name: string;
  notification_number: string | null;
  vacancy_count: string | null;
  job_category: string | null;
  application_start_date: string | null;
  application_deadline: string | null;
  exam_date: string | null;
  admit_card_date: string | null;
  result_date: string | null;
  description: string | null;
  pdf_url: string | null;
  source_url: string;
  published_date: string | null;
  notification_status?: string | null;
}

export function cleanText(value?: string | null): string | null {
  if (!value) return null;
  const cleaned = value.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  return cleaned.length > 0 ? cleaned : null;
}

export function resolveUrl(baseUrl: string, href?: string | null): string | null {
  const cleaned = cleanText(href);
  if (!cleaned) return null;
  try {
    return new URL(cleaned, baseUrl).toString();
  } catch {
    return cleaned;
  }
}

export interface PortalParser {
  parserKey: string;
  parse(html: string, sourceUrl?: string): ParsedNotification[];
}
