===== FILE: server/scrapers/index.ts =====

import * as cheerio from 'cheerio';
import { cleanText, resolveUrl, type ParsedNotification, type PortalParser } from './base.ts';

const MONTHS: Record<string, string> = {
  jan: '01', january: '01',
  feb: '02', february: '02',
  mar: '03', march: '03',
  apr: '04', april: '04',
  may: '05',
  jun: '06', june: '06',
  jul: '07', july: '07',
  aug: '08', august: '08',
  sep: '09', sept: '09', september: '09',
  oct: '10', october: '10',
  nov: '11', november: '11',
  dec: '12', december: '12',
};

export function normalizeHumanDate(raw?: string | null): string | null {
  const cleaned = cleanText(raw);
  if (!cleaned || /^(-+|—+|n\/?a|nil|tba|not\s+applicable)$/i.test(cleaned)) return null;
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(cleaned)) return cleaned;

  // YYYYMMDD embedded in NTA notice filenames like Notice_20261119125453.pdf
  const compact = cleaned.match(/(?:^|_)(\d{4})(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])(?:\d{6})?(?:\.pdf|$)/i);
  if (compact) {
    return `${compact[1]}-${compact[2]}-${compact[3]}`;
  }

  // DD/MM/YYYY or DD-MM-YYYY or DD.MM.YYYY anywhere inside text
  const dmy = cleaned.match(/(\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{4})/);
  if (dmy) {
    const day = Number(dmy[1]);
    const mon = Number(dmy[2]);
    if (day >= 1 && day <= 31 && mon >= 1 && mon <= 12) {
      return `${dmy[3]}-${String(mon).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }
  }

  // e.g. "20 May 2026" or "10th August 2026"
  const dMonY = cleaned.match(/(\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+([A-Za-z]{3,9}),?\s+(\d{4})/);
  if (dMonY) {
    const mon = MONTHS[dMonY[2].toLowerCase()] || MONTHS[dMonY[2].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()];
    if (mon) {
      return `${dMonY[3]}-${mon}-${dMonY[1].padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }
  }

  // e.g. "Nov 02, 2026" or "November 23rd, 2026"
  const monDY = cleaned.match(/\b([A-Za-z]{3,9})\s+(\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?,\s*(\d{4})\b/);
  if (monDY) {
    const mon = MONTHS[monDY[1].toLowerCase()] || MONTHS[monDY[1].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()];
    if (mon) {
      return `${monDY[3]}-${mon}-${monDY[2].padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

export function isPublicHttpUrl(url: string | null): boolean {
  if (!url) return false;
  return /^https?:\/\/(?!10\.|127\.|192\.168\.|172\.(1[6-9]|2\d|3[01])\.)/i.test(url);
}

export function parseUpscDetailHtml(item: ParsedNotification, html: string): ParsedNotification {
  const $ = cheerio.load(html);
  const next: ParsedNotification = { ...item };
  const details: string[] = [];

  if (!next.notification_number) {
    const bodyText = $('body').text();
    const noticeMatch = bodyText.match(/Examination Notice No:\s*([A-Z0-9/()-]+)/i);
    if (noticeMatch && noticeMatch[1] !== '—') {
      next.notification_number = cleanText(noticeMatch[1]);
    }
  }

  $('.exam-detail-card .col-lg, .exam-detail-card > div').each((_, col) => {
    const children = $(col).children();
    if (children.length < 2) return;
    const label = cleanText(children.eq(0).text()) || '';
    const value = cleanText(children.eq(1).text()) || '';
    if (!label || !value) return;

    if (/date of notification/i.test(label)) {
      const iso = normalizeHumanDate(value);
      if (iso) {
        next.published_date = iso;
        if (!next.application_start_date) {
          next.application_start_date = iso;
        }
      }
    } else if (/date of upload/i.test(label) && !next.published_date) {
      const iso = normalizeHumanDate(value);
      if (iso) next.published_date = iso;
    } else if (/commencement of examination/i.test(label)) {
      const iso = normalizeHumanDate(value);
      if (iso) next.exam_date = iso;
    } else if (/last date for submission/i.test(label)) {
      const iso = normalizeHumanDate(value);
      next.application_deadline = iso;
      if (!iso) {
        next.application_start_date = null;
      }
    } else if (/duration of examination/i.test(label)) {
      details.push(`Duration: ${value}`);
    }
  });

  // Extract official Documents & Downloads rows (e-Admit Card, Written Result, Final Result, Time Table)
  const docLabels: string[] = [];
  $('table tr').each((_, tr) => {
    const tds = $(tr).find('td');
    if (tds.length < 2) return;
    const docType = cleanText($(tds[0]).text()) || '';
    const uploadDate = normalizeHumanDate($(tds[1]).text());
    if (!docType || /document type/i.test(docType)) return;
    docLabels.push(docType.replace(/keyboard_arrow_down/gi, '').trim());

    if (/admit\s*card/i.test(docType) && uploadDate) {
      next.admit_card_date = uploadDate;
    } else if (/(?:written|final|recommended).*result|^\s*result/i.test(docType) && uploadDate) {
      next.result_date = uploadDate;
    }
  });

  // Extract official PDF URL embedded in UPSC Next.js payload or anchors
  const pdfMatches = Array.from(
    new Set(html.match(/https?:\/\/(?:www\.)?upsc\.gov\.in\/uploads\/[^"'\s\\]+\.pdf/gi) || [])
  ).filter((u) => isPublicHttpUrl(u));
  if (pdfMatches.length > 0) {
    const englishNotif =
      pdfMatches.find((u) => /notif/i.test(u) && /engl/i.test(u)) ||
      pdfMatches.find((u) => /notif/i.test(u)) ||
      pdfMatches.find((u) => /engl/i.test(u)) ||
      pdfMatches[0];
    next.pdf_url = englishNotif;
  }

  const factParts: string[] = [];
  if (next.notification_number) factParts.push(`Notice No: ${next.notification_number}`);
  factParts.push(...details);
  if (docLabels.length > 0) factParts.push(`Documents: ${docLabels.slice(0, 5).join(', ')}`);
  if (factParts.length > 0) {
    next.description = factParts.join(' · ');
  }
  return next;
}

export function parseRbiDetailHtml(item: ParsedNotification, html: string, detailUrl: string): ParsedNotification {
  const $ = cheerio.load(html);
  const next: ParsedNotification = { ...item };
  const container = $('#pnlContent, .tablebg, main, body').first();
  const txt = cleanText(container.text()) || '';

  const pubMatch = txt.match(/Date\s*:\s*([A-Za-z]+\s+\d{1,2},\s*\d{4}|\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})/i);
  if (pubMatch) {
    next.published_date = normalizeHumanDate(pubMatch[1]);
  }

  const lastDateMatch = txt.match(
    /(?:last\s+date[^.]{0,80}?|on\s+or\s+before|reach[^.]{0,40}?by)\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|[A-Za-z]+\s+\d{1,2},\s*\d{4}|\d{1,2}\s+[A-Za-z]+\s*,?\s*\d{4})/i
  );
  if (lastDateMatch) {
    next.application_deadline = normalizeHumanDate(lastDateMatch[1]);
  }

  const vacMatch =
    txt.match(/\b(?:fill|total|engage)\s+(?:[a-z]+\s+)?\((\d{1,4})\)\s*(?:posts?|vacanc(?:y|ies))/i) ||
    txt.match(/\b(\d{1,4})\s+(?:posts?|vacanc(?:y|ies))\b/i);
  if (vacMatch) {
    next.vacancy_count = vacMatch[1];
  }

  // Filter out persistent site-wide header/footer PDFs
  const pdfLinks: string[] = [];
  $('a[href*=".pdf"]').each((_, a) => {
    const resolved = resolveUrl(detailUrl, $(a).attr('href'));
    if (
      resolved &&
      isPublicHttpUrl(resolved) &&
      !/Annual_Recruitment_Calendar|Utkarsh2029/i.test(resolved)
    ) {
      pdfLinks.push(resolved);
    }
  });
  if (pdfLinks.length > 0) {
    next.pdf_url = pdfLinks[0];
  }

  const para = cleanText($('#pnlContent p, .tablebg p').first().text());
  if (para && para.length > 30) {
    next.description = para.slice(0, 260);
  }
  return next;
}

export function extractIbpsSubpageDates(items: ParsedNotification[], html: string, pageUrl: string): void {
  const $ = cheerio.load(html);
  const entries: { date: string | null; title: string; pdfUrl: string | null }[] = [];

  $('a').each((_, a) => {
    const href = resolveUrl(pageUrl, $(a).attr('href'));
    const text = cleanText($(a).text());
    if (!href || !text || text.length < 15) return;
    if (!/\.pdf$/i.test(href) && !/notification|result|allotment|call\s*letter|score/i.test(text)) return;

    const parentText = cleanText($(a).closest('li, tr, p, div').text()) || text;
    const dateMatch = parentText.match(/(\d{1,2}\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9}\s+\d{4}|\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})/);
    const pubDate = dateMatch ? normalizeHumanDate(dateMatch[1]) : null;
    entries.push({
      date: pubDate,
      title: text,
      pdfUrl: /\.pdf$/i.test(href) && isPublicHttpUrl(href) ? href : null,
    });
  });

  if (entries.length === 0) return;

  for (const item of items) {
    const titleLower = item.title.toLowerCase();
    const match =
      entries.find((e) => e.title.toLowerCase() === titleLower) ||
      entries.find(
        (e) =>
          (item.notification_number &&
            e.title.toLowerCase().includes(item.notification_number.toLowerCase())) ||
          (/notification/i.test(e.title) && item.source_url === pageUrl)
      ) ||
      entries.find((e) => item.source_url === pageUrl);

    if (match) {
      if (!item.published_date && match.date) item.published_date = match.date;
      if (!item.pdf_url && match.pdfUrl) item.pdf_url = match.pdfUrl;
      if (/result|provisional\s+allotment|score/i.test(item.title) && match.date) {
        item.result_date = match.date;
      }
      if (/call\s*letter|admit\s*card/i.test(item.title) && match.date) {
        item.admit_card_date = match.date;
      }
    }
  }
}

const upscParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'upsc',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://upsc.gov.in/active-examinations/') {
    const $ = cheerio.load(html);
    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];

    // 1. Offline HTML Fixture structure
    const fixtureRows = $('table.upsc-notice-table tbody tr.upsc-row');
    if (fixtureRows.length > 0) {
      fixtureRows.each((_, el) => {
        const row = $(el);
        const titleEl = row.find('td.col-title a').first();
        const title = cleanText(titleEl.text());
        if (!title) return;
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'Union Public Service Commission (UPSC)',
          notification_number: cleanText(row.find('td.col-advt').text()),
          vacancy_count: cleanText(row.find('td.col-vacancy').text()),
          job_category: cleanText(row.attr('data-category')) || 'Central Civil Services',
          application_start_date: cleanText(row.find('td.col-start').text()),
          application_deadline: cleanText(row.find('td.col-deadline').text()),
          exam_date: cleanText(row.find('td.col-exam').text()),
          admit_card_date: cleanText(row.find('td.col-admit').text()),
          result_date: cleanText(row.find('td.col-result').text()),
          description: cleanText(row.find('td.col-title p.summary').text()),
          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, row.find('td.col-pdf a').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: cleanText(row.find('td.col-published').text()),
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    // 2. Live upsc.gov.in/active-examinations/ HTML table
    $('table tr').each((_, tr) => {
      const tds = $(tr).find('td');
      if (tds.length < 3) return;
      const anchor = $(tds[0]).find('a').first();
      const title = cleanText(anchor.text() || $(tds[0]).text());
      if (!title || /examination name/i.test(title)) return;
      const noticeNo = cleanText($(tds[1]).text());
      const examDate = normalizeHumanDate($(tds[2]).text());
      const deadline = tds.length >= 4 ? normalizeHumanDate($(tds[3]).text()) : null;
      const href = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, anchor.attr('href')) || sourceUrl;
      results.push({
        title,
        organization_name: 'Union Public Service Commission (UPSC)',
        notification_number: noticeNo && noticeNo !== '—' ? noticeNo : null,
        vacancy_count: null,
        job_category: 'UPSC Active Examination',
        application_start_date: null,
        application_deadline: deadline,
        exam_date: examDate,
        admit_card_date: null,
        result_date: null,
        description: noticeNo && noticeNo !== '—' ? `Examination Notice No: ${noticeNo}` : null,
        pdf_url: href.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf') ? href : null,
        source_url: href,
        published_date: null,
      });
    });
    return results.slice(0, 12);
  },
};

const sscParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'ssc',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://ssc.gov.in/') {
    const $ = cheerio.load(html);
    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];
    const cards = $('section.ssc-notice-board article.notice-board-card');
    if (cards.length > 0) {
      cards.each((_, el) => {
        const card = $(el);
        const titleEl = card.find('.notice-title a').first();
        const title = cleanText(titleEl.text());
        if (!title) return;
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'Staff Selection Commission (SSC)',
          notification_number: cleanText(card.find('.notice-num').text()),
          vacancy_count: cleanText(card.find('.vacancies').text()),
          job_category: cleanText(card.attr('data-category')) || 'Central Group B & C',
          application_start_date: cleanText(card.find('.start-date').text()),
          application_deadline: cleanText(card.find('.deadline').text()),
          exam_date: cleanText(card.find('.exam-date').text()),
          admit_card_date: cleanText(card.find('.admit-date').text()),
          result_date: cleanText(card.find('.result-date').text()),
          description: cleanText(card.find('.notice-desc').text()),
          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, card.find('a.pdf-link').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: cleanText(card.find('.published-date').text()),
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    // Live SSC fallback
    $('a[href*=".pdf"], .latest-news-list a, .notice-board a').each((_, el) => {
      const title = cleanText($(el).text());
      const href = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, $(el).attr('href'));
      if (title && href && title.length > 18) {
        const parentText = cleanText($(el).closest('li, tr, div').text()) || '';
        const dateMatch = parentText.match(/(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9}\s+\d{4})/);
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'Staff Selection Commission (SSC)',
          notification_number: null,
          vacancy_count: null,
          job_category: 'SSC Notice',
          application_start_date: null,
          application_deadline: null,
          exam_date: null,
          admit_card_date: null,
          result_date: null,
          description: null,
          pdf_url: href.toLowerCase().includes('.pdf') && isPublicHttpUrl(href) ? href : null,
          source_url: href,
          published_date: dateMatch ? normalizeHumanDate(dateMatch[1]) : null,
        });
      }
    });
    return results.slice(0, 12);
  },
};

const tnpscParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'tnpsc',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://www.tnpsc.gov.in/English/Notification.aspx') {
    const $ = cheerio.load(html);
    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];

    // 1. Offline HTML Fixture
    const fixtureRows = $('table#notification-grid tbody tr.tnpsc-item');
    if (fixtureRows.length > 0) {
      fixtureRows.each((_, el) => {
        const row = $(el);
        const titleEl = row.find('td.post-title a').first();
        const title = cleanText(titleEl.text());
        if (!title) return;
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'Tamil Nadu Public Service Commission (TNPSC)',
          notification_number: cleanText(row.find('td.advt-no').text()),
          vacancy_count: cleanText(row.find('td.vacancies').text()),
          job_category: cleanText(row.find('td.category').text()) || 'State Civil Services',
          application_start_date: cleanText(row.find('td.start').text()),
          application_deadline: cleanText(row.find('td.deadline').text()),
          exam_date: cleanText(row.find('td.exam').text()),
          admit_card_date: cleanText(row.find('td.admit').text()),
          result_date: cleanText(row.find('td.result').text()),
          description: cleanText(row.find('td.desc').text()),
          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, row.find('td.pdf a').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: cleanText(row.find('td.published').text()),
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    // 2. Live TNPSC Notification.aspx table
    $('table tr').each((_, tr) => {
      const tds = $(tr).find('td');
      if (tds.length < 5) return;
      const notifNo = cleanText($(tds[1]).text());
      if (!notifNo || !/\d{1,3}\/\d{4}/.test(notifNo)) return;

      const postTd = $(tds[2]);
      const pdfAnchor = postTd
        .find('a[href*=".pdf"]')
        .filter((__, a) => !/addendum/i.test($(a).text()))
        .first();
      const title =
        cleanText(pdfAnchor.text()) ||
        cleanText(
          postTd
            .clone()
            .find('a')
            .filter((__, a) => /apply online|hall ticket|addendum/i.test($(a).text()))
            .remove()
            .end()
            .text()
        );
      if (!title) return;

      const pdfUrl = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, pdfAnchor.attr('href'));
      const startDate = normalizeHumanDate($(tds[3]).text());
      const deadline = normalizeHumanDate($(tds[4]).text());
      const rawExamText = tds.length >= 6 ? cleanText($(tds[5]).text()) : null;
      const examDate = normalizeHumanDate(rawExamText);

      const descParts = [`TNPSC Notification ${notifNo}`];
      if (rawExamText) descParts.push(`Exam Schedule: ${rawExamText}`);
      const hasHallTicket = postTd
        .find('a')
        .toArray()
        .some((a) => /hall ticket/i.test($(a).text()) && !/display:\s*none/i.test($(a).attr('style') || ''));
      if (hasHallTicket) descParts.push('Hall Ticket download link active on portal');

      results.push({
        title,
        organization_name: 'Tamil Nadu Public Service Commission (TNPSC)',
        notification_number: notifNo,
        vacancy_count: null,
        job_category: 'State Civil Services',
        application_start_date: startDate,
        application_deadline: deadline,
        exam_date: examDate,
        admit_card_date: null,
        result_date: null,
        description: descParts.join(' · '),
        pdf_url: isPublicHttpUrl(pdfUrl) ? pdfUrl : null,
        source_url: sourceUrl,
        published_date: startDate,
      });
    });
    return results.slice(0, 12);
  },
};

const tnusrbParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'tnusrb',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://www.tnusrb.tn.gov.in/') {

    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];

          if (key) fields[key] = cleanText($(li).text());
        });

          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, card.find('a.btn-pdf').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: fields.published ?? null,
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    $('a[href*=".pdf"], a').each((_, el) => {
      const title = cleanText($(el).text());
      const href = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, $(el).attr('href'));
      if (title && href && title.length > 18 && /sub-inspector|constable|recruitment|notification|brochure/i.test(title)) {
        const parentText = cleanText($(el).closest('li, tr, div').text()) || '';
        const dateMatch = parentText.match(/(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9}\s+\d{4})/);
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'Tamil Nadu Uniformed Services Recruitment Board (TNUSRB)',
          notification_number: null,
          vacancy_count: null,
          job_category: 'Police & Uniformed Services',
          application_start_date: null,
          application_deadline: null,
          exam_date: null,
          admit_card_date: null,
          result_date: null,
          description: null,
          pdf_url: href.toLowerCase().includes('.pdf') && isPublicHttpUrl(href) ? href : null,
          source_url: href,
          published_date: dateMatch ? normalizeHumanDate(dateMatch[1]) : null,
        });
      }
    });
    return results.slice(0, 10);
  },
};

const ibpsParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'ibps',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://www.ibps.in/') {
    const $ = cheerio.load(html);
    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];
    const items = $('.crp-notification-list .crp-item');
    if (items.length > 0) {
      items.each((_, el) => {
        const item = $(el);
        const titleEl = item.find('h3 a').first();
        const title = cleanText(titleEl.text());
        if (!title) return;
        const kv: Record<string, string | null> = {};
        item.find('dl.crp-dates dd[data-key]').each((__, dd) => {
          const key = $(dd).attr('data-key');
          if (key) kv[key] = cleanText($(dd).text());
        });
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'Institute of Banking Personnel Selection (IBPS)',
          notification_number: cleanText(item.find('.crp-ref').text()),
          vacancy_count: kv.vacancies ?? null,
          job_category: cleanText(item.attr('data-stream')) || 'Public Sector Banking',
          application_start_date: kv.start ?? null,
          application_deadline: kv.deadline ?? null,
          exam_date: kv.exam ?? null,
          admit_card_date: kv.admit ?? null,
          result_date: kv.result ?? null,
          description: cleanText(item.find('p.crp-desc').text()),
          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, item.find('a.crp-pdf').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: kv.published ?? null,
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    // Live IBPS portal links (prioritizing specific notifications/allotments over generic category nav)
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    $('a').each((_, el) => {
      const title = cleanText($(el).text());
      const href = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, $(el).attr('href'));
      if (!title || !href || title.length < 18) return;
      if (/^CRP\s*[-\s]?(RRBs|CSA.*|PO\/MTs|Clerks|Specialist Officers)$/i.test(title)) return;
      if (!/CRP|Notification for Common Recruitment|Provisional Allotment|Result/i.test(title)) return;
      const dedupeKey = title.toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(dedupeKey)) return;
      seen.add(dedupeKey);

      const crpMatch = title.match(/CRP[-\sA-Z/]+(?:XVII|XVI|XV|XIV)/i)?.[0] || null;
      const isPdf = href.toLowerCase().includes('.pdf');
      // Try extracting date embedded in PDF filename like _30.06.2026.pdf
      const fileDateMatch = isPdf ? href.match(/(\d{2}\.\d{2}\.\d{4})\.pdf$/i) : null;
      const parentText = cleanText($(el).closest('li, p, div').text()) || '';
      const parentDateMatch = parentText.match(/(\d{1,2}\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9}\s+\d{4})/);
      const pubDate = fileDateMatch
        ? normalizeHumanDate(fileDateMatch[1])
        : parentDateMatch
          ? normalizeHumanDate(parentDateMatch[1])
          : null;

      results.push({
        title,
        organization_name: 'Institute of Banking Personnel Selection (IBPS)',
        notification_number: crpMatch,
        vacancy_count: null,
        job_category: 'Public Sector Banking',
        application_start_date: null,
        application_deadline: null,
        exam_date: null,
        admit_card_date: null,
        result_date: /provisional allotment|result/i.test(title) && pubDate ? pubDate : null,
        description: null,
        pdf_url: isPdf && isPublicHttpUrl(href) ? href : null,
        source_url: isPdf ? sourceUrl : href,
        published_date: pubDate,
      });
    });
    return results.slice(0, 12);
  },
};

const sbiParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'sbi',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://sbi.co.in/web/careers/current-openings') {
    const $ = cheerio.load(html);
    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];

    // 1. Offline HTML Fixture

    if (fixtureRows.length > 0) {
      fixtureRows.each((_, el) => {
        const row = $(el);
        const titleEl = row.find('td.title-col a').first();
        const title = cleanText(titleEl.text());
        if (!title) return;
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'State Bank of India (SBI Careers)',
          notification_number: cleanText(row.find('td.advt-col').text()),
          vacancy_count: cleanText(row.find('td.vac-col').text()),
          job_category: cleanText(row.attr('data-category')) || 'SBI Banking Recruitment',
          application_start_date: cleanText(row.find('td.start-col').text()),
          application_deadline: cleanText(row.find('td.end-col').text()),
          exam_date: cleanText(row.find('td.exam-col').text()),
          admit_card_date: cleanText(row.find('td.admit-col').text()),
          result_date: cleanText(row.find('td.res-col').text()),
          description: cleanText(row.find('td.title-col .opening-note').text()),
          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, row.find('td.pdf-col a').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: cleanText(row.find('td.pub-col').text()),
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    // 2. Live SBI Careers current-openings accordion cards
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    $('li.icon-touch a[href*=".pdf"]').each((_, a) => {
      const rawHref = $(a).attr('href') || '';
      const linkText = cleanText($(a).text()) || '';
      if (!/english|adv|detailed/i.test(linkText + rawHref)) return;
      const resolvedPdf = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, rawHref);
      if (!isPublicHttpUrl(resolvedPdf)) return;

      const collapseDiv = $(a).closest('.panel-collapse, .accordion-collapse, .collapse, .card-body');
      const prevTitle = cleanText(collapseDiv.prev().text());
      const card = $(a).closest('.panel, .card, .accordion-item');
      const cardMeta = cleanText(card.find('h3, h4, h5, button, .panel-title, .accordion-button').first().text()) || '';
      const rawTitle = prevTitle || cardMeta;
      if (!rawTitle || rawTitle.length < 15) return;

      const fullCardText = cleanText(card.text()) || cardMeta;
      const cleanTitle = cleanText(rawTitle.replace(/\(Apply Online from[^)]+\)/gi, '')) || rawTitle;
      if (seen.has(cleanTitle)) return;
      seen.add(cleanTitle);

      // Extract exact officially announced Apply Online window and Last Date
      const windowMatch = fullCardText.match(
        /Apply Online from\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})\s*TO\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})/i
      );
      const startDate = windowMatch ? normalizeHumanDate(windowMatch[1]) : null;
      const windowEndDate = windowMatch ? normalizeHumanDate(windowMatch[2]) : null;
      const lastDateMatch = fullCardText.match(/LAST DATE TO APPLY\s*:\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})/i);
      const deadline = (lastDateMatch ? normalizeHumanDate(lastDateMatch[1]) : null) || windowEndDate;

      const advtMatch =
        fullCardText.match(/ADVERTISEMENT NO:\s*([A-Z0-9/_-]+)/i)?.[1] ||
        rawHref.match(/CRPD[-_][A-Z0-9_-]+|SCO-\d+/i)?.[0] ||
        null;

      results.push({
        title: cleanTitle,
        organization_name: 'State Bank of India (SBI Careers)',
        notification_number: advtMatch,
        vacancy_count: null,
        job_category: 'SBI Banking Recruitment',
        application_start_date: startDate,
        application_deadline: deadline,
        exam_date: null,
        admit_card_date: null,
        result_date: null,
        description: advtMatch
          ? `SBI Current Opening (${advtMatch})${startDate && deadline ? ` · Online window ${startDate} to ${deadline}` : ''}`
          : null,
        pdf_url: resolvedPdf,
        source_url: sourceUrl,
        published_date: null,
      });
    });
    return results.slice(0, 12);
  },
};

const rbiParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'rbi',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://opportunities.rbi.org.in/Scripts/Vacancies.aspx') {
    const $ = cheerio.load(html);
    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];
    const notices = $('.rbi-vacancies-list .rbi-notice');
    if (notices.length > 0) {
      notices.each((_, el) => {
        const notice = $(el);
        const titleEl = notice.find('a.rbi-link').first();
        const title = cleanText(titleEl.text());
        if (!title) return;
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'Reserve Bank of India (RBI Opportunities)',
          notification_number: cleanText(notice.find('.rbi-advt').text()),
          vacancy_count: cleanText(notice.find('.f-vac').text()),
          job_category: cleanText(notice.attr('data-category')) || 'Central Banking',
          application_start_date: cleanText(notice.find('.f-start').text()),
          application_deadline: cleanText(notice.find('.f-deadline').text()),
          exam_date: cleanText(notice.find('.f-exam').text()),
          admit_card_date: cleanText(notice.find('.f-admit').text()),
          result_date: cleanText(notice.find('.f-result').text()),
          description: cleanText(notice.find('p.rbi-summary').text()),
          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, notice.find('a.rbi-pdf').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: cleanText(notice.find('.f-pub').text()),
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    // Live RBI Opportunities vacancy rows
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    $('a[href*="bs_viewcontent"]').each((_, el) => {
      const title = cleanText($(el).text());
      const href = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, $(el).attr('href'));
      if (!title || !href || title.length < 20) return;
      if (/^(Recruitment|Promotion|Rosters at ROs)$/i.test(title)) return;
      if (seen.has(href)) return;
      seen.add(href);

      const idMatch = href.match(/[?&]Id=(\d+)/i);
      results.push({
        title,
        organization_name: 'Reserve Bank of India (RBI Opportunities)',
        notification_number: idMatch ? `RBI-VAC-${idMatch[1]}` : null,
        vacancy_count: null,
        job_category: 'Central Banking',
        application_start_date: null,
        application_deadline: null,
        exam_date: null,
        admit_card_date: null,
        result_date: null,
        description: null,
        pdf_url: null,
        source_url: href,
        published_date: null,
      });
    });
    return results.slice(0, 10);
  },
};

const ntaParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'nta',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://nta.ac.in/') {
    const $ = cheerio.load(html);
    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];
    const rows = $('section.public-notices-grid .nta-notice-row');
    if (rows.length > 0) {
      rows.each((_, el) => {
        const row = $(el);
        const titleEl = row.find('.nta-title a').first();
        const title = cleanText(titleEl.text());
        if (!title) return;
        const meta: Record<string, string | null> = {};
        row.find('.nta-meta span[data-m]').each((__, sp) => {
          const key = $(sp).attr('data-m');
          if (key) meta[key] = cleanText($(sp).text());
        });
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'National Testing Agency (NTA)',
          notification_number: cleanText(row.find('.nta-ref').text()),
          vacancy_count: meta.vacancies ?? null,
          job_category: cleanText(row.attr('data-category')) || 'National Examination',
          application_start_date: meta.start ?? null,
          application_deadline: meta.deadline ?? null,
          exam_date: meta.exam ?? null,
          admit_card_date: meta.admit ?? null,
          result_date: meta.result ?? null,
          description: cleanText(row.find('p.nta-desc').text()),
          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, row.find('a.nta-pdf').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: meta.published ?? null,
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    // 2. Live NTA Public Notices (<p><content>Title</content><a href="/Download/Notice/Notice_YYYYMMDD...pdf">Read More</a></p>)
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const datedResults: ParsedNotification[] = [];
    const generalResults: ParsedNotification[] = [];
    $('a[href*="/Download/Notice/"]').each((_, a) => {
      const href = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, $(a).attr('href'));
      if (!href || !isPublicHttpUrl(href)) return;
      const parentP = $(a).closest('p, li, div');
      const contentTitle = cleanText(parentP.find('content').first().text());
      const fallbackParent = cleanText(
        parentP
          .clone()
          .find('a')
          .remove()
          .end()
          .text()
      );
      const linkText = cleanText($(a).text());
      const title =
        contentTitle ||
        (fallbackParent && fallbackParent.length > 15 ? fallbackParent : null) ||
        (linkText && !/^read\s*more$/i.test(linkText) ? linkText : null);
      if (!title || title.length < 12 || seen.has(title)) return;
      if (/^procedure for duplicate score card$/i.test(title)) return;
      seen.add(title);

      const pubDate = normalizeHumanDate(href);
      const inlineDate = normalizeHumanDate(title);
      const isAdmit = /admit\s*card|city\s*intimation/i.test(title);
      const isResult = /result|score\s*card|answer\s*key/i.test(title);
      const category = isAdmit
        ? 'Admit Card / Intimation'
        : isResult
          ? 'Examination Result'
          : 'National Examination Notice';

      const entry: ParsedNotification = {
        title,
        organization_name: 'National Testing Agency (NTA)',
        notification_number: null,
        vacancy_count: null,
        job_category: category,
        application_start_date: null,
        application_deadline: null,
        exam_date: /schedule|exam/i.test(title) && inlineDate ? inlineDate : null,
        admit_card_date: isAdmit ? pubDate : null,
        result_date: isResult ? pubDate : null,
        description: pubDate ? `Official NTA Public Notice released on ${pubDate}.` : null,
        pdf_url: href,
        source_url: sourceUrl,
        published_date: pubDate,
      };
      if (pubDate) {
        datedResults.push(entry);
      } else {
        generalResults.push(entry);
      }
    });

    const combinedNta = [...datedResults, ...generalResults];
    if (combinedNta.length > 0) {
      return combinedNta.slice(0, 12);
    }

    // Fallback to NTA exam portal links only if no public notice PDFs matched
    $('a').each((_, el) => {
      const title = cleanText($(el).text());
      const href = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, $(el).attr('href'));
      if (!title || !href || title.length < 15 || seen.has(title)) return;
      if (/nta\.nic\.in|Notice|Examination|CUET|JEE|NEET|UGC|AISSEE|NCET|SWAYAM/i.test(title + href)) {
        seen.add(title);
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'National Testing Agency (NTA)',
          notification_number: null,
          vacancy_count: null,
          job_category: 'National Examination',
          application_start_date: null,
          application_deadline: null,
          exam_date: null,
          admit_card_date: null,
          result_date: null,
          description: null,
          pdf_url: href.toLowerCase().includes('.pdf') ? href : null,
          source_url: href,
          published_date: null,
        });
      }
    });
    return results.slice(0, 12);
  },
};

const indiapostParser: PortalParser = {
  parserKey: 'indiapost',
  parse(html, sourceUrl = 'https://www.indiapost.gov.in/') {
    const $ = cheerio.load(html);
    const results: ParsedNotification[] = [];
    const entries = $('.gds-engagement-list .indiapost-entry');
    if (entries.length > 0) {
      entries.each((_, el) => {
        const entry = $(el);
        const titleEl = entry.find('h4.ip-title a').first();
        const title = cleanText(titleEl.text());
        if (!title) return;
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'India Post — Department of Posts',
          notification_number: cleanText(entry.find('.ip-notif-no').text()),
          vacancy_count: cleanText(entry.find('.ip-vac').text()),
          job_category: cleanText(entry.attr('data-category')) || 'Postal Services',
          application_start_date: cleanText(entry.find('.ip-start').text()),
          application_deadline: cleanText(entry.find('.ip-deadline').text()),
          exam_date: cleanText(entry.find('.ip-exam').text()),
          admit_card_date: cleanText(entry.find('.ip-admit').text()),
          result_date: cleanText(entry.find('.ip-result').text()),
          description: cleanText(entry.find('p.ip-desc').text()),
          pdf_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, entry.find('a.ip-pdf').attr('href')),
          source_url: resolveUrl(sourceUrl, titleEl.attr('href')) || sourceUrl,
          published_date: cleanText(entry.find('.ip-pub').text()),
        });
      });
      return results;
    }

    // 2. Live India Post Next.js /vacancies RSC stream JSON extraction
    const unescaped = html.replace(/\\"/g, '"');
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const rscRegex =
      /"title"\s*:\s*"([^"]{15,300})"\s*,\s*"category"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"\s*,\s*"date"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"[^}]{0,220}?"version"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"[^}]{0,120}?"url"\s*:\s*"(\/api\/documents\/file\/[^"]+)"/g;
    let m: RegExpExecArray | null;
    const englishFirst: ParsedNotification[] = [];
    const otherLangs: ParsedNotification[] = [];

    while ((m = rscRegex.exec(unescaped)) !== null) {
      const title = cleanText(m[1]);
      const rawCat = cleanText(m[2]) || 'Postal Services';
      const pubDate = normalizeHumanDate(m[3]);
      const version = cleanText(m[4]) || '';
      const pdfUrl = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, m[5]);
      if (!title || !pdfUrl || !isPublicHttpUrl(pdfUrl)) continue;
      if (/^hindi version$/i.test(version) && englishFirst.some((r) => r.title === title)) continue;
      const dedupeKey = title.toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(dedupeKey)) continue;
      seen.add(dedupeKey);

      const vacMatch = title.match(/\b(\d{1,4})\s+(?:vacant\s+)?posts?\b/i);
      const notifNoMatch = title.match(/\bNo\.?\s*([A-Z0-9/-]+(?:-\d{4})?)\b/i);
      const parsed: ParsedNotification = {
        title,
        organization_name: 'India Post — Department of Posts',
        notification_number: notifNoMatch ? notifNoMatch[1] : null,
        vacancy_count: vacMatch ? vacMatch[1] : null,
        job_category: /recruit/i.test(rawCat) ? 'Postal Recruitment' : rawCat,
        application_start_date: null,
        application_deadline: null,
        exam_date: null,
        admit_card_date: null,
        result_date: null,
        description: version ? `India Post ${rawCat} (${version})` : `India Post ${rawCat}`,
        pdf_url: pdfUrl,
        source_url: sourceUrl,
        published_date: pubDate,
      };
      if (/english/i.test(version)) {
        englishFirst.push(parsed);
      } else {
        otherLangs.push(parsed);
      }
    }

    const rscCombined = [...englishFirst, ...otherLangs];
    if (rscCombined.length > 0) {
      return rscCombined.slice(0, 12);
    }

    // 3. Generic anchor fallback
    $('a').each((_, el) => {
      const title = cleanText($(el).text());
      const href = resolveUrl(sourceUrl, $(el).attr('href'));
      if (
        title &&
        href &&
        title.length > 22 &&
        !/^(Vacancies|Recruitment Rules|Recruitments)$/i.test(title) &&
        !/recruitment\s*rules/i.test(title) &&
        /gds|gramin dak|recruitment|engagement|vacancy/i.test(title + href)
      ) {
        results.push({
          title,
          organization_name: 'India Post — Department of Posts',
          notification_number: null,
          vacancy_count: null,
          job_category: 'Postal Services',
          application_start_date: null,
          application_deadline: null,
          exam_date: null,
          admit_card_date: null,
          result_date: null,
          description: null,
          pdf_url: href.toLowerCase().includes('.pdf') ? href : null,
          source_url: href,
          published_date: null,
        });
      }
    });
    return results.slice(0, 10);
  },
};

export const PARSER_REGISTRY: Record<string, PortalParser> = {
  upsc: upscParser,
  ssc: sscParser,
  tnpsc: tnpscParser,
  tnusrb: tnusrbParser,
  rrbcdg: rrbcdgParser,
  ibps: ibpsParser,
  sbi: sbiParser,
  rbi: rbiParser,
  nta: ntaParser,
  indiapost: indiapostParser,
};

export function getParser(parserKey: string): PortalParser {
  const parser = PARSER_REGISTRY[parserKey];
  if (!parser) {
    throw new Error(`Unsupported parser_key: ${parserKey}`);
  }
  return parser;
}

===== FILE: server/changeDetector.ts =====

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { db } from './db.ts';
import type { ParsedNotification } from './scrapers/base.ts';

export const TRACKED_FIELDS = [
  'title',
  'organization_name',
  'notification_number',
  'vacancy_count',
  'job_category',
  'application_start_date',
  'application_deadline',
  'exam_date',
  'admit_card_date',
  'result_date',
  'description',
  'pdf_url',
  'source_url',
  'published_date',
  'notification_status',
] as const;

export type TrackedField = (typeof TRACKED_FIELDS)[number];

const PRESERVABLE_FIELDS: readonly TrackedField[] = [
  'notification_number',
  'vacancy_count',
  'job_category',
  'application_start_date',
  'application_deadline',
  'exam_date',
  'admit_card_date',
  'result_date',
  'description',
  'pdf_url',
  'published_date',
];

const HISTORY_WORTHY_FIELDS = new Set<string>([
  'exam_date',
  'application_deadline',
  'application_start_date',
  'admit_card_date',
  'result_date',
  'vacancy_count',
  'notification_status',
  'title',
  'notification_number',
  'published_date',
  'pdf_url',
  'source_url',
]);


export interface FieldDiff {
  field: string;
  old_value: string | null;
  new_value: string | null;
}

export function normalizeStr(value: unknown): string | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
  const cleaned = String(value).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  return cleaned.length > 0 ? cleaned : null;
}

export function resolveNotificationStatus(
  rawStatus: unknown,
  title: unknown,
  description: unknown
): string {
  const s = (normalizeStr(rawStatus) || '').toLowerCase();
  if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'canceled' || s === 'withdrawn') return 'cancelled';
  if (s === 'removed') return 'removed';
  const combined = `${normalizeStr(title) || ''} ${normalizeStr(description) || ''}`;
  if (/\b(?:cancelled|canceled|withdrawn|annulled)\b/i.test(combined)) {
    return 'cancelled';
  }
  return 'active';
}

export function buildEntityKey(sourceCode: string, item: ParsedNotification): string {
  const notifNo = normalizeStr(item.notification_number);
  if (notifNo) {
    const slug = notifNo.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
    return `${sourceCode}::${slug}`;
  }
  const titleNorm = (normalizeStr(item.title) || 'untitled')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
  return `${sourceCode}::${titleNorm.slice(0, 120)}`;
}

export function buildSnapshot(record: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, string | null> {
  const snap: Record<string, string | null> = {};
  for (const field of TRACKED_FIELDS) {
    if (field === 'notification_status') {
      snap[field] = resolveNotificationStatus(
        record.notification_status ?? record.lifecycle_status,
        record.title,
        record.description
      );
    } else {
      snap[field] = normalizeStr(record[field]);
    }
  }
  return snap;
}

export function mergePreservingMissingFields(
  oldSnap: Record<string, string | null>,
  newSnap: Record<string, string | null>
): Record<string, string | null> {
  const merged = { ...newSnap };
  if (merged.notification_status === 'cancelled') {
    return merged;
  }
  for (const field of PRESERVABLE_FIELDS) {
    if (merged[field] === null && oldSnap[field] !== null) {
      merged[field] = oldSnap[field];
    }
  }
  return merged;
}

export function computeContentHash(snapshot: Record<string, string | null>): string {
  const sortedKeys = Object.keys(snapshot).sort();
  const ordered: Record<string, string | null> = {};
  for (const k of sortedKeys) ordered[k] = snapshot[k];
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(ordered)).digest('hex');
}

export function computeFieldDiffs(
  oldSnap: Record<string, string | null>,
  newSnap: Record<string, string | null>
): FieldDiff[] {
  const diffs: FieldDiff[] = [];
  for (const field of TRACKED_FIELDS) {
    const oldVal = oldSnap[field] ?? null;
    const newVal = newSnap[field] ?? null;
    if (oldVal !== newVal) {
      diffs.push({ field, old_value: oldVal, new_value: newVal });
    }
  }
  return diffs;
}

export function classifyFieldChange(
  field: string,
  oldVal: string | null,
  newVal: string | null
): string {
  switch (field) {
    case 'exam_date':
      return 'EXAM DATE CHANGED';
    case 'application_deadline':
      if (
        oldVal &&
        newVal &&
        /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(oldVal) &&
        /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(newVal) &&
        newVal > oldVal
      ) {
        return 'APPLICATION DEADLINE EXTENDED';
      }
      return 'APPLICATION DEADLINE CHANGED';
    case 'application_start_date':
      return 'APPLICATION START DATE CHANGED';
    case 'admit_card_date':
      return 'ADMIT CARD DATE UPDATED';
    case 'result_date':
      return 'RESULT DATE UPDATED';
    case 'vacancy_count':
      return 'VACANCY COUNT UPDATED';
    case 'notification_status':
    case 'lifecycle_status':
      if (newVal === 'cancelled') return 'NOTIFICATION CANCELLED';
      if (newVal === 'removed') return 'NOTIFICATION REMOVED FROM PORTAL';
      return 'NOTIFICATION STATUS CHANGED';
    case 'pdf_url':
      return 'OFFICIAL PDF UPDATED';
    case 'source_url':
      return 'OFFICIAL NOTIFICATION URL UPDATED';
    case 'title':
      return 'NOTIFICATION TITLE UPDATED';
    case 'published_date':
      return 'PUBLISHED DATE UPDATED';
    case 'notification_number':
      return 'NOTIFICATION NUMBER UPDATED';
    default:
      return 'NOTIFICATION DETAILS UPDATED';
  }
}

function saveVersionSnapshot(
  notifId: number,
  versionNumber: number,
  snap: Record<string, string | null>,
  contentHash: string,
  runId: string | null,
  capturedAt: string,
  fallbackOrg: string,
  fallbackUrl: string
): void {
  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO notification_versions (
      notification_id, version_number, title, organization_name, notification_number,
      vacancy_count, job_category, application_start_date, application_deadline,
      exam_date, admit_card_date, result_date, description, pdf_url, source_url,
      published_date, notification_status, content_hash, snapshot_json, run_id, captured_at
    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
  ).run(
    notifId,
    versionNumber,
    snap.title || 'Untitled Notification',
    snap.organization_name || fallbackOrg,
    snap.notification_number,
    snap.vacancy_count,
    snap.job_category,
    snap.application_start_date,
    snap.application_deadline,
    snap.exam_date,
    snap.admit_card_date,
    snap.result_date,
    snap.description,
    snap.pdf_url,
    snap.source_url || fallbackUrl,
    snap.published_date,
    snap.notification_status || 'active',
    contentHash,
    JSON.stringify(snap),
    runId,
    capturedAt
  );
}

export interface ApplyCounts {
  parsed_count: number;
  new_count: number;
  updated_count: number;
  duplicate_count: number;
  removed_count: number;
}

export function applyScrapeResults(
  source: Record<string, unknown>,
  parsedItems: ParsedNotification[],
  runId: string,
  isDemoData: boolean,
  allowRemovalCheck = true,
  removalThreshold = 2,
  suppressHistory = false
): ApplyCounts {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const counts: ApplyCounts = {
    parsed_count: parsedItems.length,
    new_count: 0,
    updated_count: 0,
    duplicate_count: 0,
    removed_count: 0,
  };

  if (parsedItems.length === 0) {
    return counts;
  }

  const sourceId = Number(source.id);
  const sourceCode = String(source.code);
  const existingRows = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM recruitment_notifications WHERE source_id = ?')
    .all(sourceId) as Record<string, unknown>[];
  const existingByKey = new Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>();
  for (const row of existingRows) {
    existingByKey.set(String(row.entity_key), row);
  }

  const seenKeys = new Set<string>();

  for (const item of parsedItems) {
    const entityKey = buildEntityKey(sourceCode, item);
    if (seenKeys.has(entityKey)) {
      counts.duplicate_count += 1;
      continue;
    }
    seenKeys.add(entityKey);

    const rawSnap = buildSnapshot(item as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
    const existing = existingByKey.get(entityKey);

    if (!existing) {
      const newHash = computeContentHash(rawSnap);
      const info = db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO recruitment_notifications (
            source_id, entity_key, content_hash, title, organization_name, notification_number,
            vacancy_count, job_category, application_start_date, application_deadline,
            exam_date, admit_card_date, result_date, description, pdf_url, source_url,
            published_date, lifecycle_status, is_removed, missing_count, is_demo_data,
            first_seen_at, last_seen_at, source_updated_at, last_run_id
          ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, 0, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
        )
        .run(
          sourceId,
          entityKey,
          newHash,
          rawSnap.title || 'Untitled Notification',
          rawSnap.organization_name || String(source.organization_name),
          rawSnap.notification_number,
          rawSnap.vacancy_count,
          rawSnap.job_category,
          rawSnap.application_start_date,
          rawSnap.application_deadline,
          rawSnap.exam_date,
          rawSnap.admit_card_date,
          rawSnap.result_date,
          rawSnap.description,
          rawSnap.pdf_url,
          rawSnap.source_url || String(source.base_url),
          rawSnap.published_date,
          rawSnap.notification_status || 'active',
          isDemoData ? 1 : 0,
          now,
          now,
          now,
          runId
        );

      const notifId = Number(info.lastInsertRowid);
      saveVersionSnapshot(
        notifId,
        1,
        rawSnap,
        newHash,
        runId,
        now,
        String(source.organization_name),
        String(source.base_url)
      );

      if (!suppressHistory) {
        const newSummaryDiffs: FieldDiff[] = [
          { field: 'title', old_value: null, new_value: rawSnap.title },
          ...(rawSnap.published_date ? [{ field: 'published_date', old_value: null, new_value: rawSnap.published_date }] : []),
          ...(rawSnap.application_start_date
            ? [{ field: 'application_start_date', old_value: null, new_value: rawSnap.application_start_date }]
            : []),
          ...(rawSnap.application_deadline
            ? [{ field: 'application_deadline', old_value: null, new_value: rawSnap.application_deadline }]
            : []),
          ...(rawSnap.exam_date ? [{ field: 'exam_date', old_value: null, new_value: rawSnap.exam_date }] : []),
          ...(rawSnap.result_date ? [{ field: 'result_date', old_value: null, new_value: rawSnap.result_date }] : []),
        ];
        const changeInfo = db
          .prepare(
            `INSERT INTO change_history (
              run_id, source_id, notification_id, entity_key, notification_title,
              change_type, changed_field, change_label, previous_value, new_value,
              changed_fields_json, before_snapshot_json, after_snapshot_json,
              source_url, is_verified, detected_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'NEW', 'title', 'NEW NOTIFICATION PUBLISHED', NULL, ?, ?, NULL, ?, ?, 0, ?)`
          )
          .run(
            runId,
            sourceId,
            notifId,
            entityKey,
            rawSnap.title || 'Untitled Notification',
            rawSnap.title || 'Untitled Notification',
            JSON.stringify(newSummaryDiffs),
            JSON.stringify(rawSnap),
            rawSnap.source_url || String(source.base_url),
            now
          );

        const changeId = Number(changeInfo.lastInsertRowid);
        db.prepare(
          `INSERT INTO alerts (
            change_id, source_id, notification_id, title, message, alert_type, severity, is_read, is_verified, created_at
          ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'NEW', 'info', 0, 0, ?)`
        ).run(
          changeId,
          sourceId,
          notifId,
          `New Recruitment: ${rawSnap.title}`,
          `${sourceCode.toUpperCase()} published a new notification (${rawSnap.notification_number || 'Unavailable'}).`,
          now
        );
      }
      counts.new_count += 1;
    } else {
      const notifId = Number(existing.id);
      const wasRemoved = Boolean(existing.is_removed);
      db.prepare(
        'UPDATE recruitment_notifications SET last_seen_at = ?, last_run_id = ?, missing_count = 0, is_demo_data = ? WHERE id = ?'
      ).run(now, runId, isDemoData ? 1 : 0, notifId);

      if (wasRemoved) {
        db.prepare("UPDATE recruitment_notifications SET is_removed = 0, lifecycle_status = 'active' WHERE id = ?").run(
          notifId
        );
      }

      const oldSnap = buildSnapshot(existing);
      const newSnap = mergePreservingMissingFields(oldSnap, rawSnap);
      const newHash = computeContentHash(newSnap);

      if (String(existing.content_hash) === newHash && !wasRemoved) {
        counts.duplicate_count += 1;
        continue;
      }

      const diffs = computeFieldDiffs(oldSnap, newSnap);
      if (diffs.length === 0 && !wasRemoved) {
        counts.duplicate_count += 1;
        continue;
      }
      if (wasRemoved && diffs.length === 0) {
        diffs.push({ field: 'notification_status', old_value: 'removed', new_value: 'active' });
      }

      const verRow = db
        .prepare('SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version_number), 0) as max_ver FROM notification_versions WHERE notification_id = ?')
        .get(notifId) as { max_ver: number };
      let maxVer = Number(verRow?.max_ver || 0);
      if (maxVer === 0) {
        saveVersionSnapshot(
          notifId,
          1,
          oldSnap,
          String(existing.content_hash),
          existing.last_run_id ? String(existing.last_run_id) : null,
          String(existing.first_seen_at || now),
          String(existing.organization_name),
          String(existing.source_url)
        );
        maxVer = 1;
      }

      const nextLifecycle = newSnap.notification_status === 'cancelled' ? 'cancelled' : 'updated';

      db.prepare(
        `UPDATE recruitment_notifications SET
          content_hash = ?, title = ?, organization_name = ?, notification_number = ?,
          vacancy_count = ?, job_category = ?, application_start_date = ?, application_deadline = ?,
          exam_date = ?, admit_card_date = ?, result_date = ?, description = ?, pdf_url = ?,
          source_url = ?, published_date = ?, lifecycle_status = ?, source_updated_at = ?
        WHERE id = ?`
      ).run(
        newHash,
        newSnap.title || String(existing.title),
        newSnap.organization_name || String(existing.organization_name),
        newSnap.notification_number,
        newSnap.vacancy_count,
        newSnap.job_category,
        newSnap.application_start_date,
        newSnap.application_deadline,
        newSnap.exam_date,
        newSnap.admit_card_date,
        newSnap.result_date,
        newSnap.description,
        newSnap.pdf_url,
        newSnap.source_url || String(existing.source_url),
        newSnap.published_date,
        nextLifecycle,
        now,
        notifId
      );

      saveVersionSnapshot(
        notifId,
        maxVer + 1,
        newSnap,
        newHash,
        runId,
        now,
        String(existing.organization_name),
        String(existing.source_url)
      );

      const historyDiffs = diffs.filter((d) => HISTORY_WORTHY_FIELDS.has(d.field));
      for (const diff of historyDiffs) {
        const label = classifyFieldChange(diff.field, diff.old_value, diff.new_value);
        const changeInfo = db
          .prepare(
            `INSERT INTO change_history (
              run_id, source_id, notification_id, entity_key, notification_title,
              change_type, changed_field, change_label, previous_value, new_value,
              changed_fields_json, before_snapshot_json, after_snapshot_json,
              source_url, is_verified, detected_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'UPDATED', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)`
          )
          .run(
            runId,
            sourceId,
            notifId,
            entityKey,
            newSnap.title || String(existing.title),
            diff.field,
            label,
            diff.old_value,
            diff.new_value,
            JSON.stringify([diff]),
            JSON.stringify(oldSnap),
            JSON.stringify(newSnap),
            newSnap.source_url || String(existing.source_url),
            now
          );

        const changeId = Number(changeInfo.lastInsertRowid);
        const severity =
          diff.new_value === 'cancelled'
            ? 'critical'
            : ['exam_date', 'application_deadline', 'admit_card_date', 'result_date', 'vacancy_count', 'notification_status'].includes(diff.field)
              ? 'warning'
              : 'info';

        db.prepare(
          `INSERT INTO alerts (
            change_id, source_id, notification_id, title, message, alert_type, severity, is_read, is_verified, created_at
          ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'UPDATED', ?, 0, 0, ?)`
        ).run(
          changeId,
          sourceId,
          notifId,
          `${label}: ${newSnap.title || existing.title}`,
          `${sourceCode.toUpperCase()} — Previous: ${diff.old_value || 'Unavailable'} → New: ${diff.new_value || 'Unavailable'}`,
          severity,
          now
        );
      }

      if (historyDiffs.length > 0) {
        counts.updated_count += 1;
      } else {
        counts.duplicate_count += 1;
      }
    }
  }

  if (allowRemovalCheck) {
    for (const existing of existingRows) {
      const entityKey = String(existing.entity_key);
      if (!seenKeys.has(entityKey) && !Boolean(existing.is_removed)) {
        const nextMissing = Number(existing.missing_count || 0) + 1;
        const notifId = Number(existing.id);
        if (nextMissing >= removal_threshold_val(removalThreshold)) {
          const oldSnap = buildSnapshot(existing);
          const removedSnap = { ...oldSnap, notification_status: 'removed' };
          const verRow = db
            .prepare('SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version_number), 0) as max_ver FROM notification_versions WHERE notification_id = ?')
            .get(notifId) as { max_ver: number };
          saveVersionSnapshot(
            notifId,
            Number(verRow?.max_ver || 0) + 1,
            removedSnap,
            computeContentHash(removedSnap),
            runId,
            now,
            String(existing.organization_name),
            String(existing.source_url)
          );
          db.prepare(
            "UPDATE recruitment_notifications SET missing_count = ?, is_removed = 1, lifecycle_status = 'removed', source_updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?"
          ).run(nextMissing, now, notifId);
          const changeInfo = db
            .prepare(
              `INSERT INTO change_history (
                run_id, source_id, notification_id, entity_key, notification_title,
                change_type, changed_field, change_label, previous_value, new_value,
                changed_fields_json, before_snapshot_json, after_snapshot_json,
                source_url, is_verified, detected_at
              ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'REMOVED', 'notification_status', 'NOTIFICATION REMOVED FROM PORTAL', 'active', 'removed', ?, ?, NULL, ?, 0, ?)`
            )
            .run(
              runId,
              sourceId,
              notifId,
              entityKey,
              String(existing.title),
              JSON.stringify([{ field: 'notification_status', old_value: 'active', new_value: 'removed' }]),
              JSON.stringify(oldSnap),
              String(existing.source_url),
              now
            );
          const changeId = Number(changeInfo.lastInsertRowid);
          db.prepare(
            `INSERT INTO alerts (
              change_id, source_id, notification_id, title, message, alert_type, severity, is_read, is_verified, created_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'REMOVED', 'warning', 0, 0, ?)`
          ).run(
            changeId,
            sourceId,
            notifId,
            `Removed from Portal: ${existing.title}`,
            `${sourceCode.toUpperCase()} listing absent across ${removalThreshold} consecutive healthy checks.`,
            now
          );
          counts.removed_count += 1;
        } else {
          db.prepare('UPDATE recruitment_notifications SET missing_count = ? WHERE id = ?').run(nextMissing, notifId);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return counts;
}

function removal_threshold_val(val: number): number {
  return val;
}

export function recordFailedScrape(
  source: Record<string, unknown>,
  runId: string,
  triggerType: string,
  modeUsed: string,
  httpStatus: number | null,
  errorMessage: string,
  durationMs: number,
  startedAt: string
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const sourceId = Number(source.id);
  const sourceCode = String(source.code);
  const isRateLimited = httpStatus === 403 || httpStatus === 429;
  const freshness = isRateLimited ? 'Rate Limited' : 'Error';

  db.prepare(
    'UPDATE monitoring_sources SET last_attempted_at = ?, last_http_status = ?, last_error = ?, freshness_status = ? WHERE id = ?'
  ).run(now, httpStatus, errorMessage, freshness, sourceId);

  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO scrape_runs (
      run_id, source_id, trigger_type, mode_used, status, http_status,
      parsed_count, new_count, updated_count, duplicate_count, removed_count,
      error_count, duration_ms, error_message, started_at, completed_at
    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
  ).run(
    runId,
    sourceId,
    triggerType,
    modeUsed,
    isRateLimited ? 'rate_limited' : 'error',
    httpStatus,
    durationMs,
    errorMessage,
    startedAt,
    now
  );

  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO alerts (
      change_id, source_id, notification_id, title, message, alert_type, severity, is_read, is_verified, created_at
    ) VALUES (NULL, ?, NULL, ?, ?, 'SOURCE_ERROR', ?, 0, 0, ?)`
  ).run(
    sourceId,
    `Source Check Failed: ${sourceCode.toUpperCase()}`,
    `${errorMessage} Existing records preserved safely (stale-data protection).`,
    isRateLimited ? 'critical' : 'warning',
    now
  );

  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM scrape_runs WHERE run_id = ?').get(runId) as Record<string, unknown>;
}

export async function seedDatabase(): Promise<void> {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const userCountRow = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM users').get() as { cnt: number };
  if (userCountRow.cnt === 0) {
    db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO users (full_name, email, password_hash, role, target_exams, email_alerts_enabled, is_active, created_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 1, ?)`
    ).run(
      'Arjun Krishnan (Admin)',
      'admin@govtrack.in',
      hashPassword('Admin@2026'),
      'admin',
      'All 10 Official Portals',
      now
    );

    db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO users (full_name, email, password_hash, role, target_exams, email_alerts_enabled, is_active, created_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 1, ?)`
    ).run(
      'Priya Sharma',
      'aspirant@govtrack.in',
      hashPassword('Aspirant@2026'),
      'user',
      'UPSC, SSC, IBPS, TNPSC',
      now
    );
  }

  let seededNewSources = false;
  for (const src of DEFAULT_SOURCES) {
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM monitoring_sources WHERE code = ?').get(src.code);
    if (!existing) {
      db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO monitoring_sources (
          code, name, organization_name, base_url, parser_key, fixture_filename,
          scrape_mode, crawl_interval_minutes, rate_limit_seconds, robots_policy,
          permission_note, is_active, is_demo_data, freshness_status, created_at
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 1, 'Pending', ?)`
      ).run(
        src.code,
        src.name,
        src.organization_name,
        src.base_url,
        src.parser_key,
        src.fixture_filename,
        src.scrape_mode,
        src.crawl_interval_minutes,
        src.rate_limit_seconds,
        src.robots_policy,
        src.permission_note,
        now
      );
      seededNewSources = true;
    }
  }

  db.prepare(
    "UPDATE monitoring_sources SET base_url = 'https://www.indiapost.gov.in/vacancies' WHERE code = 'indiapost' AND base_url = 'https://www.indiapost.gov.in/'"
  ).run();

  if (seededNewSources) {
    const sources = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources ORDER BY id ASC').all() as Record<string, unknown>[];
    await Promise.all(
      sources.map((src) =>
        runSourceScrape(src, { triggerType: 'scheduled', modeOverride: 'auto', suppressHistory: true })
      )
    );
  }
}

===== FILE: server/pdfExtractor.ts =====

import zlib from 'node:zlib';
import { cleanText, type ParsedNotification } from './scrapers/base.ts';
import { isPublicHttpUrl, normalizeHumanDate } from './scrapers/index.ts';

const USER_AGENT =
  process.env.SCRAPER_USER_AGENT ||
  'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/126.0.0.0 Safari/537.36';

export interface PdfExtractedFields {
  application_start_date: string | null;
  application_deadline: string | null;
  exam_date: string | null;
  admit_card_date: string | null;
  result_date: string | null;
  vacancy_count: string | null;
  notification_status: string | null;
}

export function extractTextFromPdfBuffer(buffer: Buffer): string {
  const rawLatin = buffer.toString('latin1');
  const chunks: string[] = [];
  const streamRegex = /stream\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n?endstream/g;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
  let streamCount = 0;

  while ((match = streamRegex.exec(rawLatin)) !== null && streamCount < 160) {
    streamCount += 1;
    const rawStream = Buffer.from(match[1], 'latin1');
    let inflated: Buffer | null = null;
    try {
      inflated = zlib.inflateSync(rawStream);
    } catch {
      try {
        inflated = zlib.inflateRawSync(rawStream);
      } catch {
        inflated = null;
      }
    }
    const target = inflated ? inflated.toString('latin1') : match[1];
    // Extract literal strings (...) inside PDF content streams
    const strMatches = target.match(/\((?:\\.|[^\\()])*\)/g);
    if (strMatches && strMatches.length > 0) {
      const joined = strMatches
        .map((s) =>
          s
            .slice(1, -1)
            .replace(/\\n/g, ' ')
            .replace(/\\r/g, ' ')
            .replace(/\\t/g, ' ')
            .replace(/\\\(/g, '(')
            .replace(/\\\)/g, ')')
            .replace(/\\\\/g, '\\')
        )
        .join(' ')
        .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]+/g, ' ')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      if (joined.length > 8) {
        chunks.push(joined);
      }
    }
  }

  return chunks.join(' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
}

export function extractStructuredFieldsFromPdfText(text: string): PdfExtractedFields {
  const result: PdfExtractedFields = {
    application_start_date: null,
    application_deadline: null,
    exam_date: null,
    admit_card_date: null,
    result_date: null,
    vacancy_count: null,
    notification_status: null,
  };
  if (!text || text.length < 30) return result;

  if (/\b(?:notification|examination|recruitment)\s+(?:stands\s+)?(?:cancelled|canceled|withdrawn|annulled)\b/i.test(text)) {
    result.notification_status = 'cancelled';
  }

  // Registration date range: "Online Registration ... 01.07.2026 to 21.07.2026"
  const regWindow = text.match(
    /(?:On-?line\s+Registration(?:\s+of\s+Applications)?|Registration\s+Window)[^0-9]{0,55}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})\s*(?:to|TO|–|-|&)\s*(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4})/i
  );
  if (regWindow) {
    result.application_start_date = normalizeHumanDate(regWindow[1]);
    result.application_deadline = normalizeHumanDate(regWindow[2]);
  }

  if (!result.application_deadline) {
    const deadlineMatch = text.match(
      /(?:Last\s+Date\s+(?:for|of)\s+(?:Online\s+)?(?:Submission|Application|Registration)|Closing\s+Date)[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,25}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9},?\s+\d{4})/i
    );
    if (deadlineMatch) {
      result.application_deadline = normalizeHumanDate(deadlineMatch[1]);
    }
  }

  const examMatch = text.match(
    /(?:Date\s+of\s+(?:Online\s+)?(?:Preliminary\s+|Main\s+|Written\s+|CBT\s+)?Examination|Online\s+Examination\s*\(?(?:Preliminary|Mains|CBT)?\)?)[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,30}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9},?\s+\d{4})/i
  );
  if (examMatch) {
    result.exam_date = normalizeHumanDate(examMatch[1]);
  }

  const admitMatch = text.match(
    /(?:Download\s+of\s+)?(?:Call\s+Letters?|Admit\s+Card|Hall\s+Ticket)[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,35}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9},?\s+\d{4})/i
  );
  if (admitMatch) {
    result.admit_card_date = normalizeHumanDate(admitMatch[1]);
  }

  const resultMatch = text.match(
    /Declaration\s+of\s+(?:Preliminary|Main|Final|Written\s+)?Result[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,35}(\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{4}|\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s+[A-Za-z]{3,9},?\s+\d{4})/i
  );
  if (resultMatch) {
    result.result_date = normalizeHumanDate(resultMatch[1]);
  }

  const vacMatch = text.match(/\bTotal\s+(?:Tentative\s+)?Vacancies\s*[:\-–]?\s*(\d{1,6})\b/i);
  if (vacMatch) {
    const num = Number(vacMatch[1]);
    if (num >= 1 && num <= 500000) {
      result.vacancy_count = String(num);
    }
  }

  return result;
}

export async function enrichNotificationFromPdf(item: ParsedNotification): Promise<ParsedNotification> {
  if (!item.pdf_url || !isPublicHttpUrl(item.pdf_url)) {
    return item;
  }
  const missingImportant =
    !item.exam_date || !item.application_deadline || !item.application_start_date || !item.vacancy_count;
  if (!missingImportant) {
    return item;
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 9000);
  try {
    const resp = await fetch(item.pdf_url, {
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': USER_AGENT,
        Accept: 'application/pdf,*/*;q=0.8',
      },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    if (!resp.ok) return item;
    const lenHeader = Number(resp.headers.get('content-length') || '0');
    if (lenHeader > 4_000_000) return item;
    const arrBuf = await resp.arrayBuffer();
    if (arrBuf.byteLength > 4_000_000 || arrBuf.byteLength < 500) return item;
    const buf = Buffer.from(arrBuf);
    if (!buf.subarray(0, 5).toString('latin1').startsWith('%PDF')) {
      return item;
    }

    const pdfText = extractTextFromPdfBuffer(buf);
    if (!pdfText) return item;

    const extracted = extractStructuredFieldsFromPdfText(pdfText);
    const next: ParsedNotification = { ...item };
    if (!next.application_start_date && extracted.application_start_date) {
      next.application_start_date = extracted.application_start_date;
    }
    if (!next.application_deadline && extracted.application_deadline) {
      next.application_deadline = extracted.application_deadline;
    }
    if (!next.exam_date && extracted.exam_date) {
      next.exam_date = extracted.exam_date;
    }
    if (!next.admit_card_date && extracted.admit_card_date) {
      next.admit_card_date = extracted.admit_card_date;
    }
    if (!next.result_date && extracted.result_date) {
      next.result_date = extracted.result_date;
    }
    if (!next.vacancy_count && extracted.vacancy_count) {
      next.vacancy_count = extracted.vacancy_count;
    }
    if (extracted.notification_status === 'cancelled') {
      next.notification_status = 'cancelled';
    }
    if (!next.description && extracted.exam_date) {
      next.description = cleanText(`Exam Date: ${extracted.exam_date}`);
    }
    return next;
  } catch {
    return item;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

===== FILE: server/auth.ts =====

import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import { db } from './db.ts';

const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET_KEY || 'govtrack-local-dev-secret-change-in-prod';

export interface AuthUser {
  id: number;
  full_name: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'user' | 'admin';
  target_exams: string | null;
  email_alerts_enabled: boolean;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface AuthedRequest extends Request {
  user?: AuthUser;
}

export function hashPassword(password: string): string {
  return bcrypt.hashSync(password, 10);
}

export function verifyPassword(plain: string, hash: string): boolean {
  return bcrypt.compareSync(plain, hash);
}

export function createAccessToken(user: { id: number; email: string; role: string }): string {
  return jwt.sign({ sub: String(user.id), email: user.email, role: user.role }, JWT_SECRET, {
    expiresIn: '12h',
  });
}

export function mapUserRow(row: Record<string, unknown>): AuthUser {
  return {
    id: Number(row.id),
    full_name: String(row.full_name),
    email: String(row.email),
    role: row.role === 'admin' ? 'admin' : 'user',
    target_exams: row.target_exams ? String(row.target_exams) : null,
    email_alerts_enabled: Boolean(row.email_alerts_enabled),
    is_active: Boolean(row.is_active),
    created_at: String(row.created_at),
  };
}

function resolveUserFromHeader(req: Request): AuthUser | null {
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) return null;
  const token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
  try {
    const decoded = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as { sub?: string };
    const userId = Number(decoded.sub);
    if (!userId) return null;
    const row = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(userId) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    if (!row || !row.is_active) return null;
    return mapUserRow(row);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function requireAuth(req: AuthedRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  const user = resolveUserFromHeader(req);
  if (!user) {
    res.status(401).json({ detail: 'Authentication required' });
    return;
  }
  req.user = user;
  next();
}

export function optionalAuth(req: AuthedRequest, _res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  req.user = resolveUserFromHeader(req) || undefined;
  next();
}

export function requireAdmin(req: AuthedRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  const user = resolveUserFromHeader(req);
  if (!user) {
    res.status(401).json({ detail: 'Authentication required' });
    return;
  }
  if (user.role !== 'admin') {
    res.status(403).json({ detail: 'Admin role required' });
    return;
  }
  req.user = user;
  next();
}


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: server/pdfGenerator.ts =====

function escapePdfText(input: string): string {
  return input
    .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\\/g, '\\\\')
    .replace(/\(/g, '\\(')
    .replace(/\)/g, '\\)');
}

function wrapLine(text: string, maxLen = 84): string[] {
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const lines: string[] = [];
  let current = '';
  for (const w of words) {
    if ((current + ' ' + w).trim().length > maxLen) {
      if (current) lines.push(current);
      current = w;
    } else {
      current = (current + ' ' + w).trim();
    }
  }
  if (current) lines.push(current);
  return lines.length ? lines : [''];
}

export function buildNotificationPdf(notif: Record<string, unknown>): Buffer {
  const lines: { text: string; bold?: boolean; size?: number }[] = [
    { text: 'GOVTRACK - OFFICIAL RECRUITMENT NOTIFICATION SUMMARY', bold: true, size: 13 },
    { text: '==========================================================================', size: 9 },
    { text: '' },
    { text: String(notif.title || 'Untitled Notification'), bold: true, size: 12 },
    { text: '' },
  ];

  const fields: [string, unknown][] = [
    ['Organization', notif.organization_name],
    ['Notification No.', notif.notification_number],
    ['Job Category', notif.job_category],
    ['Vacancy Count', notif.vacancy_count],
    ['Published Date', notif.published_date],
    ['Application Start', notif.application_start_date],
    ['Application Deadline', notif.application_deadline],
    ['Examination Date', notif.exam_date],
    ['Admit Card Date', notif.admit_card_date],
    ['Result Date', notif.result_date],
    ['Source Mode', notif.is_demo_data ? 'Offline HTML Fixture Snapshot (Demo)' : 'Live Extracted'],
    ['Original Source URL', notif.source_url],
    ['Official PDF URL', notif.pdf_url],
    ['First Seen At', notif.first_seen_at],
    ['Last Checked At', notif.last_seen_at],
  ];

  for (const [label, val] of fields) {
    const valStr = val ? String(val) : 'Not specified on source';
    const wrapped = wrapLine(`${label}: ${valStr}`, 84);
    for (const w of wrapped) {
      lines.push({ text: w, size: 10 });
    }
  }

  lines.push({ text: '' });
  lines.push({ text: 'Description:', bold: true, size: 10 });
  for (const w of wrapLine(String(notif.description || 'Not specified on source.'), 84)) {
    lines.push({ text: w, size: 10 });
  }

  lines.push({ text: '' });
  lines.push({ text: 'Generated by GovTrack Recruitment Monitoring System for verification & archival.', size: 8 });

  const streamOps: string[] = ['BT'];
  let y = 760;
  for (const item of lines) {
    if (y < 50) break;
    const font = item.bold ? '/F2' : '/F1';
    const size = item.size || 10;
    streamOps.push(`${font} ${size} Tf 50 ${y} Td (${escapePdfText(item.text)}) Tj`);
    streamOps.push(`0 0 Td`);
    // Reset matrix each line for clean absolute positioning
    streamOps.pop();
    streamOps[streamOps.length - 1] = `1 0 0 1 50 ${y} Tm ${font} ${size} Tf (${escapePdfText(item.text)}) Tj`;
    y -= size + 5;
  }
  streamOps.push('ET');
  const contentStream = streamOps.join('\n');

  const objects: string[] = [
    '1 0 obj\n<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>\nendobj\n',
    '2 0 obj\n<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>\nendobj\n',
    '3 0 obj\n<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 612 792] /Contents 4 0 R /Resources << /Font << /F1 5 0 R /F2 6 0 R >> >> >>\nendobj\n',
    `4 0 obj\n<< /Length ${Buffer.byteLength(contentStream, 'utf8')} >>\nstream\n${contentStream}\nendstream\nendobj\n`,
    '5 0 obj\n<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica >>\nendobj\n',
    '6 0 obj\n<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica-Bold >>\nendobj\n',
  ];

  let pdf = '%PDF-1.4\n';
  const offsets: number[] = [0];
  for (const obj of objects) {
    offsets.push(Buffer.byteLength(pdf, 'utf8'));
    pdf += obj;
  }
  const xrefPos = Buffer.byteLength(pdf, 'utf8');
  pdf += `xref\n0 ${objects.length + 1}\n0000000000 65535 f \n`;
  for (let i = 1; i <= objects.length; i++) {
    pdf += `${String(offsets[i]).padStart(10, '0')} 00000 n \n`;
  }
  pdf += `trailer\n<< /Size ${objects.length + 1} /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n${xrefPos}\n%%EOF\n`;
  return Buffer.from(pdf, 'utf8');
}
