===== FILE: README.md =====

# GovTrack — Intelligent Government Recruitment & Examination Notification Change Monitoring System

GovTrack is a full-stack web monitoring platform designed to eliminate manual checking across Indian government recruitment websites. It automatically extracts structured notification fields from 10 approved official portals, detects new, updated, and removed notices with before-and-after diff evidence, suppresses formatting-only duplicates, and alerts aspirants through a centralized blue-and-white dashboard.

---

## 1. Monitored Official Websites (10 Dedicated Parsers)

1. **UPSC** — `https://upsc.gov.in/` (`upsc.py`)
2. **SSC** — `https://ssc.gov.in/` (`ssc.py`)
3. **TNPSC** — `https://www.tnpsc.gov.in/` (`tnpsc.py`)
4. **TNUSRB** — `https://www.tnusrb.tn.gov.in/` (`tnusrb.py`)
5. **Railway Recruitment Boards (RRB Chandigarh)** — `https://www.rrbcdg.gov.in/` (`rrbcdg.py`)
6. **IBPS** — `https://www.ibps.in/` (`ibps.py`)
7. **SBI Careers** — `https://sbi.co.in/web/careers` (`sbi.py`)
8. **RBI Opportunities** — `https://opportunities.rbi.org.in/` (`rbi.py`)
9. **NTA** — `https://nta.ac.in/` (`nta.py`)
10. **India Post** — `https://www.indiapost.gov.in/` (`indiapost.py`)

---

## 2. Tech Stack

- **Frontend:** React 19, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS v4, React Router, Lucide Icons
- **Primary Backend (Local / Major Project):** Python 3.11+, FastAPI, SQLAlchemy 2.0, SQLite (`govtrack.db`), Requests, BeautifulSoup4, APScheduler, PyJWT, bcrypt
- **Integrated Node Runtime (Single-Command Preview):** Express + Node 22 `node:sqlite` + Cheerio + `node-cron` sharing the identical SQLite database and REST contract
- **Testing:** `pytest` / `unittest` with 15 labelled change-detection & offline HTML fixture tests

---

## 3. Folder Structure

```text
govtrack/
├── backend/
│   ├── app/
│   │   ├── main.py              # FastAPI REST API + lifespan scheduler
│   │   ├── models.py            # SQLAlchemy ORM models
│   │   ├── schemas.py           # Pydantic request/response schemas
│   │   ├── auth.py              # JWT + bcrypt authentication & role guards
│   │   ├── diff_core.py         # Deterministic SHA-256 hashing & field diffing
│   │   ├── change_detector.py   # New/Updated/Duplicate/Safe-Removal engine
│   │   ├── scraper_engine.py    # Polite fetcher (robots.txt, backoff, 403/429 stop)
│   │   ├── scheduler.py         # APScheduler periodic job controller
│   │   ├── seed.py              # Seeds 10 portals + default accounts
│   │   └── scrapers/            # 10 separate website parser modules
│   ├── fixtures/                # Offline HTML snapshots + CP-5 test fixtures
│   ├── tests/
│   │   └── test_change_detection.py  # 15 change-detection & safety tests
│   └── requirements.txt
├── src/                         # React + TypeScript + Tailwind CSS frontend (11 pages)
├── server/ & server.ts          # Unified Node/Express runtime for single-command dev
├── docs/
│   ├── DATA_DICTIONARY.md
│   └── SOURCE_APPROVAL_AND_ETHICS.md
├── setup_windows.bat
├── start_windows.bat
├── .env.example
└── package.json
```

---

## 4. Windows VS Code Setup & Local Execution

### Prerequisites
- **Python 3.10+** (check *"Add Python to PATH"* during installation)
- **Node.js 20+ (LTS)**
- **VS Code**

### Option A: Automated Windows Setup (Recommended)
1. Download/extract the project and open the folder in **VS Code**.
2. Open a Command Prompt (`cmd`) or VS Code terminal and run:
   ```bat
   setup_windows.bat
   ```
3. Start both **Python FastAPI** (`http://127.0.0.1:8000`) and **React Vite** (`http://localhost:3000`):
   ```bat
   start_windows.bat
   ```

### Option B: Manual Terminal Commands (Windows PowerShell / CMD)

**Terminal 1 — Python FastAPI Backend:**
```bat
cd backend
python -m venv venv
venv\Scripts\activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
uvicorn app.main:app --reload --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000
```
API Swagger docs are available at `http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs`.

**Terminal 2 — React Frontend (proxying `/api` to FastAPI):**
```bat
copy .env.example .env
npm install
set VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET=http://127.0.0.1:8000
npm run dev:frontend
```
Open `http://localhost:3000` in your browser.

### Option C: Single-Command Unified Node Runtime
If you want to run everything with a single command (Node.js 22+):
```bat
npm install
npm run dev
```

## 6. Running the 15 Change-Detection Tests

From the project root:
```bat
cd backend
venv\Scripts\activate
pytest -v
```
Or without installing external packages using Python's built-in test runner:
```bash
python3 -m unittest discover -s backend/tests -v
```

### Covered Test Scenarios (15 Cases)
1. New notification detection & alert creation
2. Exact rerun duplicate suppression (zero false alerts)
3. Formatting-only HTML & whitespace edit produces zero false updates (CP-5)
4. Deadline extension & vacancy count update with Before/After diff evidence
5. HTTP `404` failure never deletes existing records
6. HTTP `404` followed by restored identical page causes zero false changes (CP-5)
7. HTTP `429` rate-limit response halts politely & preserves database state
8. Zero-result parser mismatch guard prevents mass deletions
9. In-batch duplicate row suppression
10. Missing fields remain `None` (never fabricated)
11. Safe removal requires consecutive healthy non-zero parses
12. Corrigendum PDF link change detection
13. Canonical entity key fallback when notification number is absent
14. Verification of all 10 offline HTML fixture snapshots
15. Three-cycle timestamped refresh consistency check

---

## 7. Demonstration Walkthrough for Viva / Evaluation

1. **Sign In**: Log in as `admin@govtrack.in` (`Admin@2026`) or `aspirant@govtrack.in` (`Aspirant@2026`).
2. **Dashboard & Notifications**: Show the 10 monitored portals, upcoming deadlines, and click any notification to inspect all **14 structured fields** + source attribution + official PDF link.
3. **Live Change Detection Demo (Monitoring Sources Page)**:
   - On **UPSC**, click **Replay v2 (Deadline Extended + New)** -> Observe `05/2026-CSP` updated (deadline `2026-03-05 -> 2026-03-15`, vacancies `1056 -> 1105`) and `07/2026-CMS` added.
   - Click **Replay Formatting-Only Edit** -> Observe `0` new/updated alerts and `Duplicates Suppressed` incremented.
   - Click **Simulate HTTP 404** -> Observe source freshness becomes `Error`, existing notifications stay intact, and an error alert is logged.
   - Click **Restore Baseline** -> Observe safe recovery.
4. **Change History & Verification**: Navigate to **Change History**, inspect the Before/After diff table, and click **Verify Change**.
5. **Admin Panel**: Demonstrate scheduler pause/resume, interval configuration, batch sync (`Run All Active Sources`), scrape run audit logs, and user role management.

===== FILE: docs/DATA_DICTIONARY.md =====

# GovTrack Data Dictionary (SRS CP-2)

## 1. Extracted Recruitment Notification Fields (14 Core Fields)

Every parser module outputs normalized records conforming to this contract. Missing fields on the source page are stored as `NULL` and displayed as *"Not specified on source"* — **never fabricated**.

| Field Name | Type | Nullable | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `title` | `String(500)` | No | Official recruitment or examination title |
| `organization_name` | `String(200)` | No | Issuing government commission, board, or bank |
| `notification_number` | `String(120)` | Yes | Advertisement / CEN / CRP / F.No. reference identifier |
| `vacancy_count` | `String(80)` | Yes | Published post count (if announced on the notice) |
| `job_category` | `String(120)` | Yes | Examination or service category (e.g., Civil Services, Banking) |
| `application_start_date` | `String(40)` | Yes | Online registration opening date (`YYYY-MM-DD`) |
| `application_deadline` | `String(40)` | Yes | Last date for online application submission (`YYYY-MM-DD`) |
| `exam_date` | `String(40)` | Yes | Preliminary / CBT / Written examination date |
| `admit_card_date` | `String(40)` | Yes | Admit card / e-Call letter release date |
| `result_date` | `String(40)` | Yes | Result publication date |
| `description` | `Text` | Yes | Short notice summary extracted from the source row |
| `pdf_url` | `String(600)` | Yes | Direct link to official notification / corrigendum PDF |
| `source_url` | `String(600)` | No | Original official notice or portal URL (mandatory attribution) |
| `published_date` | `String(40)` | Yes | Official notification issue date |

## 2. Provenance & Change-Tracking Fields

| Field Name | Type | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `entity_key` | `String(255)` | Canonical stable identifier (`<source_code>::<normalized-advt-or-title>`) |
| `content_hash` | `String(64)` | SHA-256 hash over normalized 14-field JSON (ignores HTML formatting/whitespace edits) |
| `first_seen_at` | `DateTime(UTC)` | Timestamp when GovTrack first discovered this notification |
| `last_seen_at` | `DateTime(UTC)` | Timestamp of the most recent healthy check that observed this item |
| `source_updated_at` | `DateTime(UTC)` | Timestamp of the most recent semantic field update |
| `is_demo_data` | `Boolean` | `true` when ingested from offline HTML fixtures; `false` when fetched live |
| `is_removed` | `Boolean` | Marked `true` only after 2 consecutive healthy non-zero parses omit the item |
| `last_run_id` | `String(64)` | Run identifier (`RUN-...`) of the latest scrape job touching this record |

## 3. Change History Audit Record (`change_history`)

| Field Name | Type | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `run_id` | `String(64)` | Scrape run ID that detected the change |
| `change_type` | `String(30)` | `NEW`, `UPDATED`, or `REMOVED` |
| `changed_fields_json` | `JSON` | Array of `{field, old_value, new_value}` field-level diffs |
| `before_snapshot_json` | `JSON` | Complete normalized 14-field state before the update (`NULL` for `NEW`) |
| `after_snapshot_json` | `JSON` | Complete normalized 14-field state after the update (`NULL` for `REMOVED`) |
| `is_verified` | `Boolean` | Manual review confirmation flag |
| `verified_by` | `String(120)` | Name of the reviewer who verified the change |


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: docs/SOURCE_APPROVAL_AND_ETHICS.md =====

# Source Approval, Ethics & Scraping Governance (SRS CP-1)

## 1. Approved Official Government Recruitment Sources

| # | Portal | Official URL | Parser Module | Policy & Access Notes |
| :- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | UPSC | `https://upsc.gov.in/` | `upsc.py` | Public examination notices; non-sensitive data only; Crawl-delay respected |
| 2 | SSC | `https://ssc.gov.in/` | `ssc.py` | Public notice board & PDFs; polite User-Agent; stops on 403/429 |
| 3 | TNPSC | `https://www.tnpsc.gov.in/` | `tnpsc.py` | Public state PSC notification table; low-frequency periodic checks |
| 4 | TNUSRB | `https://www.tnusrb.tn.gov.in/` | `tnusrb.py` | Public uniformed services recruitment brochures |
| 5 | RRB Chandigarh | `https://www.rrbcdg.gov.in/` | `rrbcdg.py` | Public Railway CEN notices and CBT schedules |
| 6 | IBPS | `https://www.ibps.in/` | `ibps.py` | Public CRP banking recruitment notices |
| 7 | SBI Careers | `https://sbi.co.in/web/careers` | `sbi.py` | Public careers page; uses offline fixture fallback if bot-protected |
| 8 | RBI Opportunities | `https://opportunities.rbi.org.in/` | `rbi.py` | Public central bank vacancy notices |
| 9 | NTA | `https://nta.ac.in/` | `nta.py` | Public entrance & fellowship notices |
| 10 | India Post | `https://www.indiapost.gov.in/` | `indiapost.py` | Public GDS / departmental recruitment circulars |

## 2. Engineering Ethics & Failure Safety Controls

1. **Descriptive User-Agent**: Every live request sends `GovTrackMonitor/1.0 (+Academic-Research-Project; respectful-crawler)`.
2. **Robots.txt Check**: Live mode checks `/robots.txt` prior to fetching. Disallowed paths abort cleanly with HTTP 403 logged.
3. **Never Bypass CAPTCHA or Anti-Bot Walls**: If Cloudflare, reCAPTCHA, or HTTP `403`/`429` is detected, scraping halts immediately without retry loops.
4. **No False Deletions**: HTTP `404`, `403`, `429`, timeouts, or `0`-record parses update `last_attempted_at`, set freshness to `Error` / `Rate Limited`, preserve `last_successful_at`, and **never mark active notifications as removed**.
5. **Clear Demo vs. Live Attribution**: Records parsed from offline HTML snapshots in `backend/fixtures/` are explicitly marked `is_demo_data = true` and labeled *Offline HTML Fixture (Demo)* in the UI.


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: server.ts =====

import 'dotenv/config';
import express from 'express';
import cron from 'node-cron';
import path from 'node:path';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import { createServer as createViteServer } from 'vite';
import { db, initSchema } from './server/db.ts';
import { seedDatabase } from './server/seed.ts';
import { apiRouter, schedulerState } from './server/routes.ts';
import { runSourceScrape } from './server/scraperEngine.ts';

async function startServer() {
  initSchema();
  await seedDatabase();

  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json({ limit: '1mb' }));

  app.use('/api', apiRouter);

  // Periodic background monitoring job (every 6 hours by default, respects pause toggle)
  cron.schedule('0 */6 * * *', async () => {
    if (schedulerState.paused) return;
    const sources = db
      .prepare('SELECT * FROM monitoring_sources WHERE is_active = 1')
      .all() as Record<string, unknown>[];
    for (const src of sources) {
      await runSourceScrape(src, { triggerType: 'scheduled' });
    }
    schedulerState.next_run_at = new Date(Date.now() + schedulerState.interval_minutes * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
  });

  const distDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'dist');
  const isProd = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && fs.existsSync(distDir);

  if (isProd) {
    app.use(express.static(distDir));
    app.get('*', (_req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(distDir, 'index.html'));
    });
  } else {
    const vite = await createViteServer({
      server: { middlewareMode: true },
      appType: 'spa',
    });
    app.use(vite.middlewares);
  }

  const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT || 3000);
  app.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log(`GovTrack unified server listening on http://localhost:${PORT}`);
  });
}

startServer().catch((err) => {
  console.error('Failed to start GovTrack server:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});


===== END FILE =====


===== FILE: setup_windows.bat =====

@echo off
echo ============================================
echo   GovTrack Windows Setup (Python + Node)
echo ============================================

if not exist ".env" (
  copy ".env.example" ".env"
  echo Created .env from .env.example
)

echo [1/3] Creating Python virtual environment...
cd backend
if not exist "venv" (
  python -m venv venv
)
call venv\Scripts\activate.bat
echo [2/3] Installing Python FastAPI & scraping dependencies...
pip install --upgrade pip
pip install -r requirements.txt
cd ..

echo [3/3] Installing Node.js frontend dependencies...
call npm install

echo.
echo Setup complete!
echo To run Python FastAPI + React frontend together, run: start_windows.bat
echo To run unit tests, run: cd backend ^&^& venv\Scripts\activate ^&^& pytest -v
pause

===== FILE: start_windows.bat =====

@echo off
echo ============================================
echo   Starting GovTrack (FastAPI + React)
echo ============================================

start "GovTrack FastAPI Backend (Port 8000)" cmd /k "cd backend && call venv\Scripts\activate.bat && uvicorn app.main:app --reload --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000"

timeout /t 3 /nobreak > nul

set VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET=http://127.0.0.1:8000
start "GovTrack React Frontend (Port 3000)" cmd /k "npm run dev:frontend"

echo.
echo Backend API docs: http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs
echo Frontend App:     http://localhost:3000
pause

===== FILE: vite.config.ts =====

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [
      react(),
      tailwindcss(),
      // Copies src/assets into dist/ so root-relative URLs like
      // "/src/assets/images/..." (e.g. generate_image output) still resolve
      // in production builds. Do not remove.
      {
        name: 'copy-unbundled-src-assets',
        apply: 'build',
        closeBundle(error?: Error): void {
          if (error) {
            return;
          }
          const distDir = path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist');
          const srcAssets = path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/assets');
          const distAssets = path.resolve(distDir, 'src/assets');
          if (fs.existsSync(distDir) && fs.existsSync(srcAssets)) {
            fs.cpSync(srcAssets, distAssets, {recursive: true, force: true});
          }
        },
      },
    ],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
      ...(process.env.VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET
        ? {
            proxy: {
              '/api': {
                target: process.env.VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET,
                changeOrigin: true,
              },
            },
          }
        : {}),
    },
  };
});


===== END FILE =====
